### Practice sessions & materials

### H-12 `all-answered-unfinished-session-invisible` — A fully answered session whose finish call failed disappears; the step stays in progress

- **Severity:** High
- **Affected user flow:** Finishing a session after the last answer, with a dropped network or a killed app.
- **Location:** `apps/mobile/app/practice/[id].tsx:252-256` (automatic finish), `:275` (`finishSession`), not queued in the outbox; `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/home.ts:137-141` (resume requires `answered < total`), `:154-161` (the result card requires `finished`), `:174-178` (`practice_ready` requires `prepared`); `apps/api/src/modules/practice/service.ts:200-203`.
- **Current behavior:** If `/finish` fails, the session stays active with every item answered. Home shows no resume card, no result and no practice card. The step stays `in_progress` with no evidence, and no sweep repairs it (still unchanged after 48 h and ticks).
- **Expected behavior:** A fully answered session is finished eventually, server-side or from the outbox, and Buddy's step gets its evidence.
- **Problem:** Finishing depends on one client request with no durable retry.
- **User impact:** She finishes 10 questions in the tram, the finish call fails, and there is no "Geschafft", no result, and Buddy never learns the step was done.
- **Concrete scenario:** Step-bound session, last answer stored → POST `/finish` fails → "Zurück zu Buddy" → home is empty from then on.
- **Root cause:** Finishing is client-driven and not durable, and the home filters assume every answered session was finished.
- **Recommended fix:** Finish on the server when the last item closes (inside the answer transaction), or enqueue a finish job. Add a sweep for stale sessions.
- **Blast radius:** Any session whose finish failed or was interrupted; step-bound sessions also lose Buddy's evidence.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced). A late `/finish` still works, so only the retry is missing.

### H-13 `draft-resume-dead-end` — Resuming a draft whose material was abandoned or deleted loops on "Das gibt es nicht mehr"

- **Severity:** High
- **Affected user flow:** Draft kept after the app closed → home "Weiter" → capture → "Nochmal senden".
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/materials/service.ts:149-153` (the `client_request_id` lookup has no `archived_at` filter) → `:80-83` (`not_found` for archived rows); `:45`, `:646-649` (abandoned after 24 h); `:672` (delete archives); `apps/mobile/lib/capture/draft.ts:53` (drafts kept 7 days); `apps/mobile/app/capture.tsx:127` (resume reuses the saved request id).
- **Current behavior:** Each resend finds the archived row and answers 404 "Das gibt es nicht mehr. Deine Fotos sind noch hier." The client never changes the request id.
- **Expected behavior:** The photos can still be sent (a fresh material), and there are no dead ends (rule 12).
- **Problem:** The device keeps a draft for 7 days while the server abandons the material after 24 h, and the idempotency lookup ignores the archive flag.
- **User impact:** A child who comes back the next day gets the same error on every tap. The only exits are "Verwerfen", which loses the photos, or the non-obvious trick of adding or removing a photo.
- **Concrete scenario:** `repro-12`: POST `/materials` with request id R → 201 → clock +25 h → tick → the row becomes `failed/photos_missing` and archived → resending with R returns 404 twice → a fresh id returns 201.
- **Root cause:** The lookup keyed on request id ignores archived rows, the client never rotates the id, and the two lifetimes disagree.
- **Recommended fix:** Exclude archived rows from the lookup, or rotate the request id on `not_found`. Align the draft's maximum age with the abandon window.
- **Blast radius:** Every resumed draft older than 24 h, and every draft whose sheet was deleted.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced; `repro-12`).

### H-14 `p2-NM-05` — The prompts allow "up to 8" accepted answers but the schema allows 6, so one rich vocab word fails the whole list

- **Severity:** High
- **Affected user flow:** Photographing or typing a vocabulary list → one structured model call → zod parse of all items.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/practice/items.ts:33` (`.max(6)`) vs `apps/api/src/modules/materials/extract.ts:88` and `apps/api/src/modules/practice/generate.ts:72` ("up to 8"); `apps/api/src/modules/materials/service.ts:436` (the whole result fails with `model_error`); `generate.ts:189,193` (`model_unavailable`).
- **Current behavior:** An item with 7 or 8 accepted answers fails the whole `ExtractionResult` or `GeneratedSet`. These calls have no repair round.
- **Expected behavior:** One consistent limit, with trimming per item. `items.ts` promises that broken items are dropped.
- **Problem:** The prompt and the validator contradict each other, and parsing is all-or-nothing.
- **User impact:** A list with a word like "to get" (7 translations, articles included) fails as a reading error or "Buddy kann gerade nicht antworten". Retrying can fail the same way.
- **Concrete scenario:** A typed list with "to get – bekommen, erhalten, kriegen, holen, besorgen, werden, verstehen" → safeParse fails → 503.
- **Root cause:** The prompt was changed in commit b62c58b without updating the bound.
- **Recommended fix:** Align the numbers. Add `.transform(a => a.slice(0, N))` or a per-item safeParse.
- **Blast radius:** Photographed and typed vocabulary lists (the main vocab journey).
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced via safeParse). How often the model emits more than 6 answers is not measured.

### H-15 `p2-figure-bounds-kill-whole-set` — One figure over a zod bound fails the whole extraction or generated set

- **Severity:** High
- **Affected user flow:** Worksheet photo → extraction, or POST `/practice/topic` → generated set with figures.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/practice/items.ts:57` (`figure: Figure.nullable()` with no `.catch`); `packages/shared-types/src/contracts/figure.ts:28` (at most 8 points), `:66-70` (at most 6 columns, at most 10 rows); `apps/api/src/modules/materials/service.ts:436`; `apps/api/src/modules/practice/generate.ts:189`.
- **Current behavior:** An 8-column value table, an 11-row table or a number line with 9 points fails the whole parse. The material is marked failed (`model_error`) with no retry; topic practice returns 503.
- **Expected behavior:** An invalid figure is dropped (null) and the question survives, as the comments in `items.ts` and `extract.ts:2-3` promise.
- **Problem:** Figure validation is part of an all-or-nothing parse, and the bounds reach the model only as description hints.
- **User impact:** A function worksheet ("Wertetabelle für x = −5 … 5") fails as unreadable, and retrying fails the same way.
- **Concrete scenario:** Table header x|−3|…|3 (8 cells) → `items.2.figure.header: too_big` → material failed.
- **Root cause:** There is no `.catch` per figure or per item; `usableFigure` runs only after a successful parse.
- **Recommended fix:** Use `Figure.nullable().catch(null)` (or clip the arrays) and parse items one by one. Shares its fix with H-14.
- **Blast radius:** All extractions and generated sets with a table, number line, chart or geometry figure over a bound.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced via safeParse).

### H-16 `p2-ml-photo-not-linked-to-goal-or-capture-step` — Photos sent from anywhere but the capture card are not linked to the exam goal

- **Severity:** High
- **Affected user flow:** Plan an exam → Buddy asks for the worksheet → she sends the photo → exam practice.
- **Location:** `apps/mobile/app/buddy.tsx:650` (the composer camera pushes `/capture` with no params), `:692`; `apps/mobile/app/library.tsx:114`; `AreaCard.tsx:21`; only the NowCard passes `step_id`/`goal_id`; `apps/api/src/modules/materials/service.ts:116-139`, `:569-584` (the capture step is closed only with `step_id`/`goal_id`); `apps/api/src/modules/practice/selection.ts:31-44` (once goal material exists, only goal-linked items are used); `buddy/home.ts:226-235`.
- **Current behavior:** A sheet sent with the always-visible composer camera is read but not linked to the goal. The capture step stays `planned`, and "Schick mir ein Foto" comes back. Buddy's context says the exam has "material: 0 ready". A second sheet sent this way never enters exam practice.
- **Expected behavior:** A photo sent while a capture step is open completes it, or at least links it to the goal (by default when exactly one step is open).
- **Problem:** Linking lives in one UI path; the server has no rule and the model no tool for it.
- **User impact:** Buddy keeps asking for a sheet she already sent, and exam practice leaves out her material.
- **Concrete scenario:** "Mathearbeit am Freitag über Brüche" → capture card → she uses the composer camera → the step stays planned → at d-3 the context still shows 0 ready material.
- **Root cause:** The goal/step link is a client-side route parameter that only NowCard passes.
- **Recommended fix:** In `createMaterial`, attach the single open capture step and its goal. Pass step/goal from every `/capture` entry point, including "Neues Foto" on a failed reading.
- **Blast radius:** Capture steps, exam material counts, exam practice selection, countdown checks.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced).

### Identity, PIN & onboarding

### H-17 `put-pin-no-lockout` — PUT `/account/pin` checks `current_pin` without counting failures or honouring the lock

- **Severity:** High (the critical rating on `repro-08` is overridden to high after the second opinion; the brute-force findings are merged here).
- **Affected user flow:** Changing the PIN (settings PinCard) and the adult gate for a minor's account.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/identity/routes.ts:188-214` (`verifyPin(current_pin)` at :197; no `pin_failed_count`/`pin_locked_until` handling; a success resets the lock at :211) vs `:223-238` (the lock exists only on POST `/account/admin-session`); no rate limiter in `apps/api/src/app.ts`; promise in `docs/privacy.md:18-19` and `docs/architecture.md:63` ("5 tries, 15 min lock").
- **Current behavior:** A wrong `current_pin` returns 403 `pin_required` and changes no counter. A locked account is not checked. A correct guess sets a new PIN and clears a running lock.
- **Expected behavior:** Every route that verifies the PIN counts failures and honours the 15-minute lock.
- **Problem:** The lock logic is duplicated into only one of the two routes that verify the PIN.
- **User impact:** Anyone holding the child's signed-in session (on web the token sits in localStorage) can try all 10,000 PINs (≈50 ms each, under 10 minutes). The hit replaces the parents' PIN, which opens contact loosening, export and deletion.
- **Concrete scenario:** `repro-08`: 200 wrong `current_pin` guesses in 10 s left `pin_failed_count` at 0 with no lock. The correct PIN then returned 200, and the new PIN opened an admin session. With the account locked through admin-session (423), PUT with the correct `current_pin` returned 200 and cleared the lock.
- **Root cause:** No shared "verify with lock" function exists.
- **Recommended fix:** Add one `verifyPinWithLock` (atomic, see H-18) and use it in both routes. Refuse PUT while locked. Test: 5 wrong `current_pin`, then a 423.
- **Blast radius:** All minor accounts: the adult gate required by rule 6.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced; `repro-08`).

### H-18 `admin-session-lockout-lost-update` — The PIN failure counter is a non-atomic read-modify-write

- **Severity:** High
- **Affected user flow:** PIN check → admin session (POST `/account/admin-session`).
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/http/context.ts:56` (`requireAccount` loads the row at request start); `apps/api/src/modules/identity/routes.ts:223` (lock check on that stale row), `:229-238` (`failures = account.pin_failed_count + 1` is written as an absolute value).
- **Current behavior:** Concurrent wrong attempts all read the same count and write count + 1, so a burst of any size counts as one failure.
- **Expected behavior:** Five wrong attempts lock the account (`docs/privacy.md:18-19`).
- **Problem:** No atomic increment, no `for update`, no rate limit.
- **User impact:** Even with H-17 fixed, the lock only slows a script by the burst factor. The documented "5 attempts" guarantee does not hold.
- **Concrete scenario:** `repro-09`: 10 parallel wrong PINs gave `pin_failed_count` = 1 and the correct PIN then returned 200. Bursts of 10 allowed 50 checked guesses before the first lock.
- **Root cause:** The counter is kept in application memory from a stale row.
- **Recommended fix:** `update accounts set pin_failed_count = pin_failed_count + 1 … returning`, with lock-on-threshold in the same statement (or `select … for update` in a transaction). Add a `Promise.all` integration test.
- **Blast radius:** All minor accounts.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced; `repro-09`).

### H-19 `p2-ux-narrative-parent-handover` — After one PIN-unlocked step, the admin token stays live while Settings stays open

- **Severity:** High
- **Affected user flow:** Settings on a minor's profile, after a parent unlocked one step (export, allow contact) and handed the phone back.
- **Location:** `apps/mobile/app/settings.tsx:41` (the token is cleared only when the screen loses focus); `apps/mobile/components/settings/ContactSection.tsx:87,97` (`canLoosen` is true while a token exists); `components/settings/AccountAccessCard.tsx:54-58`; `components/settings/AdultSection.tsx` (no gated action clears the token); `docs/privacy.md:19-21` ("the app forgets it as soon as … the one step they unlocked is done").
- **Current behavior:** After an export (the share sheet does not blur the route), the token stays valid for up to 10 minutes. The contact button reads "Erlauben" instead of "Eltern fragen". Caps, quiet hours and "Passwort ändern" open without the PIN.
- **Expected behavior:** The token is cleared once the unlocked step is done, and the unlocked state is visible.
- **Problem:** Ordinary UI bypasses the PIN gate after any legitimate unlock.
- **User impact:** The child can loosen her own contact rules. Through the password sheet, and then the fresh-sign-in PIN reset, the parents can lose control of their own gate.
- **Concrete scenario:** Mother exports and hands the phone back → Lena taps "Erlauben" and sets 4× per week and quiet hours from 21:00 → "Passwort ändern" opens without the PIN.
- **Root cause:** Token lifetime is tied to screen focus rather than to the step. Password change is gated only on the client (see `account-change-gated-client-only`, §6). Steps 5–6 of the chain are the path narrowed in `repro-07` (§6).
- **Recommended fix:** Clear the token after each gated action and when the app goes to the background. Move the credential-change gate to the server.
- **Blast radius:** Every family with a minor profile.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (code-traced). This combines member findings (`p2-lc-admin-token-outlives-step-and-lock`, `p2-ux-pin-unlock-lingers-in-settings`); it was not run end to end.

### H-20 `onboarding-pin-dead-end` — A failed PIN call after the child profile exists strands the profile screen

- **Severity:** High
- **Affected user flow:** Creating a child profile (two-step form) and the first PIN.
- **Location:** `apps/mobile/app/profile.tsx:84-91` (`createLearner`, then `setPin`, in one try), `:96-99` (the catch only shows a toast), the CTA stays enabled; `apps/api/src/modules/identity/routes.ts:195` (5-minute freshness), `:206` (`reauth_required`), `:149-150` (a second POST `/learner` returns 409); `apps/mobile/app/index.tsx:46-47` (the gate runs only on mount).
- **Current behavior:** If more than 5 minutes pass after sign-in, or a response is lost, the learner is created but the PIN is refused. The toast asks for the password, but the screen has no password field. Every retap returns 409 "Das hat sich gerade geändert." Only restarting the app moves on.
- **Expected behavior:** Once the profile exists, route onward and show that the PIN is still missing, or ask for the password inline (as PinCard does).
- **Problem:** Two non-atomic requests with no handling of partial success. The 5-minute window is easily exceeded by a real onboarding (e-mail confirmation, consent text, two form steps).
- **User impact:** The parent is stuck with a misleading message, and the child profile exists without an adult PIN.
- **Concrete scenario:** `repro-10`: sign-in → 6 minutes later POST `/learner` 201, PUT `/account/pin` 403 `reauth_required`, retap 409; `/me` shows the learner present and `pin_set:false`. At 4 minutes: 201/200.
- **Root cause:** `submit()` treats the two calls as one unit and never records that the learner was created.
- **Recommended fix:** Track that the learner was created (or re-read `/me` on error), offer the password field inline on `reauth_required`, or accept the PIN inside POST `/learner` in one transaction.
- **Blast radius:** Child onboardings that take more than 5 minutes after sign-in, or that hit a flaky connection.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced at the API level; `repro-10`); the client side is code-traced.

### H-21 `child-16-17-profile-500` — Creating a "child" profile aged 16 or 17 fails with a 500

- **Severity:** High
- **Affected user flow:** A parent sets up a child's profile (`apps/mobile/app/profile.tsx` → POST `/learner`).
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/identity/routes.ts:112` (`const minor = age < 16`), `:137-138` (consent columns are written only when minor), `:149-151` (only unique violations are mapped); `infra/supabase/migrations/0001_baseline.sql:71-73` (`learners_child_consent` requires consent for every `relation='child'`); `apps/api/src/lib/db.ts:105` (23505 only); `apps/api/src/app.ts:100` (500 `internal`).
- **Current behavior:** For a child aged 16 or older, consent is stored as null although the app sent `minor_consent:true`. Postgres rejects the insert (23514) and the client gets 500 "Da ist was schiefgegangen. Versuch's gleich nochmal." Every retry fails the same way.
- **Expected behavior:** A 16- or 17-year-old child profile is created (`docs/privacy.md` requires consent only under 16), never a server error.
- **Problem:** The code rule (age) and the schema rule (relation) contradict each other.
- **User impact:** Parents of 16- and 17-year-olds (the product targets grades 7–12) cannot finish onboarding. The only workaround, choosing "Ich selbst", mislabels the profile.
- **Concrete scenario:** `repro-11`: age 16 and age 17 → 500 with the server log naming `learners_child_consent`; age 12 → 201. Afterwards `/me` shows no learner.
- **Root cause:** Two sources of truth for who needs consent; no test covers a 16- or 17-year-old child.
- **Recommended fix:** Store consent for every `relation='child'` row, or relax the constraint in a new migration. Map 23514 to a 4xx. Add int tests for ages 16 and 17.
- **Blast radius:** Every account whose learner is a 16- or 17-year-old child.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced; `repro-11`).

### H-22 `trapped-at-consent-or-profile` — No way out before the profile exists

- **Severity:** High
- **Affected user flow:** Sign-in → consent → profile gate.
- **Location:** `apps/mobile/app/index.tsx:46-47` (redirects to `/consent` or `/profile`); `apps/mobile/app/consent.tsx` (only "Weiter", disabled until the box is ticked); `apps/mobile/app/profile.tsx` (no sign-out); the only `signOut` caller is `components/settings/AdultSection.tsx`, which cannot be reached without an account and a learner.
- **Current behavior:** A user who declines consent, or who signed in with the wrong account, is sent back to consent or profile on every start, with no control to leave. Only uninstalling or clearing site data ends the session.
- **Expected behavior:** Consent can be refused ("Nicht einverstanden" signs out), and the profile step allows switching account (UX-PRINCIPLES: control).
- **Problem:** Sign-out exists only inside the parents' area.
- **User impact:** A hard dead end for a parent who declines, or on a shared device signed in with the wrong e-mail.
- **Concrete scenario:** The parent reads the six consent points and decides not to agree → only a disabled "Weiter" → relaunch → `/consent` again, indefinitely.
- **Root cause:** Consent and profile screens were built for the happy path only.
- **Recommended fix:** Add a ghost `<Btn>` "Abmelden" to consent and profile, and a "Nicht einverstanden" that signs out. Optionally, delete accounts that never got a learner.
- **Blast radius:** Every user who stops between sign-in and profile completion.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (code-traced).

---

### Account deletion & retention

### H-23 `deletion-job-parks-failed-account-stuck` — An account deletion that fails three times is parked silently and never retried

- **Severity:** High
- **Affected user flow:** POST `/account/deletion` → 7-day hold → tick → `executeAccountDeletion`.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/identity/privacy.ts:118-119` (`storage.remove`, `deleteUser` throw generic errors), `:78` (a second request returns the old date and enqueues nothing); `apps/api/src/modules/scheduler/tick.ts:158-167` (`runJobSafely` parks the job at `attempts >= max_attempts`, line 163); `scheduler/jobs.ts:59-78` (default 3 attempts); `identity/routes.ts:66`; `apps/mobile/components/settings/AdultSection.tsx:57,186-213`.
- **Current behavior:** A storage or auth error lasting about 3 ticks (≈6 minutes) at the due time marks the job `failed`. `deletion_due_at` stays set in the past, and settings shows "Die Löschung ist geplant: ab <past date>". A new request is a no-op, and nothing alerts anyone (`/health` stays ok).
- **Expected behavior:** Erasure completes eventually (`docs/privacy.md` §deletion), or the failure becomes visible (rule 5).
- **Problem:** The deletion path has no terminal-failure handling, and the idempotent re-request blocks the only retry in the product.
- **User impact:** A parent who used the Art. 17 right believes the data is gone. Conversations, memories, photos and the login stay indefinitely.
- **Concrete scenario:** `repro-24`: storage throws → the job fails after 3 attempts; 24 h later, with storage healthy, it is still not retried. Account, learner, photo and auth user all remain; `/me` shows a past date; chat still works.
- **Root cause:** `delete_account` is treated like a best-effort job.
- **Recommended fix:** Retry with backoff without limit (or daily). Re-enqueue on request when no job is queued. Record and surface a deletion error in `/me` and `/health`. Delete the account even if photo removal fails, and queue the photo removal separately.
- **Blast radius:** Every account whose deletion hits a transient storage or auth error; all of its data.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced; `repro-24`).

### H-24 `p2-sec-deletion-over-1000-photos-never-completes` — Deletion sends every photo path ever stored in one unchunked request (includes `p2-T7`)

- **Severity:** High
- **Affected user flow:** Account deletion after the 7-day hold.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/identity/privacy.ts:113-118` (selects every `material_photos` row, including photos already purged, and calls `storage.remove` once); `apps/api/src/modules/materials/service.ts:624-636` (a purge only sets `photos_deleted_at`); `apps/api/src/storage/gateway.ts:62-66` (one `remove(paths)` call, throws on error).
- **Current behavior:** All historical paths go into one bulk delete. Where the Storage API's 1000-object limit is enforced, the request fails every time, and the job is then parked (H-23).
- **Expected behavior:** Skip purged paths, delete in chunks of at most 1000, treat "not found" as success.
- **Problem:** Erasure fails deterministically for exactly the accounts that hold the most data.
- **User impact:** After about a year of daily use, a family's deletion never completes and nobody is told.
- **Concrete scenario:** 1,050 `material_photos` rows, most already purged → `remove(1050)` rejected → job parked.
- **Root cause:** Historical rows are treated as live objects, and the bulk delete is assumed to be unbounded.
- **Recommended fix:** Select `photos_deleted_at is null`, chunk to at most 1000, ignore "not found". Add a fake that enforces the limit.
- **Blast radius:** Erasure of long-term accounts.
- **Confidence:** Partly confirmed. The code path (no filter, no chunking; also reported as `p2-T7`) is confirmed. Whether the hosted Storage API rejects more than 1000 prefixes depends on its hard-limit configuration; this needs runtime verification against the hosted project.

### H-25 `recovery-failed-material-never-purged` — The tick's recovery marks a material failed without scheduling its photo purge

- **Severity:** High
- **Affected user flow:** An extraction job dies (3 expired leases or 3 unexpected throws) → tick recovery.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/scheduler/tick.ts:66-71` (a bare `update materials … status='failed'`) vs `apps/api/src/modules/materials/service.ts:316-349` (`fail()` enqueues `purge:<id>` and bumps the context); feeders `scheduler/jobs.ts:65-78`, `tick.ts:158-167`.
- **Current behavior:** The material becomes `failed/model_error` with no purge job and no `bumpContext` (reproduced: 0 purge jobs, `context_version` unchanged, photo still in storage).
- **Expected behavior:** Photos are deleted 7 days after reading, "also when unreadable" (`docs/privacy.md:34`, `docs/architecture.md`).
- **Problem:** There are two ways a material becomes failed, and the one taken after infrastructure trouble is incomplete.
- **User impact:** Photos of a child's worksheet (name, class, handwriting) stay in the bucket until she deletes the sheet, retries successfully, or deletes the account. The bucket is service-role only, so nothing is exposed.
- **Concrete scenario:** `repro-14`: `storage.download` throws → the job fails after 3 attempts → the sweep marks the material `failed/model_error` with `photos_deleted_at` null → after 8 days of ticks the photo is still in storage and no purge job exists.
- **Root cause:** The terminal-failure bookkeeping lives in `fail()`, and the recovery re-implements only the status change.
- **Recommended fix:** Reuse `fail()` logic in the recovery (in one transaction). Add a maintenance safety net that enqueues a purge for any failed or ready material that still has photos.
- **Blast radius:** Every material whose extraction dies without reaching `fail()`.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced; `repro-14`). The original claim that retry is then refused (`retry_limit`) is wrong: lease retries reuse one job row, so retry returns 202, and a successful retry schedules the purge.

### H-26 `p2-ml-material-delete-is-soft-only` — "Blatt löschen" promises deletion but keeps the transcript, questions and answers

- **Severity:** High
- **Affected user flow:** Mein Stoff → Blatt löschen; Frage löschen; memory "Entfernen".
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/materials/service.ts:664-690` (`archiveMaterial` sets `archived_at` at :672 and :677 and purges only the photos); `apps/api/src/modules/identity/privacy.ts:14-52` (the export selects archived rows too); `apps/mobile/locales/de/library.json:29-32` ("… werden gelöscht … nicht rückgängig"); `docs/privacy.md:37`; `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/routes.ts:302-306` (a retracted memory keeps its statement and quote).
- **Current behavior:** `extracted_text`, title, items, solutions and her `practice_turns` stay in Postgres until the account is deleted, and they appear in the export. The only DELETE statements are for accounts and push tokens.
- **Expected behavior:** Deleted content is removed or redacted (at once or after a documented undo window), or the copy says it is only hidden.
- **Problem:** No erasure path exists for content the user deleted.
- **User impact:** A child who photographed something private by mistake, or a parent who deletes a sheet, believes it is gone. It stays and is exported. Item-level Art. 17 requests are silently not honoured.
- **Concrete scenario:** A page containing a note about a classmate → Löschen → "Gelöscht." → the export still contains `extracted_text`.
- **Root cause:** Soft delete keeps the practice history consistent, and no purge stage exists.
- **Recommended fix:** Add a content purge next to `purge_photos` (null the text and title, delete or redact the items and turn text), or change the copy and `privacy.md`. Do the same for retracted memories.
- **Blast radius:** Materials, items, practice_turns, buddy_memories; export; the privacy promises.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced). Includes the materials half of `p2-xs-delete-is-soft-archive-content-kept`.

---

### Auth resilience & offline

### H-27 `p2-api-verifier-outage-becomes-401` — The API turns any Supabase Auth failure into 401

- **Severity:** High
- **Affected user flow:** Every authenticated request, most visibly the launch gate (`/me`).
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/auth/verifier.ts:58` (`if (error || !data.user) return null`); `apps/api/src/http/context.ts:49` (null → `unauthenticated`); `apps/mobile/lib/api/client.ts:85-88` (a 401 triggers a refresh); `apps/mobile/lib/auth/supabase.ts:229-237`; `apps/mobile/app/_layout.tsx:46-53`.
- **Current behavior:** A network error, 5xx or timeout from Supabase Auth becomes 401. The app refreshes. If the refresh also fails, H-28 signs her out and wipes local data. If the refresh succeeds, the gate shows "Bitte melde dich neu an." with only a retry.
- **Expected behavior:** Only a definitely invalid token yields 401. An unreachable verifier yields 503 and the session is kept (rule 5).
- **Problem:** An infrastructure error is reported as an authentication failure.
- **User impact:** During an auth incident, every open app is signed out or dead-ends, and a child cannot sign back in without the parents' password.
- **Concrete scenario:** Supabase Auth returns 503 → GET `/me` 401 → refresh 503 → `clearSession` → drafts and outbox deleted.
- **Root cause:** `verify()` discards the error kind, and `requireUser` has a single failure code.
- **Recommended fix:** Throw `AppError('unavailable')` for network, 5xx and retryable errors, and return null only for invalid tokens. Add an int test where `verify` throws and expect 503.
- **Blast radius:** All authenticated routes and screens.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced against the real verifier with a 503 server and an unreachable port).

### H-28 `refresh-5xx-signs-out-and-wipes` — A 5xx or 429 during token refresh signs the learner out and deletes unsent answers and photos

- **Severity:** High
- **Affected user flow:** Session refresh (a minute before expiry, or after a 401) → `refreshSession` → `clearSession` → root listener.
- **Location:** `apps/mobile/lib/auth/supabase.ts:229-237` (the session is kept only when the reason is `network`), `:84` (`network` only for status undefined or 0); `apps/mobile/lib/api/client.ts:36-54`; `apps/mobile/app/_layout.tsx:46-53` (`clearOutbox()`, `drafts.clearAll()`, `queryClient.clear()`).
- **Current behavior:** auth-js reports 502/503/504 as `AuthRetryableFetchError` with that status, and 500/429 as `AuthApiError`. All of these become `unknown` or `rate_limited`, which leads to `clearSession`, and the listener deletes the outbox and draft photos.
- **Expected behavior:** Only definitive codes (`refresh_token_not_found`, `…_already_used`, `session_not_found`, invalid_grant) end the session. Transient failures keep the tokens, as the outbox already does for the API (`docs/architecture.md` §Offline).
- **Problem:** Transient failures are classified as final, and session loss destroys unsent work.
- **User impact:** Answers and worksheet photos made offline are lost for good, and the child is locked out until an adult signs in.
- **Concrete scenario:** `repro-26` ran the real `refreshSession` with stubbed fetch. 503 (8 calls over 25 s), 500 and 429 → session wiped and the listener fired; a thrown fetch → session kept.
- **Root cause:** `reasonOf` maps only status 0 or undefined to `network`, `error.code` is not passed in, and the layout wipes data on every session loss.
- **Recommended fix:** Treat retryable errors, status ≥ 500 and 429 as transient. Clear only on definitive codes. Clear the outbox and drafts only when a different user signs in.
- **Blast radius:** Every signed-in device; local data loss that cannot be recovered.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced; `repro-26`).

---

### Contact & reminders

### H-29 `p2-in-app-contact-not-counted` — With push off (the default), "schreib mir weniger" changes nothing

- **Severity:** High
- **Affected user flow:** Reducing contact in the chat ("fewer") or in settings (per week).
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/delivery.ts:57` (`COUNTED` excludes `in_app`), `:59-79` (`contactHistory`), `:311-338` (every row becomes `in_app` when push is disabled, no device exists, or the learner is in the app); `apps/api/src/config.ts:84` (`PUSH_BACKEND` defaults to `disabled`); `buddy/tools.ts:749`; copy `buddy.json` / `settings.json` "höchstens {{count}}× pro Woche".
- **Current behavior:** The cap is halved and the card promises "höchstens 2× pro Woche", but delivered in-app messages are never counted, so thread posts stay as frequent as before.
- **Expected behavior:** A request for fewer messages reduces Buddy-initiated posts, or the copy says the cap applies to phone messages only.
- **Problem:** The only "fewer" control has no effect in the shipped default. The child's explicit request is acknowledged and ignored.
- **User impact:** She asks for fewer messages, sees a confirmation, and gets the same amount.
- **Concrete scenario:** Harness with push disabled and `max_per_week=2`: one Buddy idea per day for 6 days was scheduled and delivered each day as `in_app`. `repro-02`: five checks 45 minutes apart gave five thread posts within three hours, including the same topic twice and three unanswered follow-ups. With push enabled, the same script gave 1 accepted and 4 suppressed.
- **Root cause:** In-app rows are left out of the history used for caps, so topic dedupe and the unanswered gate are bypassed too.
- **Recommended fix:** Count `in_app` rows in `contactHistory`, or reword the cap as "aufs Handy" and show the in-app rate separately.
- **Blast radius:** Every learner in the default deployment.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced; `repro-02`).

### H-30 `p2-reminder-practice-ignores-step-subject` — An agreed reminder prepares questions from any subject

- **Severity:** High
- **Affected user flow:** `plan_step` (agreed) → `step_due` → `sendAgreedReminder` prepares practice → "Übung bereit" card.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/decision.ts:262-274` (`plan_step` has no subject or topics); `buddy/tools.ts:549-553` (the step row has no payload); `buddy/check.ts:204-211` (`selectPracticeItems({goalId: step.goal_id})`); `practice/selection.ts:47-72`; `practice/service.ts:181-190`.
- **Current behavior:** With no goal, the selection is unscoped. A reminder titled "Englisch Vokabeln" prepares due maths items and says "6 Aufgaben liegen bereit".
- **Expected behavior:** Prepare questions for the named subject, or honestly send only "Wie verabredet: <title>" and ask for material.
- **Problem:** The model cannot express the scope of a reminder, and code fills the gap with whatever is due (a rule 5 claim).
- **User impact:** She asked to be reminded about English and gets fractions under an English title.
- **Concrete scenario:** Harness: a goal-less agreed step "Englisch Vokabeln" with only Mathe items → at 16:00 the step is prepared with 6 Mathe items → card "Übung bereit: Englisch Vokabeln".
- **Root cause:** No subject argument, and "no scope" falls back to "everything".
- **Recommended fix:** Add an optional subject alias and focus topics to `plan_step`, stored in the payload. Prepare nothing when neither a goal nor a subject is known. Add an int test.
- **Blast radius:** Every agreed practice reminder without a goal.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced).

### H-37 `agreed-reminder-vanishes` — An agreed reminder is silently dropped when a pause lands while it is deferred, when quiet hours are widened over it, or when the scheduler is more than 6 h late

- **Severity:** High (raised from medium on the `repro-15` evidence).
- **Affected user flow:** Agreed reminder (`step_due`) → `planOutreach` → `sendDueOutreach`; PATCH `/buddy/settings`; the `set_contact` tool.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/delivery.ts:97-98` (the in-app fallback only for `contact_disabled`/`paused` at planning time), `:239` (expiry sweep with no thread post), `:277` (send-time suppression), `:306` (quiet hours → `expired`); `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/policy.ts:127` (`no_slot`); `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/check.ts:242` (`expires_at = run_at + 6 h`), `:245` (`inAppWhenOff`); `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/routes.ts:428` and `tools.ts:760` (bulk cancel of every `scheduled` row, agreed ones included); promise in `docs/architecture.md:166-167`.
- **Current behavior:** The fallback "the reminder waits in the app" applies only when a pause or contact-off is already in force at the due minute. Every other terminal path writes no `buddy_messages` row: a pause while the reminder is deferred (bulk cancel), quiet hours widened over the agreed time (`no_slot`), and a tick more than 6 h late (`expired`). The step stays `planned` and the `step_due` job is `done`, so nothing retries.
- **Expected behavior:** "With contact off or paused the reminder waits in the app, as Buddy promised" (`docs/architecture.md` §Proactivity). A reminder the learner explicitly asked for at least appears in the thread, with honest copy if it is late.
- **Problem:** The in-app guarantee is enforced on one branch only. The send-time re-check, the expiry sweep and the bulk cancels were written for Buddy's own initiatives and do not distinguish `origin='agreed'`.
- **User impact:** She asks "erinnere mich Donnerstag um 17:30". If she or a parent pauses Buddy while the reminder waits for a slot, if quiet hours are moved earlier, or if the scheduler is down for over 6 h, the reminder never appears (no push, no thread message, no card), and she never learns it was dropped (rule 5).
- **Concrete scenario:** `repro-15` on the real harness:
  - (1A) Pause before the due tick: protected; the reminder was posted in the app.
  - (1B) Quiet hours 17:00–19:00, reminder deferred to 19:00, then a pause via PATCH: the row becomes `cancelled/paused`; after the pause there are no posts, the thread is empty and there are no push attempts.
  - (1B') `quiet_start` moved to 17:00 over an agreed 17:30: `suppressed/no_slot`, no post.
  - (1C) Tick 7 h late: `expired`, no post.
  - (1D) Tick 7 h late into quiet hours: `suppressed/no_slot`.
- **Root cause:** `inAppWhenOff` is evaluated only inside `planOutreach`, and only for two suppress reasons.
- **Recommended fix:** For `origin='agreed'`:
  1. Treat `no_slot` like contact-off (in_app + `postToThread`).
  2. In `sendDueOutreach`, replace suppressed/expired with in_app + `postToThread`.
  3. Exclude agreed rows from the bulk cancels, or convert them to in_app with a thread post.
  4. Use honest copy for a late reminder ("Ich wollte dich um 11 Uhr erinnern …").

  Add one integration test per path.

- **Blast radius:** Every agreed reminder, the one proactive feature promised without a model.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced; `repro-15`).

---

### Child safety

### H-31 `p2-T2` — No safeguarding path for a child's distress message

- **Severity:** High
- **Affected user flow:** Chat or talk turn: learner message → Gemini (safety settings) → reply or `failTurn`.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/prompts.ts:11-58` (nothing on distress, bullying or self-harm; ":22 You cannot contact other people"); `apps/api/src/llm/vertex.ts:37,41,49` (`BLOCK_MEDIUM_AND_ABOVE`), `:169,182,188` (`LlmError('blocked')`); `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/turn.ts:247` (`blocked` → `model_invalid`); `apps/mobile/locales/de/errors.json:41`, `buddy.json:101`; no eval case in `apps/api/evals/`.
- **Current behavior:** If the safety filter blocks the turn, the child sees "Nicht angekommen" and "Buddy hat sich verhaspelt. Schick's nochmal.", and each resend costs budget. If nothing is blocked, the model improvises with only "1–3 sentences, warm". A repo-wide grep for helpline, "Kummer", self-harm and similar terms finds nothing.
- **Expected behavior:** A stated, code-backed path: a calm reply that takes her seriously and points to a trusted adult or a helpline (e.g. Nummer gegen Kummer). A block becomes that fixed reply. At least one eval asserts it.
- **Problem:** This safety-relevant behaviour for minors exists in neither prompt, code nor evals. The only path it can reach is labelled a technical glitch.
- **User impact:** A child disclosing bullying or suicidal thoughts is either answered generically or told to send the message again.
- **Concrete scenario:** "die in meiner klasse sagen ich soll mich umbringen …" → blocked → `model_invalid` → "Schick's nochmal".
- **Root cause:** The prompts were written for learning only, every block is treated as model noise, and the fakes never emit `blocked`.
- **Recommended fix:** Add a distress rule to CORE. In `turn.ts`, map `blocked` on a learner message to a fixed, localised caring reply stored as Buddy's answer. Add eval cases and an int test that scripts `LlmError('blocked')`.
- **Blast radius:** Every learner, every locale, chat and talk mode.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced for the scripted `blocked` path; the absence of any safeguarding path is code-traced). Whether Vertex actually blocks such messages needs live verification.

### H-32 `p2-blocked-disclosure-poisons-thread` — A failed learner message stays in every later prompt, so one block can mute Buddy

- **Severity:** High
- **Affected user flow:** Chat and talk after a message that trips a provider filter.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/turn.ts:166-168` (the dialogue includes failed messages); `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/state.ts:236-244` (last 24 messages of any status); `apps/api/src/llm/vertex.ts:181-188`; `turn.ts:247`; `apps/mobile/components/buddy/Conversation.tsx:156-162`; `apps/api/src/llm/call.ts` (a block keeps its budget reservation).
- **Current behavior:** The failed message is sent again with each later turn, merged into the next user turn. If Vertex keeps blocking, every later message fails with "verhaspelt" until 24 newer rows exist.
- **Expected behavior:** A block of the child's own words gets a fixed, kind reply chosen by code, and the blocked text is replaced by a placeholder in later model dialogues.
- **Problem:** Failed messages count as context, and `blocked` shares an error code and copy with malformed output.
- **User impact:** The companion goes silent at the moment she confides something, and the copy tells her to repeat it.
- **Concrete scenario:** A bullying disclosure is blocked → "Nochmal senden" gives the same → "Wollen wir Mathe üben?" is also blocked.
- **Root cause:** Two deliberate design choices interact without anyone having considered safety blocks.
- **Recommended fix:** Add a separate `model_blocked` code with no resend. Exclude or mask blocked messages in the dialogue. Write a fixed reply chosen by code. Release the budget for blocks.
- **Blast radius:** Exactly the conversations about bullying, violence or self-harm.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced with scripted blocks). Whether Vertex blocks such disclosures in practice needs live verification.

### Data location & processors

### H-33 `supabase-region-london-not-eu` — The committed Supabase link points at London while privacy.md promises an EU region

- **Severity:** High
- **Affected user flow:** All data at rest, auth and storage for every learner.
- **Location:** `supabase/.temp/pooler-url` (tracked in git despite `.gitignore:59`, committed in ac0f6b7; host `aws-1-eu-west-2.pooler.supabase.com`); `supabase/.temp/linked-project.json`; `docs/privacy.md:63` ("EU region of the project"); `apps/api/src/config.ts:8-12` (the EU check exists only for Vertex).
- **Current behavior:** The only linked hosted project resolves to AWS eu-west-2 (London). The UK is not an EU member state. Nothing checks the database region at boot.
- **Expected behavior:** Minors' data lives in an EU member state as documented, or the doc states the real region. The region is enforced in code, like the Vertex location.
- **Problem:** The region is decided outside the repo, nothing verifies it, and the doc claims EU.
- **User impact:** Parents are told the data is stored in the EU. On the linked project it would be stored in the UK.
- **Concrete scenario:** The operator creates the project in London, links and deploys it, while the privacy text says EU.
- **Root cause:** No config or CI check on the project region. The link state also sits at the repo root, while `config.toml` and the migrations live in `infra/supabase`.
- **Recommended fix:** Use an EU region (e.g. eu-central-1) or correct `privacy.md` and the consent text. Add a boot check on the host against an EU allowlist. Remove `supabase/.temp` from git.
- **Blast radius:** All accounts.
- **Confidence:** Partly confirmed. The repo state (London) and the missing check hold. The live project could not be queried (no permission, and it does not appear in the accessible org), so a live London project holding learner data is not proven.

### H-34 `p2-ios-on-device-flag-dropped-for-other-locale` — iOS "strictly on-device" recognition silently falls back to Apple's servers for other locales

- **Severity:** High
- **Affected user flow:** Voice on iOS (chat mic, hands-free practice, talk) when the recognition language differs from the device language.
- **Location:** `apps/mobile/lib/speech/recognize.ts:34-36` (`installedLocales()` is null on iOS), `:52` (`supportsOnDeviceRecognition()` with no locale), `:211` (`requiresOnDeviceRecognition: true`); `apps/mobile/lib/speech/engine.ts:303-312`; `node_modules/expo-speech-recognition/ios/ExpoSpeechRecognitionModule.swift:352-355` (the check uses the device-locale recogniser); `ios/ExpoSpeechRecognizer.swift:588-590` (the flag is set only if that locale supports on-device, otherwise dropped); `docs/privacy.md:71-77`.
- **Current behavior:** The app decides "device" from a device-wide check. For a locale without on-device support (for example fr-FR on a German iPhone), the library omits the flag, and Apple's servers process the audio. No error is raised and nobody is told.
- **Expected behavior:** Use the system recogniser only when it can work on-device for that language; otherwise use the EU recording path.
- **Problem:** A minor's voice goes to a processor the privacy text says is never used.
- **User impact:** The privacy promise to parents is broken with no visible sign (online case only).
- **Concrete scenario:** German iPhone, French vocab answer in voice mode → `engineFor('fr-FR')` returns "device" → the flag is dropped → server recognition.
- **Root cause:** The app treats on-device support as device-wide while iOS reports it per locale, and the library degrades instead of failing.
- **Recommended fix:** Check support per locale (a small native call or a patched library that throws) and route unsupported locales to the recording path. Verify on a real iPhone. Until then, update `privacy.md`.
- **Blast radius:** iOS learners using the mic in a language other than the phone's dictation language.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (code-traced, including the bundled library source). Not run on a device.

### Deployment

### H-35 `vercel-deploy-config-invalid` — `vercel.json` has an invalid runtime, and the catch-all matches a single path segment only

- **Severity:** High
- **Affected user flow:** Deploying the API, and every request.
- **Location:** `apps/api/vercel.json:6-12` (`"runtime": "nodejs22.x"` at line 8; pattern `api/[[...slug]].ts`); `apps/api/api/[[...slug]].ts`; `README.md:51-52` (GET `/health`); `apps/api/src/app.ts:151`.
- **Current behavior:** Vercel's own detector (`@vercel/fs-detectors@7.7.3`) rejects the runtime with `invalid_function_runtime`, so the build fails. Without the runtime key, `[[...slug]]` becomes `^/api/([^/]+)$`, so multi-segment paths (`/api/buddy/messages`) return 404. `/health` at the root is outside the rewrite.
- **Expected behavior:** A deployment definition that has been exercised end to end once, or a note that it has not.
- **Problem:** The only production entry point has never been validated and contains a value the platform rejects.
- **User impact:** No API at all, or an API that returns 404 to the app. Visible to the operator at deploy time.
- **Concrete scenario:** Connect the repo with root `apps/api` → the build fails on the runtime validation.
- **Root cause:** The config was written from memory and never deployed.
- **Recommended fix:** Remove `runtime` (set Node through engines or project settings), use a routing pattern that matches nested paths, fix the README to `/v1/health`, and record a successful deploy.
- **Blast radius:** The entire hosted API.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced with Vercel's detector; no live deploy).

---

### Rendering

### H-36 `p2-function-plot-y-labels-offscreen` — Function graphs starting at x ≥ 0 draw every y-axis label outside the SVG

- **Severity:** High
- **Affected user flow:** A question with a `function_plot` figure → `FigureView` FunctionPlot.
- **Location:** `apps/mobile/components/math/FigureView.tsx:333` (`left = 8`), `:349` (`axisY = X(x0)` when 0 is not in range), `:481` and `:494` (labels at `axisY − 6`, anchored `end`).
- **Current behavior:** With `x_min ≥ 0`, the y-axis sits at x = 8. Every y tick label and the origin "0" are drawn at negative x and clipped. The grid lines show; the values do not.
- **Expected behavior:** Reserve a left margin as wide as the widest y label, or draw the labels inside the plot.
- **Problem:** A fixed 8 px padding, with labels placed left of the axis.
- **User impact:** A distance-time graph ("0–10 h, 0–200 km: Wie weit nach 3 h?") shows no y values, so the question cannot be answered from the figure.
- **Concrete scenario:** `{x_min:0, x_max:10, y_min:0, y_max:200, expr:'20*x'}` → the labels 50…200 end at x = 2 px.
- **Root cause:** The padding ignores label width.
- **Recommended fix:** Add the estimated label width to `left` when the axis is at the edge, or anchor the labels to the right of the axis.
- **Blast radius:** All first-quadrant plots (proportional functions, growth, distance-time) on all platforms.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (code-traced, including react-native-svg clipping; not rendered on a device).

## 6. Medium / Lower-Priority Findings

This section covers every live finding below HIGH in `final-rest.json`, plus two items from `final-top.json` that the editorial decisions place here:

- `repro-07`, narrowed from critical to medium per the second opinion (M-1).
- `repro-20`, reported at medium and merged into M-65.

Refuted claims (`final-refuted.json`) are not listed.

**6a input and consolidation.** 107 medium records + `repro-07` + `repro-20` = 109 records, consolidated into **85 medium entries (M-1 … M-85)**:

- **5 moved to §5.** They are the same defect as a high finding and are reported only there: `repro-02` → H-29 `p2-in-app-contact-not-counted`; `repro-12` → H-13 `draft-resume-dead-end`; `repro-14` → H-25 `recovery-failed-material-never-purged`; `p2-T7` → H-24 `p2-sec-deletion-over-1000-photos-never-completes`; `agreed-reminder-vanishes` (with `repro-15`) → H-37.
- **19 merged into another medium entry.** Each shares its root cause and code location with that entry. Runtime reproductions are cited under the descriptive id, following the BRIEF addendum: `repro-01`→M-45, `repro-06`→M-56, `repro-13`→M-13, `repro-16`→M-12, `repro-17`→M-47, `repro-19`→M-64, `repro-20`→M-65, `repro-21`→M-48, `repro-22`→M-49, `repro-23`→M-77, `repro-25`→M-75. `repro-28` keeps its own entry (M-53). Every merge is named in the entry's "Also covers" line.
- **6 low findings raised to medium.** Where a runtime reproduction proved a larger impact, its low-rated descriptive id is raised (see 6b).

**Line numbers.** About 40 medium citations were spot-checked against HEAD `62a6028`, and nearly all hold. The corrections applied inline:

- `apps/mobile/lib/auth/supabase.ts` has 246 lines, so the `:462-513` citations in M-73 are replaced by the real function lines.
- `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/stream.ts` has 53 lines (M-53).
- `apps/mobile/lib/push.ts` ends at :85 (M-64).
- `PinPad.tsx` has 120 lines (M-84).
- `Composer.tsx` clears the field at :45-46 (M-76).
- The `PUT /account/pin` freshness check is at `identity/routes.ts:194-199` (M-1).

**Confidence labels:** _Confirmed (runtime-reproduced)_ = a verifier ran code or tests that reproduced it; _Confirmed (code-traced)_ = verified by reading code across layers; _Partly confirmed_ = the entry states which part holds; _Needs runtime verification_ = the entry names the run needed.

**Severity notes** such as "(lowered from high)" record verification adjustments to the original rating.

### 6a. Medium findings

### 6a.1 Account, consent and adult gate

#### M-1 `account-change-gated-client-only` — Password change is gated only in the client, and a fresh password sign-in can overwrite an existing PIN (narrowed)

_Also covers:_ `repro-07` (runtime proof of the API half; severity lowered from critical per second opinion), `login-change-client-gated-pin-reset-chain`.

- **Severity:** Medium (narrowed). The fresh-sign-in PIN reset is an intended, tested recovery path (`apps/api/src/__tests__/identity.int.test.ts:143-158`). A child cannot reach the password sheet in the app without the PIN (`AccountAccessCard.tsx:54-56` → `confirmAdult`). Exploiting it needs tokens pulled out of Keychain/Keystore or the dev-only web build.
- **Affected user flow:** Parents' area → change password/e-mail → `PUT /account/pin`.
- **Location:** `apps/mobile/components/settings/AccountAccessCard.tsx:54-59`; `apps/mobile/lib/auth/supabase.ts:202-212` (`changePassword` → `supabase.auth.updateUser({password})`, no reauth nonce); `infra/supabase/config.toml` (no `secure_password_change`); `apps/api/src/modules/identity/routes.ts:194-199` (`fresh` sign-in < 300 s replaces `current_pin`).
- **Current behavior:** Anyone holding the device's access/refresh token can call Supabase `PUT /auth/v1/user {password}` directly. After signing in with the new password, `PUT /account/pin` without `current_pin` returns 200 (reproduced: stale session → 403 `pin_required`; fresh sign-in → 200; the old PIN then fails).
- **Expected behavior:** Credential changes are gated server-side (rule 1). Overwriting an existing PIN needs the current PIN or an explicit, reauthenticated "PIN vergessen" path.
- **Problem:** The PIN gate in front of password changes exists only in the UI. The API treats any fresh password sign-in as proof of adulthood.
- **User impact:** A technically capable minor can change the parents' password, set a new PIN and unlock every adult-gated action. The parents are then locked out of their own login.
- **Concrete scenario:** Child extracts the tokens → `PUT /auth/v1/user {password:'neu12345'}` → signs in → `PUT /v1/account/pin {pin:'1111'}` → 200 → `POST /account/admin-session` → admin token.
- **Root cause:** Supabase user updates bypass the API. The PIN route accepts `authenticatedAt < 300 s` as sufficient proof even when `pin_hash` exists.
- **Recommended fix:** Enable Supabase secure password change (reauthentication nonce) and call `reauthenticate()` in `changePassword`. Require `current_pin` whenever `pin_hash` exists, except in a dedicated recovery flow that notifies the account e-mail.
- **Blast radius:** Minor accounts whose device session can be extracted (rooted or jailbroken phones, dev web build).
- **Confidence:** Partly confirmed. The API half is runtime-reproduced (`repro-07`); "only client-gated, reachable by a child in the app" is refuted by the second opinion.

#### M-2 `child-can-reconsent-for-account-holder` — After a consent-version bump, whoever holds the phone re-consents for the account holder

_Also covers:_ low `minor-consent-never-refreshed`.

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** App start after a `CONSENT_VERSION` bump → `/consent` → `POST /account`.
- **Location:** `apps/mobile/app/index.tsx:46`; `apps/mobile/app/consent.tsx:42-49`; `apps/api/src/modules/identity/routes.ts:78-97` (only `requireUser`); `learners.minor_consent_version` is written only at learner creation (`routes.ts:137-138`).
- **Current behavior:** The child's ordinary session can accept the new version: `POST /account` returns 201 and updates `accounts.consent_version`. `learners.minor_consent_version` keeps the old version forever.
- **Expected behavior:** Re-consent for a minor's account needs the adult (PIN or fresh sign-in) and refreshes the minor-consent record in the same transaction (`docs/privacy.md:9-13`).
- **Problem:** A consent decision is enforced by screen order, not by code (rule 1), and two consent records drift apart.
- **User impact:** A 12-year-old can "accept" a materially changed privacy text on the parent's behalf, and the recorded Art. 8 consent stays outdated.
- **Concrete scenario:** Version bumped → Lena opens the app → ticks the box → 201. The parent never sees the new text.
- **Root cause:** `POST /account` has no notion of who is present; minor consent is written only once.
- **Recommended fix:** For accounts with a minor learner and a PIN, require `assertAccountHolder` on re-consent, update `minor_consent_version/at` in the same transaction, and show the PIN pad on the consent screen.
- **Blast radius:** All minor accounts at every consent-version bump.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced).

#### M-3 `p2-uf-profile-uncorrectable` — Name and birth date can never be corrected; a wrong birth year moves the end of minor protection

_Also covers:_ low `p2-ml-learner-profile-uncorrectable`.

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Settings → profile after onboarding; the adult gate.
- **Location:** `apps/mobile/components/settings/ProfileSection.tsx:27-80` (language only); `packages/shared-types/src/contracts/identity.ts:53-60` (`UpdateLearnerRequest` has no `birth_date`); `apps/api/src/modules/identity/routes.ts:155-183`.
- **Current behavior:** After `POST /learner` only the language can be changed. No screen sends `display_name`, and `birth_date` is immutable in contract and API.
- **Expected behavior:** A PIN-gated correction path for name and birth date (GDPR Art. 16) that re-validates the age/relation rules.
- **Problem:** Data that drives the permission model (`isMinor`) and every prompt is write-once.
- **User impact:** A mistyped birth year makes a child count as 16+ early: loosening contact, export and deletion then succeed without the PIN. A misspelled name persists in every Buddy message.
- **Concrete scenario:** Birth date entered as 2011 instead of 2013 → at 14 the API treats Lena as an account holder.
- **Root cause:** Commit 7f17569 removed the profile form without keeping a correction path; `birth_date` was never in the update contract.
- **Recommended fix:** Add a closed "Profil korrigieren" row under "Für Eltern" (admin token required), extend `UpdateLearnerRequest` with `birth_date`, and re-run the `learners_child_consent` check.
- **Blast radius:** Identity, the adult gate and every prompt.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (code-traced).

#### M-4 `no-erasure-without-learner` — Export and deletion require a learner profile, so abandoned-onboarding accounts cannot be erased

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Export/deletion for an account that consented but never created a profile.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/identity/routes.ts:250,258,268` (`requireLearner` on export and both deletion routes); `apps/mobile/app/settings.tsx:81`.
- **Current behavior:** `GET /account/export` and `POST /account/deletion` return 403 `learner_missing`, and settings are unreachable without a learner.
- **Expected behavior:** Account-level export and erasure (`privacy.ts:49` already handles `learner == null`).
- **Problem:** Route middleware demands a learner that the underlying functions do not need.
- **User impact:** A parent who stopped at the profile screen (or hit the 16/17 child 500, see §5 H-21 `child-16-17-profile-500`) cannot delete their e-mail and consent record.
- **Concrete scenario:** Sign up, consent, abandon → weeks later `POST /account/deletion` → 403.
- **Root cause:** `requireLearner` applied to the privacy routes for convenience.
- **Recommended fix:** Drop `requireLearner` from these routes (keep `requireAccount`) and offer sign-out/delete on the consent and profile screens (see §5 H-22 `trapped-at-consent-or-profile`).
- **Blast radius:** Accounts without a completed profile.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced).

#### M-5 `set-contact-fewer-raises-cap-from-zero` — "Weniger Nachrichten" raises a weekly cap of 0 to 1 without the PIN

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Chat "schreib mir weniger" → `set_contact{fewer:true}`.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/tools.ts:749` (`Math.max(1, floor(max_per_week/2))`); contract allows 0 (`packages/shared-types/src/contracts/buddy.ts:358`).
- **Current behavior:** With `max_per_week = 0` (set by an adult via `PATCH /settings`), the tool writes 1.
- **Expected behavior:** Buddy can only reduce contact (rule 6, `docs/architecture.md` §Tools).
- **Problem:** The floor of 1 inverts the direction when the cap is already 0.
- **User impact:** A minor's message re-enables initiatives the adult switched off via the cap.
- **Concrete scenario:** Parent PATCHes `max_per_week:0` → child says "bitte weniger Nachrichten" → cap becomes 1.
- **Root cause:** `Math.max(1, …)` instead of `min(current, …)`.
- **Recommended fix:** Use `Math.min(s.max_per_week, Math.max(1, floor(s.max_per_week/2)))`, and add tests for 0 and 1.
- **Blast radius:** Only caps of 0, which the app UI cannot produce (picker offers 1–4) but the public contract can.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced).

#### M-6 `p2-sec-anon-rpc-lb-invoke-tick` — `lb_invoke_tick` (SECURITY DEFINER, public schema) is executable with the app's anon key

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Scheduler trigger pg_cron → `lb_invoke_tick` → pg_net → `POST /internal/tick`.
- **Location:** `infra/supabase/migrations/0001_baseline.sql:587-606` (only `revoke all … from public`); `infra/supabase/config.toml:5-7` (public schema exposed).
- **Current behavior:** Supabase grants EXECUTE on public functions to `anon`/`authenticated` by default. `POST /rest/v1/rpc/lb_invoke_tick` with the anon key makes the database send an authenticated tick.
- **Expected behavior:** Only pg_cron can call it, as `docs/privacy.md` states ("the anon/authenticated keys … can read or write nothing directly").
- **Problem:** A privileged side effect is reachable from a key shipped in the app bundle.
- **User impact:** Anyone can loop full ticks (Vercel function up to 300 s, 1 GB each). The likely effects are cost, pool exhaustion and delayed real ticks for every learner.
- **Concrete scenario:** `curl -X POST https://<project>.supabase.co/rest/v1/rpc/lb_invoke_tick -H 'apikey: <anon>'` in a loop.
- **Root cause:** The migration assumed `revoke … from public` removes all access.
- **Recommended fix:** In a new migration, `revoke execute … from anon, authenticated` or move the function into a non-exposed schema. Add a test asserting no public function is executable by `anon`.
- **Blast radius:** Availability and cost for all learners.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced). Grant semantics were reproduced on local Postgres; the hosted grant state was not queried.

#### M-7 `database-tls-not-enforced` — The API's Postgres connection has no TLS setting

_Also covers:_ `database-url-tls-unenforced`.

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Every API query to the Supabase pooler.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/lib/db.ts:78-84` (`pg.Pool({connectionString, …})`, no `ssl`); `apps/api/src/config.ts:38`; `apps/api/.env.example:6-8`.
- **Current behavior:** node-pg uses TLS only if the URL carries `sslmode`. The pooler URL shown in the Supabase dashboard has none, and nothing validates or documents it.
- **Expected behavior:** Production refuses a non-TLS database URL, or forces `ssl: {rejectUnauthorized: true}`.
- **Problem:** Encryption in transit is a silent, unenforced prerequisite.
- **User impact:** Children's conversations, memories and birth dates could cross Vercel → Supabase in clear text after a plausible operator paste.
- **Concrete scenario:** Operator copies the dashboard pooler URL into the Vercel env; the API boots fine.
- **Root cause:** `createDb` passes the string through; config validation does not check it.
- **Recommended fix:** Require `sslmode=require` in production config (or set `ssl` explicitly), document it in `.env.example`, and enable "Enforce SSL" on the project.
- **Blast radius:** All learner data in transit.
- **Confidence:** Partly confirmed. The code has no TLS setting; whether the hosted pooler accepts plaintext depends on the project's dashboard setting (not checked).

### 6a.2 Privacy and retention

#### M-8 `p2-J-memory-F3` — Removed, corrected and expired memories are only flagged, kept until account deletion and exported

_Also covers:_ the memory half of `p2-xs-delete-is-soft-archive-content-kept` and `p2-sec-soft-delete-contradicts-deleted-copy`. The sheet/question half is the same defect as §5 H-26 `p2-ml-material-delete-is-soft-only` and is reported there. Also folds the low `p2-forget-keeps-statement-everywhere`.

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** "Was Buddy weiß" → Entfernen/Bearbeiten; `forget`/`correct_memory` tools; temporary situations ending; parents' export.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/routes.ts:297-317` (status → `retracted`/`superseded`, statement and quote kept); `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/tools.ts:284-286,315-317`; `docs/privacy.md:31`.
- **Current behavior:** Removing or correcting a memory only changes its status. The statement, the child's verbatim quote and `source_message_id` stay in `buddy_memories` and appear in the export. Expired constraints stay `active` forever.
- **Expected behavior:** Content is erased (or statement/quote nulled) after the 7-day undo window, matching `privacy.md:31` and the toast "Entfernt. Buddy vergisst das." Alternatively, the doc states the real retention.
- **Problem:** The UI copy and the privacy doc promise erasure; storage keeps the data indefinitely.
- **User impact:** Sensitive facts about a minor ("Hat ADHS", "Eltern lassen sich scheiden") that the family removed reappear in the export.
- **Concrete scenario:** Parent removes "Hat ADHS" → toast "Buddy vergisst das" → a month later the export contains the row with status `retracted` and the child's quote.
- **Root cause:** Soft-delete was chosen for undo/audit; no retention step followed the undo window and the doc was not updated.
- **Recommended fix:** Add a scheduler job that, after 7 days, deletes (or blanks) retracted/superseded rows and expired constraints. Check copies in `buddy_actions`/`buddy_decisions` too. Otherwise correct `privacy.md`.
- **Blast radius:** All learners; every remove, correct or forget.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (code-traced).

#### M-9 `p2-disclosures-become-memories` — Nothing keeps distress, abuse or health disclosures out of persistent memory

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** `remember` tool → `buddy_memories` → STATE of every turn/check → outreach; memory screen; export.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/prompts.ts:36-37`; `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/decision.ts:169-186` (any kind, no category restriction); `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/tools.ts:227-274` (only the quote check).
- **Current behavior:** The model decides what to remember; code checks only that a ≥2-character quote occurs in the latest message. A disclosure such as "Wird zu Hause geschlagen" can be stored, shown and exported.
- **Expected behavior:** Special-category and safeguarding content is not stored automatically and never feeds lock-screen text. `privacy.md` names how it is handled.
- **Problem:** No rule in code or prompt excludes sensitive disclosures, and once stored they feed every later prompt and check.
- **User impact:** A child's abuse disclosure could become a visible profile fact, appear in the parents' export (possibly to the adult involved) or colour a push body.
- **Concrete scenario:** "Mein Papa hat mich wieder geschlagen" → `remember{kind:'fact', statement:'Wird zu Hause geschlagen', quote:'geschlagen'}` passes `requireQuote`.
- **Root cause:** The memory tool was designed for scheduling facts; its only guard is quote provenance.
- **Recommended fix:** Add a prompt and tool-description rule against remembering health, family-conflict, abuse or self-harm details. Add a structured `sensitive` flag that code refuses or keeps out of check STATE, and document the category in `privacy.md` (links to §5 H-31 `p2-T2`).
- **Blast radius:** Every learner who confides something personal.
- **Confidence:** Partly confirmed. The code and prompt gap is confirmed; whether the model actually stores such statements needs a live eval.

#### M-10 `delete-during-upload-orphans-photos` — Photos that arrive after a sheet was deleted mid-upload stay in storage indefinitely

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Capture send (create → signed upload → submit) while the learner deletes the "unvollständig" row in Mein Stoff.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/materials/service.ts:664-690` (`archiveMaterial`, one purge job keyed `purge:<id>:archived`); `apps/api/src/modules/scheduler/jobs.ts:49-62` (a done key is never re-planned); `apps/api/src/storage/gateway.ts:30-38` (signed upsert URLs stay valid).
- **Current behavior:** The library lists the in-flight material as "unvollständig – Lösch das Blatt …". Deleting it purges only the photos present at that moment; later PUTs succeed through still-valid URLs and nothing purges them.
- **Expected behavior:** Photos of a deleted sheet are removed immediately (`docs/privacy.md:34`), including late arrivals.
- **Problem:** The purge is a one-shot job, and the uploads outlast the delete.
- **User impact:** A parent believes the photos are gone; some remain until account deletion.
- **Concrete scenario:** 6-photo send → delete at +5 s → purge removes photo 0 → photos 1–5 arrive at +8…30 s and stay.
- **Root cause:** No archived guard on later uploads (the server does not see PUTs) and no second purge.
- **Recommended fix:** Enqueue a distinct `purge:<id>:late` on submit/abandon of an archived material, or add a safety-net sweep. Hide young `awaiting_upload` rows from the library (see M-20).
- **Blast radius:** Any delete during a multi-photo upload.
- **Confidence:** Partly confirmed. The mechanism is traced; the exact interleaving on hosted Storage was not run.

#### M-11 `p2-export-share-too-large-android` — A real account's export cannot be delivered on Android (share Intent exceeds the Binder limit)

_Also covers:_ `p2-export-payload-exceeds-platform-limits`; lows `export-web-no-delivery` and `repro-27` (on web the fetched export is thrown away when `navigator.share` is missing; web is a declared non-target).

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Für Eltern → PIN → "Export erstellen" (DSGVO Art. 15/20).
- **Location:** `apps/mobile/components/settings/AdultSection.tsx:90-98` (`Share.share({ message: JSON.stringify(data, null, 2) })`); RN `ShareModule.kt` (`EXTRA_TEXT`).
- **Current behavior:** The whole JSON goes into `Intent.EXTRA_TEXT`. Above roughly 500 KB, Android throws `TransactionTooLargeException`, the app shows "Teilen hat nicht geklappt. Versuch es nochmal." and every retry fails the same way.
- **Expected behavior:** Write the export to a file and share the file URI (web: Blob download). Verify the 4.5 MB Vercel response ceiling.
- **Problem:** The legally required export depends on a text share sheet with a platform size cap.
- **User impact:** Parents of any learner who used the app for a while cannot obtain their data on Android.
- **Concrete scenario:** Account with ~100 turns and 30 materials → GET 200 (~3–6 MB) → share fails → toast, and it repeats.
- **Root cause:** The export is delivered as an in-memory string, with no size consideration.
- **Recommended fix:** Use expo-file-system + expo-sharing with a file URI, gzip the API response, and add a heavy-account check.
- **Blast radius:** All Android users beyond a small account.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (code-traced). The Binder limit is platform behaviour and was not run on a device.

### 6a.3 Materials and capture

#### M-12 `merged-part-no-wake` — Pages added to an existing sheet never wake Buddy

_Also covers:_ `repro-16` (runtime proof), `added-pages-do-not-wake-buddy`, `second-page-batch-does-not-wake-buddy`, `merged-pages-do-not-wake-buddy`. The descriptive id was rated low in phase 1 and is raised to medium with the runtime proof.

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** "Seite hinzufügen" / re-photographed page → extraction → `emitEvent` → `buddy_check`.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/materials/service.ts:454-461` (`home = target ?? current`), `:586-594` (`emitEvent` with `materialId: home.id`); `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/events.ts:70-78` (`on conflict (type, ref_id) do nothing`).
- **Current behavior:** The part's event uses the root sheet's id, which already has a `material_ready` event, so no check runs and no practice or message is produced for the new questions.
- **Expected behavior:** Each reading wakes Buddy once, as page 1 did (`docs/architecture.md` §Events).
- **Problem:** Event identity conflates "this sheet was first read" with "new questions were read".
- **User impact:** Lena photographs page 2 (often the harder tasks), and Buddy stays silent while the prepared practice keeps only page-1 items.
- **Concrete scenario:** Mon: sheet S read → check → message. Tue: add page → items merged into S → `emitEvent(material_ready, S)` → conflict → no check (reproduced: 1 `buddy_check` call total).
- **Root cause:** `ref_id = home.id` for parts; `(type, ref_id)` uniqueness is also hit by legitimate later batches.
- **Recommended fix:** Emit with the part's own id and carry `root_id` in data; resolve the root in `describeTriggers`.
- **Blast radius:** Every added page of study material.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced).

#### M-13 `delete-during-extraction-ready` — Deleting a sheet while it is being read still produces a ready sheet, live items, a done capture step and a wake-up

_Also covers:_ `repro-13` (runtime proof), `delete-during-extraction-resurrects`.

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Extraction job running (1–2 min) + Library → Löschen.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/materials/service.ts:357` (archived check only before the model call), `:448-452` (final transaction re-checks only `status === 'ready'`), `:586-594` (`emitEvent`).
- **Current behavior:** The final transaction inserts un-archived items for an archived material, sets `ready`, marks the capture step done with evidence of a deleted sheet and enqueues a `buddy_check` (reproduced: `status:'ready', archived_at` set, 2 live items).
- **Expected behavior:** A deleted material never becomes ready, never feeds Buddy's picture and never triggers a message.
- **Problem:** The final transaction does not re-check `archived_at`.
- **User impact:** Buddy may announce "Dein Blatt ist fertig" or prepare practice for a sheet she just deleted, and it will not ask for the photo again.
- **Concrete scenario:** t0 submit → t0+20 s Löschen → t0+90 s model returns → items inserted, `material_ready` → check.
- **Root cause:** `service.ts:452` guards only against a concurrent successful run.
- **Recommended fix:** In the final transaction, return as `nothing_to_do` when `current.archived_at` is set; also filter `archived_at` in `check.ts` material lookups.
- **Blast radius:** Any delete within the reading window.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced).

#### M-14 `budget-refusals-consume-retry-runs` — Budget refusals and provider outages use up a sheet's three reading runs

_Also covers:_ `p2-ux-narrative-outage-day` / `p2-F-journey-extraction-outage-burns-runs` (the same run accounting during a model incident).

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Photo submit/retry when the 12/day extraction budget is spent or the provider is down.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/materials/service.ts:294-299` (`retryMaterial` counts every `extract_material` job), `:422-423` (`budget_exhausted` → `fail()`), `:424-433` (retryable errors retried, then `model_error`).
- **Current behavior:** A refused or outage-failed run counts toward `MAX_EXTRACTION_ATTEMPTS = 3`. Two taps today give 409 `retry_limit` tomorrow although the copy says "Morgen geht's wieder".
- **Expected behavior:** Runs that made no model call, or failed because of the provider, do not count; the retry button hides until the next local day when the budget is spent.
- **Problem:** Run counting does not distinguish refused or outage attempts from executed ones.
- **User impact:** The sheet becomes unreadable, and the child must re-photograph it after doing exactly what the app suggested.
- **Concrete scenario:** 13th sheet of the day fails on budget → two retries fail instantly → next morning "Das wurde schon oft versucht" (reproduced).
- **Root cause:** `count(*) from jobs` includes budget refusals and provider-outage runs.
- **Recommended fix:** Exclude jobs whose result is `budget_exhausted`/provider-unavailable from the count, or defer to the next local midnight without counting.
- **Blast radius:** Learners hitting the daily cap; everyone during a provider incident.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced).

#### M-15 `retry-after-purge-false-claim` — Retry is offered after the 7-day photo purge and then fails with the false "photos never arrived"

_Also covers:_ `retry-after-purge-false-photos-missing`, `retry-after-purge-misleading-dead-end`.

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Library → failed sheet → "Nochmal lesen".
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/materials/service.ts:275-313` (`retryMaterial`, no `photos_deleted_at` check), `:624-636` (`purgePhotos` sets `photos_deleted_at`); `apps/mobile/components/library/MaterialCard.tsx:78`.
- **Current behavior:** A sheet that failed 8+ days ago still offers retry. The run downloads nothing and fails as `photos_missing` ("Nicht alle Fotos sind angekommen"), using up a run each time.
- **Expected behavior:** After retention the state reads "Fotos sind gelöscht – fotografier es neu" and offers no retry (rule 5).
- **Problem:** Retention state is not part of the retry precondition or the failure vocabulary.
- **User impact:** False cause, wasted attempts, and finally `retry_limit`.
- **Concrete scenario:** Fails on day 0 → purge on day 7 → retry on day 9 → "photos not arrived" → repeat → limit.
- **Root cause:** `retryMaterial` ignores `photos_deleted_at`; `MaterialView` does not expose it.
- **Recommended fix:** Return 409 `photos_deleted` when purged, and expose the flag so the card hides retry.
- **Blast radius:** Every failed sheet older than 7 days.
