- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced).

#### M-16 `storage-errors-reported-as-missing-photos` — Any Storage error is treated as "photo absent", so an outage fails readings permanently

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** `submitMaterial` existence check and `runExtraction` download.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/storage/gateway.ts:49-50` (list error ignored via `data ?? []`), `:56-59` (download error → `null`); `apps/api/src/modules/materials/service.ts:247-254`, `:379-380`.
- **Current behavior:** A Storage 5xx during extraction fails the sheet as terminal `photos_missing` and schedules the 7-day purge.
- **Expected behavior:** Transient provider errors are retried like retryable model errors, not blamed on the learner.
- **Problem:** The gateway conflates absence with error (`Promise<Uint8Array | null>`).
- **User impact:** During a Storage incident every sheet fails with "Die Fotos sind nicht ganz angekommen", and children re-photograph pages that are in the bucket.
- **Concrete scenario:** Storage returns 500 on the first download → failed `photos_missing` → Lena retakes the photos.
- **Root cause:** `gateway.ts` swallows the error object.
- **Recommended fix:** Throw a `StorageError` for non-404 failures, retry the job (counting the attempt), and answer 503 on list errors.
- **Blast radius:** All uploads during any Storage error window.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (code-traced).

#### M-17 `p2-T8` — Safety-filter blocks on ordinary school material are untested and end in a futile retry loop

_Also covers:_ low `p2-blocks-look-like-outages`.

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Photo → extraction → failed → "Nochmal lesen"; the tutor on the same items.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/llm/vertex.ts:34-51` (`SEXUALLY_EXPLICIT` `BLOCK_LOW_AND_ABOVE`), `:183-188` (non-STOP → `blocked`); `apps/api/src/modules/materials/service.ts:421-434` (non-retryable → `model_error`), `:289` (retry offered).
- **Current behavior:** A block is shown as a generic reading failure with retry. Each retry sends the same photos, gets the same block and uses a run and budget.
- **Expected behavior:** Curriculum content either passes, or fails with an honest final message and no retry; the path has a test.
- **Problem:** The strict thresholds' effect on children's schoolwork is unobserved, and a block is presented as a glitch.
- **User impact:** A grade-6 biology (Sexualkunde) or history sheet could fail three times with no explanation before an exam.
- **Concrete scenario:** "Die Geschlechtsorgane des Menschen" → SAFETY → `model_error` → retry repeats it.
- **Root cause:** `blocked` is lumped into generic model errors, and the fakes never emit it.
- **Recommended fix:** Add a non-retryable `failure_reason: 'blocked'` with honest copy, int tests scripting `LlmError('blocked')`, and an extraction eval on real biology/history sheets.
- **Blast radius:** Biology, history, ethics and literature material.
- **Confidence:** Needs runtime verification. The code path is confirmed; run real curriculum sheets through Vertex to see whether they are blocked.

#### M-18 `homework-failed-new-photo-becomes-study` — The failed-reading card forgets purpose and target sheet, so "Neues Foto" creates the wrong kind of sheet

_Also covers:_ `p2-J-04` (a failed retake/added page becomes an orphan "Ohne Titel" sheet and "Neues Foto" loses the link to the root).

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Home `material_failed` card → "Neues Foto".
- **Location:** `apps/mobile/components/buddy/NowCard.tsx:123-147` (`onCapture(null, null)`); `packages/shared-types/src/contracts/buddy.ts:201-206` (card carries no `purpose`/`completes`/title); `apps/mobile/app/capture.tsx:82-89` (defaults to study); `apps/api/src/modules/materials/service.ts:316-349` (failed part keeps no title).
- **Current behavior:** Re-photographing failed homework opens a study capture, so the homework becomes drillable practice with revealed solutions. Re-photographing a failed part creates an unrelated sheet, and the failed part stays as an untitled failure.
- **Expected behavior:** "Neues Foto" re-opens capture with the same purpose and `completes`; the card names the sheet and says only the new page failed.
- **Problem:** Purpose and sheet identity are lost across the failure card.
- **User impact:** Homework solutions get revealed (breaking the homework contract), duplicate or split sheets appear, and missing pages are never merged.
- **Concrete scenario:** Homework photo → `model_error` → "Neues Foto" → study material → practice with "Lösung zeigen".
- **Root cause:** The `material_failed` contract lacks `purpose`/`completes`/title, and `onCapture(null,null)` discards context.
- **Recommended fix:** Add `purpose`, `completes` and the root title to the card (from `state.materials`), pass them to `/capture`, and hide failed parts from the library.
- **Blast radius:** Every failed homework reading and every failed part extraction.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (code-traced).

#### M-19 `p2-old-failed-card-hides-new-reading` — A failed sheet from the last 24 h outranks the sheet being read, and "Nochmal lesen" retries the old one

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Send a new sheet within 24 h of a failed one → home now card.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/home.ts:196-225` (failed < 24 h checked before `queued/processing`); `apps/mobile/components/buddy/NowCard.tsx:123-147` (no title).
- **Current behavior:** The card says "Das Blatt konnte ich nicht lesen" for yesterday's sheet while the new one is being read; its primary action retries the old sheet.
- **Expected behavior:** The current reading wins, or the failure card names its sheet and is limited to failures newer than the latest upload.
- **Problem:** Fixed card precedence plus a title-less failure card.
- **User impact:** She thinks the new photo failed, uses up the old sheet's runs, or re-photographs the new sheet.
- **Concrete scenario:** A fails at t0 → B sent at t0+3 h → now card shows A failed → tap → `POST /materials/A/retry`.
- **Root cause:** Ordering in `nowCardOf` and a missing material identity on the card.
- **Recommended fix:** Order processing (< 2 h) before failed, or scope failed to materials newer than any in-flight one; add the title to the card. Update `architecture.md` accordingly.
- **Blast radius:** Anyone re-photographing within 24 h of a failure (the usual recovery path).
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (code-traced).

#### M-20 `orphan-awaiting-upload-rows` — Changing the photo set after a failed send abandons the reserved material, which then shows as "unvollständig" and "wird gesendet"

_Also covers:_ `library-orphan-awaiting-upload`.

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Send fails → remove/add a photo → send again.
- **Location:** `apps/mobile/app/capture.tsx:154-157` (`photosChanged` drops the `MaterialUpload`); `apps/mobile/lib/capture/upload.ts:140` (new `client_request_id`); `apps/api/src/modules/materials/service.ts:692-705` (library lists `awaiting_upload`).
- **Current behavior:** The first reserved material stays `awaiting_upload`. The library asks her to delete it, and home shows "wird gesendet" and polls every 3 s for up to 10 min.
- **Expected behavior:** The client releases the abandoned reservation, or the server hides young `awaiting_upload` rows; the learner never cleans up system leftovers.
- **Problem:** The client abandons a reservation without telling the server, and the server shows every reservation.
- **User impact:** A confusing duplicate entry with an instruction to delete it, plus a false "sending" claim (rule 5).
- **Concrete scenario:** 3 photos → photo 2 fails → she removes it → resend → M2 created; M1 stays listed.
- **Root cause:** No `DELETE` of the abandoned material and no server-side filter.
- **Recommended fix:** Fire-and-forget `DELETE /materials/:id` when an instance with a reserved material is dropped; show `material_processing` only while a send is in flight.
- **Blast radius:** Any failed send followed by an edit of the photo set.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced).

#### M-21 `fresh-capture-discards-draft-silently` — A new capture silently deletes the previous draft, even while that draft is still being uploaded

_Also covers:_ `fresh-capture-silently-discards-draft`, `p2-lc-fresh-capture-kills-inflight-send`.

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Leftover draft (or background send) → start another capture from the now card, library or "Seite hinzufügen".
- **Location:** `apps/mobile/app/capture.tsx:146-149` (`touch()` → `drafts.discard()` when `!resume`), `:258-289` (send continues after unmount); `apps/mobile/lib/capture/draft.ts:116-120` (discard deletes files).
- **Current behavior:** The first photo of a fresh capture deletes the stored draft and its files with no notice or undo. If that draft is mid-send, the running upload then fails on the missing file.
- **Expected behavior:** "Closing the app loses nothing" (`docs/architecture.md` §Material); undo over confirmation; a requested send finishes.
- **Problem:** One draft slot is replaced silently, and `discard()` ignores the in-flight `sending` set.
- **User impact:** Yesterday's three pages vanish, or the sheet being sent is lost and an error toast appears on the new capture.
- **Concrete scenario:** 5 pages sending → back → camera → 1 photo → files deleted → upload fails on photo 3.
- **Root cause:** Unconditional discard for non-resume captures.
- **Recommended fix:** Skip deletion when `draft.requestId` is in `sending`. Offer "Weiter mit den 3 Fotos von gestern", or discard only when the new draft is sent, with an undo.
- **Blast radius:** Anyone with a leftover or in-flight draft who starts another capture.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (code-traced).

#### M-22 `p2-lc-android-camera-kill-loses-photo` — On Android, the photo is lost when the OS kills the app while the camera is open

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Capture → camera on memory-constrained Android phones.
- **Location:** `apps/mobile/app/capture.tsx:193-198` (awaits `launchCameraAsync`); no `ImagePicker.getPendingResultAsync` call anywhere (grep).
- **Current behavior:** When Android recreates the activity, the JS promise is gone, the app restarts at `/` and the shot is never read.
- **Expected behavior:** Recover the pending camera result on start and return to capture with the photo added.
- **Problem:** No recovery path for activity recreation, and navigation state is not persisted.
- **User impact:** On cheap phones (common for children), each shot can bounce her to home without the photo.
- **Concrete scenario:** 2 GB phone → camera → LearnBuddy killed → shot confirmed → app relaunches → no photo.
- **Root cause:** The documented `getPendingResultAsync` path is not implemented.
- **Recommended fix:** Call `getPendingResultAsync()` on Android start, append the assets to the draft and open `/capture?resume=1`.
- **Blast radius:** Android users on low-memory devices.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (code-traced). Not run on a device.

#### M-23 `p2-merged-part-page-numbers-wrong-sheet-page` — Retake/add-page batches number problem pages by batch position, not by sheet page

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Pages-not-read notice → retake or add page → part merged → second notice.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/materials/service.ts:440` (`pageProblemsOf` uses the part's own positions), `:507-512` (part gets the root title); `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/state.ts:276-281`.
- **Current behavior:** A 2-photo retake of sheet pages 2 and 4 where the second is blurry again reports "Seite 2" under the root title.
- **Expected behavior:** Page numbers refer to the physical sheet, or the notice names no number.
- **Problem:** Nothing translates part positions into sheet pages.
- **User impact:** She re-photographs a page that was already fine; the failed page is never read, and the notice counts as resolved.
- **Concrete scenario:** 4-page sheet, pages 2 and 4 blurry → retake both → p4 blurry again → notice says "Seite 2".
- **Root cause:** `page_problems` stores model photo positions; the page numbers a part stands for are never sent to the server.
- **Recommended fix:** Send per-photo sheet page numbers with `createMaterial`, store them on `material_photos` and translate in `pageProblemsOf`. Until then, drop the number from the copy.
- **Blast radius:** Every multi-photo retake/add batch that fails again.
- **Confidence:** Partly confirmed. Runtime-reproduced on the harness for batches of two or more; single-photo retakes are unaffected.

#### M-24 `p2-model-page-numbers-unlabeled-images` — Page numbers rely on the model counting unlabeled images

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Extraction → `page_problems` → notice page number and thumbnail.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/materials/service.ts:373-412` (one text part, then bare `inlineData` images); `apps/api/src/modules/materials/extract.ts:47` ("Position of the photo, starting at 1").
- **Current behavior:** With up to 20 photos, a miscount names the wrong page, and the thumbnail uses the same wrong index, so text and picture agree on the wrong page.
- **Expected behavior:** Each image is preceded by a "Foto k von n" text part.
- **Problem:** The thumbnail cannot reveal a miscount.
- **User impact:** On long sheets she retakes a good page and the unread page is lost.
- **Concrete scenario:** 8 pages, page 6 glared → model reports page 5 → notice shows photo 5 (clean).
- **Root cause:** Page identity is left to implicit counting; the server checks only `page ≤ photo_count`.
- **Recommended fix:** Interleave labels before each image and cross-check `pages.length === photo_count`.
- **Blast radius:** Multi-photo materials (the eval covers only 2 pages).
- **Confidence:** Needs runtime verification. The code is confirmed; measure miscount rates with 8–20 photo sheets on the live model.

#### M-25 `pages-notice-expiry-vs-context` — The pages notice expires 24 h after upload, not after reading, while Buddy's context keeps describing it

_Also covers:_ `pages-notice-expires-context-lies`.

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Extraction reports `page_problems` → home notice → Buddy context.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/home.ts:113-121` (`created_at < 24 h`); `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/context.ts:224-227` ("the learner sees a card …", no time bound).
- **Current behavior:** A reading that finishes late (retries, or a next-day retry) never shows the notice. After the window, Buddy's STATE still claims a retake card exists.
- **Expected behavior:** The window runs from `ready_at`, and the context describes only what the learner can see.
- **Problem:** The notice's lifetime uses the wrong timestamp and is not aligned with the context line.
- **User impact:** Buddy promises a card that does not exist; the missing page is never resolved.
- **Concrete scenario:** Submitted day 0 → model retries park the job → retry on day 1 → ready with page 2 missing → no notice ever.
- **Root cause:** `noticeOf` uses `created_at`; `context.ts` has no expiry.
- **Recommended fix:** Gate on `ready_at` in both places, or auto-set `pages_resolved_at` when the window ends; optionally surface page problems on the material screen.
- **Blast radius:** Materials with page problems read after a delay.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced).

#### M-26 `p2-tilt-subsampling-lattice-false-tilted` — The tilt check flags straight pages as "schief" when a dark desk border is visible

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Capture → on-device quality check → PhotoCheckCard.
- **Location:** `apps/mobile/lib/photo/tilt.ts:33` (ink threshold also admits a dark desk), `:36,42` (every step-th point in raster order), `:79-81` (3° grid hits the lattice's rational slopes).
- **Current behavior:** A 10–15 % dark-desk border becomes a regular lattice of "ink" points whose projections peak at 18°/27°, so straight sheets are flagged (14 of 16 synthetic variants flagged; 0 of 96 without subsampling).
- **Expected behavior:** A straight printed page photographed from above is never reported as tilted.
- **Problem:** False positives that retaking cannot fix (same desk, same result).
- **User impact:** Each affected page stalls the multi-page flow until she finds "Trotzdem behalten".
- **Concrete scenario:** Straight A4 sheet on a dark desk with a 15 % border → "Foto 1 ist schief".
- **Root cause:** Deterministic raster-order subsampling of large solid regions.
- **Recommended fix:** Take ink only inside the paper region, subsample randomly or by blocks, require a margin over angle 0, and add dark-border fixtures.
- **Blast radius:** Captures with a dark desk in frame, especially when following the "Ganze Seite" tip.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced on synthetic images).

#### M-27 `p2-blur-check-measured-below-upload-resolution` — Blur and "small" checks run at a different scale than the 1600-px upload

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Capture → quality check → upload → extraction → answer key.
- **Location:** `apps/mobile/lib/capture/upload.ts:19` (`MAX_SIDE 1600`), `:51` (640-px analysis copy); `apps/mobile/lib/photo/quality.ts:43-50,132`.
- **Current behavior:** Sharpness is measured at 640 px, calibrated on fixtures with print about 1.5× larger than 10–11 pt; "small" checks the camera original, never the upload. Synthetic 10 pt text blurred to "1/8 ≈ 1/B" passes.
- **Expected behavior:** Photos ambiguous at the resolution the model reads are flagged; double pages count as two.
- **Problem:** False accepts at the scale that matters.
- **User impact:** A misread digit can become the answer key her correct answer is graded against, or the page is reported hours later.
- **Concrete scenario:** 12 MP A4, 10 pt, σ=7 px blur → sharpness 0.55 → accepted.
- **Root cause:** The calibration and measurement pipeline differ from what is uploaded.
- **Recommended fix:** Measure a native-scale crop of the 1600-px image, base "small" on the uploaded size per page, and recalibrate with real photos.
- **Blast radius:** All captures of normal-size print, worst for maths notation.
- **Confidence:** Needs runtime verification. Recalibrate against real phone photos of 10–11 pt worksheets.

### 6a.4 Practice, grading and homework help

#### M-28 `p2-sec-homework-leak-check-skipped-on-correct` — The homework leak check is skipped on "correct" and disabled when the child lists candidate answers

_Also covers:_ `p2-T3` (the learner's own message is passed as "task text", so listed guesses whitelist the solution). The "skipped on correct" half shares its mechanism with §5 H-9 `p2-HW-01`; this entry adds the candidate-listing bypass.

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Homework help (mode `help`) answer turn.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/practice/tutor.ts:156-160` (`verdict === 'correct'` → no check), `:168-189` (values in `task` are exempt); `apps/api/src/modules/practice/service.ts:635,649` (`task = ${item.prompt}\n${text}`), `:655-663` (downgrade runs after the check).
- **Current behavior:** (a) If the model says "correct", the leak check does not run, even when code then downgrades the verdict because `homeworkSolved` is false. (b) "Ist es 1/8, 3/8, 5/8 oder 7/8?" puts 7/8 into the exempt task text, so "Von deinen Vorschlägen stimmt 7/8" passes.
- **Expected behavior:** The final reply passes the leak check whenever code decides the task is unsolved; only values from `item.prompt` are exempt ("code enforces what the prompt asks for", `tutor.ts:151-152`).
- **Problem:** Two ordinary child behaviours (a half answer, guessing several values) bypass the code guard.
- **User impact:** Homework help, whose promise to parents is "never the solution", can hand over the answer.
- **Concrete scenario:** Task "Berechne ¾ + ⅛" → child lists four fractions → the model names 7/8 → shown unchanged (reproduced with copies of the functions).
- **Root cause:** The check trusts the model's verdict before code overrides it, and the learner's text is mixed into the exemption list.
- **Recommended fix:** Run `givesAwayHomework` after the `homeworkSolved` downgrade on the final verdict, pass only `item.prompt` as task, and add unit and eval cases for both bypasses.
- **Blast radius:** All homework help items with guessable answers.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced).

#### M-29 `p2-F-journey-cannot-check-steers-to-reveal` — The "cannot check" fallback tells learners to look at the solution in every mode and costs an FSRS "again" in practice

_Also covers:_ `p2-HW-04`, `p2-ux-fallback-copy-points-to-missing-solution`; low `p2-F4b`.

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Practice, explain, test and homework help while the tutor model is down, times out or its budget is used up.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/practice/service.ts:674-700` (fallback `verdict null` + `practice.cannot_check`); `apps/api/src/i18n/de.json:28`; `service.ts:928-961` (reveal → `skipped`, FSRS `revealed`).
- **Current behavior:** Free-text answers get "Du kannst dir die Lösung ansehen und selbst vergleichen". Following it in practice records the item as not known; in tests and homework no solution button exists.
- **Expected behavior:** An unchecked answer stays pending without penalty and is re-checked later or self-assessed; the copy offers only actions that exist in the current mode.
- **Problem:** A system outage turns into a learning record that says she did not know the answer.
- **User impact:** Possibly correct answers are scheduled as failed, and the pre-exam Probetest result is wrong.
- **Concrete scenario:** Vertex 503 → explain answer → "Nicht geprüft" → "Lösung zeigen" → `skipped`/`revealed`.
- **Root cause:** Copy written for practice mode only, and no re-evaluation or self-assessment path.
- **Recommended fix:** Mode-aware copy, a re-evaluation job keyed by `practice_turn` id, and an "Ich hatte es richtig" self-assessment without FSRS penalty.
- **Blast radius:** Every free-text item in all modes during any tutor outage.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced).

#### M-30 `p2-NM-07` — `choiceNamed` picks the first option even when a negation or self-correction follows

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Voice-mode multiple choice → spoken sentence → rule verdict without the tutor.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/practice/evaluate.ts:107-111` (option ≥ 3 chars, `startsWith`, rest begins with space + non-digit).
- **Current behavior:** "Richtig ist das nicht", "Richtig, nein falsch" and "true is not the answer" all resolve to option 0 (reproduced with the real function).
- **Expected behavior:** Only "option said first and then explained" is decided by rule; negations and corrections go to the tutor.
- **Problem:** A positional prefix match stands in for language understanding (rule 3).
- **User impact:** A spoken self-correction is graded as the opposite answer; in a test it is recorded as missed.
- **Concrete scenario:** Item "3/4 > 4/5?" [richtig, falsch] → "Richtig ist das nicht" → incorrect + hint.
- **Root cause:** Any trailing words are treated as explanation.
- **Recommended fix:** Accept the prefix only if the rest contains no other option and no negation, or drop the rule and let the tutor decide.
- **Blast radius:** Voice-mode MC with options of 3+ characters, especially true/false.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced).

#### M-31 `tipp-after-last-hint-reveals` — "Tipp" after the last prepared hint silently reveals the solution and closes the item as revealed

_Also covers:_ `tipp-after-last-hint-reveals-silently`, `p2-F-journey-outage-hint-request-cannot-check`.

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Hint button in practice/explain.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/practice/service.ts:357-362` (`hint_available` ignores `hints_left`), `hintItem` → `NoPreparedHint` → `answerItem('Tipp, bitte')` → worked reply, `revealed`.
- **Current behavior:** After the hint ladder is used up, the button still reads "Tipp". The next tap makes a tutor call whose reply is discarded, shows the worked solution and schedules the item as failed.
- **Expected behavior:** The last step is labelled for what it does ("Lösung zeigen"), or Tipp is disabled.
- **Problem:** A button labelled "Tipp" performs a reveal.
- **User impact:** Asking for one more nudge loses the question.
- **Concrete scenario:** 3 hints used → tap Tipp → full solution + `revealed`.
- **Root cause:** `hint_available` does not change meaning when hints are exhausted; the app ignores `hints_left`.
- **Recommended fix:** Set `hint_available=false` when `hints_used ≥ hints.length > 0`, or relabel via `hints_left`; skip the wasted tutor call.
- **Blast radius:** Practice and explain sessions with prepared hints.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced).

#### M-32 `p2-HW-05` — Deleting a sheet or question does not reach active sessions: its tasks stay on the home card and are still asked and graded

_Also covers:_ `p2-xs-deleted-questions-still-asked-in-active-sessions`, `p2-ml-deleted-material-leaves-prepared-step`.

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Mein Stoff → Löschen / "Frage löschen" while a practice or help session is active → home "Weiter…".
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/materials/service.ts:664-690` (`archiveMaterial`), `:790-811` (`archiveMaterialItem`); `apps/api/src/modules/practice/service.ts:302-309,469-477` (no archived filter); `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/state.ts:287-304`; `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/home.ts:137-152`.
- **Current behavior:** Archiving touches only `items.archived_at`/`materials.archived_at`. Running sessions still show, count and grade the deleted items, and a help session for a deleted homework sheet keeps its resume card.
- **Expected behavior:** Deleted items close in active sessions (as `flagItem` does) and a help session of a deleted sheet ends.
- **Problem:** The material lifecycle and the session lifecycle are not linked.
- **User impact:** "Frage gelöscht" is followed minutes later by the same question; a deleted homework stays "Weiter mit deiner Hausaufgabe – noch 6 Aufgaben".
- **Concrete scenario:** Wrong page photographed as homework → deleted → home card still opens its tasks (reproduced).
- **Root cause:** Sessions snapshot item ids in `session_items` and never re-check them.
- **Recommended fix:** In both archive functions, close open `session_items` of active sessions (`skipped`, `flagged_at`) and mark help sessions of the material `abandoned`; filter them from `nowCardOf`.
- **Blast radius:** Any active session whose items are deleted.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced).

#### M-33 `stale-active-sessions-never-abandoned` — "abandoned" is never set; in-progress steps and their sessions pile up and `startFromStep` keeps returning them

_Also covers:_ `abandoned-never-set-stale-in-progress-steps`.

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Start prepared practice from a step; Buddy's plan.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/practice/service.ts:163-167` (returns any active session for the step); status enum includes `abandoned` (`0001_baseline.sql:288`), no writer (grep).
- **Current behavior:** A step started once stays `in_progress`. The home hides it (and hides the session after 12 h) while Buddy's context lists it as active forever.
- **Expected behavior:** Idle sessions are closed and their step returned to `prepared` or cancelled so Buddy can re-prepare.
- **Problem:** A dead state in schema and copy, and a slow leak of invisible steps.
- **User impact:** The practice card vanishes after one tap plus 12 h and never comes back for that step.
- **Concrete scenario:** Start → 1 answer → app closed → 13 h later no resume card, no practice card.
- **Root cause:** The session lifecycle only goes `active` → `finished`, driven by the client.
- **Recommended fix:** A scheduler sweep that marks sessions idle for more than N h as `abandoned` and resets the step.
- **Blast radius:** All learners over time.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced).

#### M-34 `speak-commits-after-finish` — A pronunciation judgement in flight commits into an already finished session

_Also covers:_ `finish-race-with-pending-answer`, `speak-result-after-unmount-read-aloud`.

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Record → `POST /practice/sessions/:id/speak` while the learner taps "Beenden".
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/practice/speak.ts:127-132` (status checked before a 3–45 s model call), `:201-248` (transaction locks only `session_items`); `apps/api/src/modules/practice/service.ts:1032-1077` (`finishSession`).
- **Current behavior:** `finishSession` commits (answered 0, step back to prepared); the late speak transaction then writes turns, FSRS and "correct" into the finished session; the client reads the result aloud on the home.
- **Expected behavior:** An answer to a finished session is refused behind the same fence (rules 1/4), and nothing is read aloud for a screen she left.
- **Problem:** Session status is checked outside the write transaction; "Beenden" is not locked during upload.
- **User impact:** Her correct pronunciation is not reflected in the summary or the step; Buddy may offer the same step again and suddenly talks on the home.
- **Concrete scenario:** Tap Fertig → tap Beenden → finish commits → 4 s later speak commits "good".
- **Root cause:** Check-then-act across a long model call (same pattern as typed answers, with a shorter window).
- **Recommended fix:** `select status … for update` inside the transaction with 409 when not active; disable Beenden while judging; mounted guard in `SpeakPanel.send()`.
- **Blast radius:** Speak sessions; typed answers with a ~1–2 s window.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced).

#### M-35 `speak-offline-wait-no-cancel` — Offline, a pronunciation upload waits indefinitely behind "Buddy hört zu…" with every control locked

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Speak item → record → send while offline.
- **Location:** `apps/mobile/lib/api/endpoints.ts:211-215` (`sendWhenOnline`); `apps/mobile/lib/api/whenOnline.ts:24-33` (no timeout/abort); `apps/mobile/components/practice/SpeakPanel.tsx:283-297,327-328,391-401`.
- **Current behavior:** The panel claims Buddy is listening while nothing was sent; re-record, skip and listen are disabled, and only "Beenden" (which drops the recording) remains.
- **Expected behavior:** An offline waiting state with "Neu aufnehmen"/"Diesmal überspringen" still available (rule 5).
- **Problem:** An indefinite operation with no cancel and copy that is false for the state.
- **User impact:** On a train the child is stuck for minutes and must end the session.
- **Concrete scenario:** Record → network drops → "Buddy hört zu …" spinner indefinitely.
- **Root cause:** `sendWhenOnline` has no abort; "sending" is one opaque busy state.
- **Recommended fix:** Track `useOnline()` in the panel, show a waiting line and allow aborting the wait via a token.
- **Blast radius:** Speak sessions offline or on captive networks.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (code-traced).

#### M-36 `test-beenden-untouched-as-missed` — After "Beenden" in a test, untouched questions are reviewed as "Noch nicht richtig" without a solution

- **Severity:** Medium (raised from low in verification)
- **Affected user flow:** Test → early exit → summary review.
- **Location:** `apps/mobile/components/practice/SessionSummary.tsx:80-90`; `apps/api/src/modules/practice/service.ts:364-369` (answer null while an item is open, even after finish).
- **Current behavior:** Items she never saw are labelled missed and show no "Lösung:" line.
- **Expected behavior:** A "Nicht dran gewesen" state, with solutions shown once the test is over.
- **Problem:** A mislabelled outcome and a withheld solution for exactly the unanswered questions.
- **User impact:** The review says she got 7 of 10 wrong after answering 3.
- **Concrete scenario:** 10-item test → 3 answers → Beenden → items 4–10 "Noch nicht richtig".
- **Root cause:** Answers are withheld by item status, not session status; no "not attempted" state.
- **Recommended fix:** After finish, expose answers for open test items and add the label.
- **Blast radius:** Early exits in test mode.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced).

#### M-37 `p2-ux-narrative-probetest` — The test result card cannot reopen the review; a test ended early has no way back to its mistakes

_Also covers:_ `p2-ml-result-card-cannot-reopen-summary`, `p2-ux-early-end-geschafft-and-lost-test-review`; low `p2-ux-offer-card-reopens-old-session`. Related: §5 H-8 `beenden-finishes-for-good`.

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Home "Probetest" → offer → test → Beenden → home.
- **Location:** `apps/mobile/components/buddy/NowCard.tsx:147-170` (`practice_result` renders text only, although it carries the session); `apps/mobile/components/learn/OfferCard.tsx:20-23`; `apps/mobile/app/practice/[id].tsx:283-297`.
- **Current behavior:** The review exists only on the practice screen right after finishing. The home result card has no action, and a used offer card reopens the old session.
- **Expected behavior:** The result card offers "Ergebnis ansehen"; offer cards reflect their state.
- **Problem:** Test preparation, the core pre-exam use, has no path back to its results.
- **User impact:** The night before the exam Lena cannot revisit her mistakes or the solutions.
- **Concrete scenario:** Probetest → 4 of 8 → Beenden → home card "Geschafft" with no way to the review.
- **Root cause:** Result and offer cards are stateless text.
- **Recommended fix:** Add a review action to `practice_result`, show the review on Beenden in test mode, and make offer cards state-aware.
- **Blast radius:** All practice tests.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (code-traced).

#### M-38 `p2-topic-generation-refuses-adult-and-university-topics` — Topic generation is hard-coded to "school student" and refuses non-school topics

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Adult/university profile → "Erklär mir …" / "Üb mit mir …" → `POST /practice/topic`.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/practice/generate.ts:51,78,82`; `apps/api/src/modules/practice/hints.ts:37`.
- **Current behavior:** The system prompt says "school student" and "not about school learning → usable=false"; the learner line says "level university". A refusal returns 422 `not_usable` ("try a school topic").
- **Expected behavior:** Pitch by level; accept study and continuing-education topics for university/adult profiles.
- **Problem:** A school-only rule applied to supported adult profiles.
- **User impact:** Adult learners are told, in effect, that the app is not for them.
- **Concrete scenario:** Jonas, 24, university: "Fehler 1. und 2. Art beim Hypothesentest" → refused if the model follows the prompt.
- **Root cause:** The audience is hard-coded in prompts.
- **Recommended fix:** A level-dependent audience line, "not about learning" as the usable rule, adapted copy, and a university eval case.
- **Blast radius:** All university/adult self-profiles.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (code-traced). How often the model actually refuses was not measured.

#### M-39 `p2-grade-number-has-no-school-system` — Grade is an unlabeled 1–13 number; French/Spanish/Italian school years are converted by the model, unverified

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** `set_level` in chat → level/grade in every generation, extraction, hint and tutor prompt.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/decision.ts:198-211` ("School grade (Klasse)"); `apps/api/src/modules/practice/generate.ts:152-153`; locales `*/buddy.json:133` (card echoes the number).
- **Current behavior:** "je suis en 4e" can be stored as 4 and shown back as "4e année", while prompts read it as German Klasse 4 (age 9–10).
- **Expected behavior:** A system-independent measure, or a grade label plus country, rendered in prompts.
- **Problem:** The scale is German, the contract does not say so, and the localized card hides any mismatch.
- **User impact:** Content pitched years too low or too high for fr/es/it learners.
- **Concrete scenario:** Camille, 13: "4e" → grade 4 → "Explique-moi les équations" generated for grade 4.
- **Root cause:** Only German numbering was considered.
- **Recommended fix:** Define the number in the schema (years of schooling), give examples per locale, show the stored meaning back, and add fr/es/it evals.
- **Blast radius:** fr/es/it learners who state their grade.
- **Confidence:** Needs runtime verification. Run `set_level` evals for "4e", "2º ESO", "terza media".

#### M-40 `p2-non-vocab-questions-read-and-heard-in-app-language` — Non-vocab questions are read and recognised in the app language, not the material's

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Voice mode on material whose language differs from the profile language.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/materials/extract.ts:95` (questions in the material's language); `apps/api/src/modules/practice/items.ts:158-159` (`lang = null` for all but vocab/speak); `apps/mobile/app/practice/[id].tsx:102-122`.
- **Current behavior:** A German biology sheet for an English-profile learner is read by an en-GB voice, and his German spoken answer is recognised with the en-GB model.
- **Expected behavior:** Items carry their language; TTS/STT use it or fall back to server transcription.
- **Problem:** Language metadata exists only for vocab and speak items.
- **User impact:** Garbled questions and nonsense transcripts graded as wrong; hands-free is unusable for these sheets.
- **Concrete scenario:** Tom (en), German school: "Welche Aufgabe hat der Zellkern?" read in English; "Er steuert die Zelle" misrecognised.
- **Root cause:** `items.ts` drops the language for ordinary kinds.
- **Recommended fix:** Return `prompt_lang` for every item and use it in `questionParts` and `AnswerComposer`.
- **Blast radius:** Learners whose profile language differs from their school's language.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (code-traced).

### 6a.5 Math rendering

#### M-41 `p2-blank-inside-math-becomes-subscript` — A fill-in blank inside `$…$` is parsed as subscripts

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Fill-in math question → QuestionCard/MathText → screen reader and voice.
- **Location:** `apps/mobile/lib/math/parse.ts:210-215` (every `_` starts a subscript); `apps/mobile/lib/math/prompt.ts:3-4,34-41` (blanks inside math are masked).
- **Current behavior:** "Erweitere: $\frac{3}{4} = \frac{___}{8}$" draws a tiny lowered underscore, the gap never fills, and the screen reader reads "Index \_ Index".
- **Expected behavior:** `___`/`\square` inside math is a Gap atom, counted, filled and read as "Lücke".
- **Problem:** The math parser gives `_` its LaTeX meaning inside blanks.
- **User impact:** The most common fraction exercise type renders broken. Display only; grading is unaffected.
- **Concrete scenario:** Generated item above with answer "6" → the gap never shows "6".
- **Root cause:** `prompt.ts` masks math before finding blanks; `parse.ts` treats underscores as operators.
- **Recommended fix:** Parse runs of 3+ `_` (and `\square`, `\Box`) as a blank atom, and count and fill them.
- **Blast radius:** Fill-in math items with the blank inside the formula.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced).

#### M-42 `p2-dollar-currency-parsed-as-math` — Two currency `$` signs are paired into a math span that glues the words between them

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Any model- or learner-written text through MathText/speakMathText.
- **Location:** `apps/mobile/lib/math/parse.ts:93-118` (pairs any two unescaped `$`), `:201-204` (whitespace dropped); `apps/mobile/components/buddy/Conversation.tsx:90-94`.
- **Current behavior:** "The pen costs $5 and the book costs $3." renders and is spoken as "5andthebookcosts".
- **Expected behavior:** Currency dollars never open math (escaped by prompt rule and server repair).
- **Problem:** The math delimiter clashes with a common currency sign, and nothing escapes it.
- **User impact:** English word problems with dollar amounts become unreadable; a child's own message is mangled.
- **Concrete scenario:** Generated "A pen costs $5 and a book costs $3 …" → glued card text.
- **Root cause:** The `$…$` convention is used without escaping rules.
- **Recommended fix:** Add a `\$` rule to `MATH_RULES` and the chat prompts, repair server-side, and escape `$` in learner-authored messages.
- **Blast radius:** Texts with two or more currency dollars; a single `$` is unaffected.
- **Confidence:** Partly confirmed. Reproduced for 2+ dollars; single amounts render correctly.

#### M-43 `p2-dollarmath-wraps-whole-prompt` — When the model omits `$`, the server wraps the whole question sentence as math

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Item storage (`usableItems`) → QuestionCard → voice reading.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/practice/items.ts:119-123` (`dollarMath` wraps the whole text), `:131` (applied to prompt).
- **Current behavior:** "Kürze den Bruch \frac{6}{8} so weit wie möglich." is stored wrapped and shown as "KürzedenBruch ¾ soweitwiemöglich." in italics.
- **Expected behavior:** Only the LaTeX runs are wrapped; whole-text wrap is limited to pure-math fields (answers, choices).
- **Problem:** A repair meant for bare answers is applied to sentences.
- **User impact:** Unreadable questions exactly when the model slips. Grading is unaffected.
- **Concrete scenario:** Extraction returns a prompt without dollars → a glued sentence is stored.
- **Root cause:** `items.ts:119-122` wraps the entire text when a command is present and no `$`.
- **Recommended fix:** Wrap maximal LaTeX runs only, or apply `dollarMath` to answer/accepted/choices only.
- **Blast radius:** Items whose prompt has bare LaTeX.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced).

#### M-44 `p2-unknown-latex-glued-names` — LaTeX commands outside the supported subset render and are spoken as glued names ("0,overline3", "gparallelh")

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Question, choice, reply or table with math → MathText/speakMathText.
- **Location:** `apps/mobile/lib/math/parse.ts:336-341` (unknown command → its name as text), `:23-61` (no `\overline`, `\angle`, `\parallel`, `\perp`, `\in`, `\mathbb`, `\vec`, `\square`).
- **Current behavior:** "$0{,}\overline{3}$" shows "0,overline3"; "$g \parallel h$" shows "gparallelh".
- **Expected behavior:** Common school notation is drawn and spoken, or falls back to a readable symbol.
- **Problem:** The model is only told the subset; nothing on the server validates or maps other commands.
- **User impact:** Grade 6/7 topics (repeating decimals, angles, parallels) become unreadable; "0,overline3" can be misread as 0,3.
- **Concrete scenario:** Key "$0{,}\overline{3}$" → the solution card shows "0,overline3".
- **Root cause:** An unknown-command fallback plus a symbol table without geometry, set or overline support.
- **Recommended fix:** Add these commands with spoken words, extend `MATH_RULES`, and keep a space after unknown commands.
- **Blast radius:** Every MathText surface when the model uses these commands.
- **Confidence:** Partly confirmed. Rendering is reproduced; it only occurs when the model ignores the subset rule.

### 6a.6 Buddy turns, decisions and memory

#### M-45 `apply-vs-tap-lock-order-deadlock` — Decision apply and card taps lock rows in opposite order; a tap during a turn deadlocks and kills the turn after its reply was streamed

_Also covers:_ `repro-01` (runtime proof). The descriptive id was rated low in phase 1 and is raised to medium because the reproduction shows the streamed reply being withdrawn. The failure handling is M-46.

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Learner turn applying a decision about a step/goal while the learner taps skip/outcome on the now card.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/apply.ts:69-72` (`buddy_settings FOR UPDATE` first, then steps/goals); `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/routes.ts:121-137,185-201` (step/goal first, then `bumpContext` → settings).
- **Current behavior:** Postgres detects the deadlock after 1 s. In the reproduction the turn was the victim: the SSE stream sent the full reply, then `error {code:'internal'}`, and the message stayed `processing` for about 3 min until the scheduler re-ran the model.
- **Expected behavior:** All fence-bumping transactions take the settings lock first, so they serialise instead of deadlocking.
- **Problem:** No documented lock order, and no `40P01` handling or retry.
- **User impact:** Buddy's reply appears, then is replaced by a generic error; the message sits as "schreibt…" for minutes, and a second billed model call follows.
- **Concrete scenario:** Card "Übung bereit" visible → learner sends a message → taps "Heute nicht" during apply → deadlock (`pg_stat_database.deadlocks` 0 → 1).
- **Root cause:** Apply is settings-first; taps are entity-first.
- **Recommended fix:** Add `select 1 from buddy_settings where learner_id=$1 for update` as the first statement in every tap route (as undo already does); disable now-card taps while a send is pending.
- **Blast radius:** Any tap on a card during a turn that touches the same step/goal.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced).

#### M-46 `non-model-error-leaves-message-processing` — Any non-model exception in a turn leaves the message "processing" with no failure code

_Also covers:_ `non-model-exception-leaves-message-processing`. Related: M-45 is the concrete trigger. The lows `p2-J-stall-recovery-loop-drains-budget` and `p2-poisoned-turn-strands-learner-jobs-and-loops` (repeat recovery with no attempt cap) are listed in 6b.

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Learner message turn (SSE and JSON) → `processTurn` → `applyDecision`.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/turn.ts:151` (`loadBuddyState`), `:164` (`pushAvailable`), `:274` (`applyDecision`), all outside the try at `:199-243`; `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/apply.ts:237` (rethrows).
- **Current behavior:** A DB error during state loading or apply propagates as SSE `error {code:'internal'}`; no `failed` status or error code is written, and there is no resend button until stall recovery (~3 min, or never if the scheduler is down).
- **Expected behavior:** "Failure → the message is marked failed with a stable code; nothing half-applied" (`turn.ts:9-10`, `docs/architecture.md` §Turns).
- **Problem:** The failure handler covers model and budget errors only.
- **User impact:** Toast "Da ist was schiefgegangen", then "Buddy schreibt…" with no outcome for minutes.
- **Concrete scenario:** The deadlock in M-45 → the turn is aborted → the message stays `processing`.
- **Root cause:** `applyDecision`/`loadBuddyState` sit outside the handler.
- **Recommended fix:** Wrap the whole attempt body so any exception records `failed`/`internal` via `failTurn` (keeping `SupersededClaim` semantics), and add a robustness test that injects a DB failure.
- **Blast radius:** All turns.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced).

#### M-47 `check-lease-shorter-than-runtime` — A Buddy check that outlives its 150 s lease is run twice, and both runs apply a decision

_Also covers:_ `repro-17` (runtime proof: double apply), `check-lease-shorter-than-worst-case-run`, `per-learner-check-lease-shorter-than-worst-case-run`. The descriptive id was rated low in phase 1 and is raised to medium.

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** `buddy_check` job whose model rounds exceed the job/learner lease.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/check.ts:44` (`LEASE_SECONDS=150`), `:75-84`, `:380-429` (up to 3 attempts × lookup rounds × 40 s); `apps/api/src/modules/scheduler/jobs.ts:65-78` (expired lease → requeued).
- **Current behavior:** A check can run about 360 s. After 150 s the next tick requeues the job and a second worker runs the same check. In the reproduction both applied decisions: two scheduled messages from one trigger and 3 billed calls.
- **Expected behavior:** One worker per learner (`check.ts:8`); a lost lease stops the loser.
- **Problem:** Lease expiry is treated as a crash while the original worker is still alive.
- **User impact:** Duplicate contacts and actions, doubled model cost, and d-1 exam checks can be parked `failed` with no fallback.
- **Concrete scenario:** Slow provider → worker 1 inside the model at +151 s → tick B recovers the job → both apply.
- **Root cause:** A fixed lease shorter than the worst-case run, with no ownership re-check before apply.
- **Recommended fix:** Heartbeat or extend the lease per attempt (or bound `decide()` by the lease deadline), re-check the lease token before apply, and run the deterministic fallback when a check is parked.
- **Blast radius:** Every learner's checks on a slow provider day.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced).

#### M-48 `timezone-header-no-context-bump` — Time-zone changes bypass the context fence, so a planned day can be off by one

_Also covers:_ `repro-21` (runtime proof: off-by-one due date), `timezone-header-updates-bypass-fence`; low `device-timezone-overwrites-learner-zone` is related. The descriptive id is raised to medium.

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Any request with a different `x-timezone` arriving while a turn is thinking (travel, second device).
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/http/context.ts:75-83` (upserts `timezone`, no `bumpContext`); `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/apply.ts:69-72` (settings reloaded inside the transaction → tools resolve in the new zone).
- **Current behavior:** STATE showed "Monday … (Europe/Berlin), +1 Tue 2026-09-29"; the zone changed to America/New_York mid-call; the decision was applied as Mon 2026-09-28 without being marked stale.
- **Expected behavior:** The zone is part of the fenced context (rule 4); a change makes the decision stale.
- **Problem:** A dependency of date resolution is written outside the fence.
- **User impact:** Exams or reminders are planned a day early or late with no error.
- **Concrete scenario:** Reproduced: `due_date` 2026-09-28 instead of 2026-09-29 after a zone switch during the model call.
- **Root cause:** The timezone write in `requireLearner` does not bump `context_version`.
- **Recommended fix:** Update the zone only when it actually changes and bump context in the same transaction, or pass the rendered zone into apply.
- **Blast radius:** Multi-device accounts and travellers; near midnight/DST.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced).

#### M-49 `quote-checked-against-trigger-only` — Split utterances: the quote rule accepts only the last message, so a fact stated across two quick messages is rejected

_Also covers:_ `repro-22` (runtime proof), `quote-checked-only-against-trigger-message`.

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** "Mathearbeit am Freitag" + "über Brüche" sent as two bubbles → one turn answers both.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/tools.ts:123-131` (`requireQuote` uses `ctx.latestLearnerText`); `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/turn.ts:153-162` (earlier message superseded), `:281` (`latestLearnerText = message.text`).
- **Current behavior:** Reproduced: message 1 is marked `done` with no reply; message 2 fails `model_invalid` ("Buddy hat sich verhaspelt"); no exam is planned.
- **Expected behavior:** Quotes are accepted from any learner message the turn covers (the same run the supersede logic already treats as one utterance).
- **Problem:** `latestLearnerText` is a single string.
- **User impact:** A normal phone typing pattern loses the plan and leaves a message "answered" with no answer.
- **Concrete scenario:** As above; recoverable only by resending.
- **Root cause:** Quote provenance is scoped to the trigger message only.
- **Recommended fix:** Pass the concatenated unanswered run as `latestLearnerText`, and align the prompt wording.
- **Blast radius:** Learners who split messages.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced).

#### M-50 `trailing-model-turn-after-outreach` — After an in-app outreach during a turn, the retry's dialogue ends with a model turn

_Also covers:_ low `dialogue-may-end-with-model-turn`.

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Learner turn (stale retry) while an agreed reminder or outreach is posted to the thread.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/context.ts:307-329` (`buildContents` maps every recent message, never cuts at the trigger); `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/delivery.ts:157-172` (`postToThread` bumps context).
- **Current behavior:** The retry's contents end with role `model`. If Gemini rejects this (400), it is classified `unavailable` → `model_unavailable`, and a resend of the same message rebuilds the same dialogue.
- **Expected behavior:** The request always ends with the learner's message.
- **Problem:** `buildContents` assumes the trigger is the newest message; the outreach bump is exactly what forces the retry.
- **User impact:** "Buddy konnte gerade nicht antworten" right when the reminder fires, and resending does not help.
- **Concrete scenario:** 17:00:02 "Hi, bin da" → 17:00:05 reminder posted → stale → retry ends with `model`.
- **Root cause:** No guarantee that the trigger is last.
- **Recommended fix:** Cut the dialogue at the trigger, or move later Buddy messages before it as a user-role note; add a robustness test.
- **Blast radius:** Turns coinciding with in-app reminders or outreach.
- **Confidence:** Needs runtime verification. The dialogue shape is reproduced on the harness; confirm Vertex's response to a trailing model turn.

#### M-51 `p2-turn-day-offsets-anchored-to-now-resolved-at-message-time` — A message resent on a later day resolves `in_days` from the original write date while the model counted from today

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** "Nochmal senden" of a failed message from an earlier day, or stall recovery across midnight → date-bearing tools.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/context.ts:89-96` ("Now" and "Next days" from the attempt's `now`); `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/turn.ts:280` (`reference = message.created_at`); `apps/api/src/lib/time.ts` `resolveDay`.
- **Current behavior:** The model copies `in_days` from a table anchored at today; code adds it to the date the message was written.
- **Expected behavior:** The model and the resolver use the same anchor (rule 2).
- **Problem:** Server-side resolution is kept mechanically, but its two halves use different reference dates, and nothing detects the mismatch.
- **User impact:** An exam is planned days early; countdown and "Wie lief es?" fire at the wrong time.
- **Concrete scenario:** Monday "Mathearbeit am Freitag" fails on budget → resent Wednesday → the model picks +2 (Friday) → resolved from Monday = Wednesday.
- **Root cause:** `buildContext` anchors to the attempt time while `ApplyInput.reference` is the message time.
- **Recommended fix:** Build "Now/Next days" from the message time and state it in STATE, or resolve relative to the attempt's `now`.
- **Blast radius:** Resends and recoveries on a later local day.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (code-traced).

#### M-52 `p2-safety-verdict-after-speech` — A streamed reply blocked by the provider at its last chunk has already been shown and spoken

_Also covers:_ `speakable-reply-spoken-before-validation`, `speakable-reply-before-validation`. Same streaming design as M-53.

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Chat and talk mode: `POST /buddy/messages` streamed over SSE, read sentence by sentence.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/llm/vertex.ts:154-168` (`onPartial` for every chunk) vs `:183-188` (`finishReason` checked after the loop); `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/turn.ts:222-231`.
- **Current behavior:** Partials of a speakable reply are displayed and spoken before Vertex's finish reason; a final SAFETY block cuts the voice mid-sentence, and the block category and the shown text are not recorded.
- **Expected behavior:** Text the safety classifier has not passed is not spoken to a child, or at least the block is recorded with its category.
- **Problem:** Early display is gated only on "does the answer change data", not on the provider's final verdict.
- **User impact:** A child can hear the start of a reply that the filter then stops; parents and operators cannot learn what was said.
- **Concrete scenario:** Talk mode, borderline question → chunk 2 spoken → chunk 3 SAFETY → silence.
- **Root cause:** Streaming was added without holding partials until the finish reason.
- **Recommended fix:** For minors, buffer at least the speech path until STOP; record the finish reason and safety category in `llm_calls`/`buddy_decisions`.
- **Blast radius:** Every streamed reply without actions.
- **Confidence:** Partly confirmed. The mechanism (partials before the finish reason) is code-traced, and a runtime probe reproduced streamed text that was later replaced. The refuter disputes the child-safety escalation; test how Gemini's streamed safety verdict actually arrives with a live borderline prompt.

#### M-53 `repro-28` — Streamed replies are shown and spoken before zod validation; an over-long reply is read in full, twice, then withdrawn

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Speakable streamed turn whose final JSON fails `TurnDecision` validation (reply > 700 chars).
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/stream.ts:18-29` (`speakable: changesNothing(raw)`, judged only from lookups/actions); `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/turn.ts:256-265` (validation after the call); `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/registry.ts:201-219` (reply max 700).
- **Current behavior:** Reproduced: an 800-char reply streamed as speakable (256 → 548 → 800), was repeated in the repair round, then failed `model_invalid`, or was replaced by a different shorter text.
- **Expected behavior:** Only text that can pass validation is shown or spoken, or the client stops as soon as the limit is exceeded.
- **Problem:** Validation happens after display and speech.
- **User impact:** She hears one answer, then sees "Buddy hat sich verhaspelt" or a different text in the thread.
- **Concrete scenario:** As reproduced (3 tests, real harness).
- **Root cause:** `replyProgress` has no length check against the schema limit.
- **Recommended fix:** Make speakable require `reply.length ≤ 700` (export the constant) and emit `speakable:false` when it is exceeded; the client drops earlier-round text on a new round.
- **Blast radius:** Long streamed replies.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced).

#### M-54 `p2-J-memory-F1` — Correcting a temporary situation cannot move its end date

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Chat correction ("Ich bin doch bis Montag krank") or memory-screen edit of a temporary entry.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/decision.ts:188-191` (`correct_memory`: memory, statement, quote only); `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/tools.ts:280-300` (copies old `valid_until`); `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/routes.ts:309-317`.
- **Current behavior:** The statement changes, the end stays; the card says "Korrigiert: …" with no date.
- **Expected behavior:** A correction can carry a new UntilSpec (≤ 60 days) resolved server-side.
- **Problem:** Neither the tool nor the edit contract has an end parameter, and the prompt steers to the tool that keeps the old end.
- **User impact:** Buddy resumes checks and outreach while she is still ill or away, and the UI shows a sentence and date that contradict each other.
- **Concrete scenario:** Tue "krank diese Woche" (until Fri) → Thu "bis Montag" → Sat the d-3 check proposes outreach.
- **Root cause:** `correct_memory` was designed for statement corrections only.
- **Recommended fix:** Add an optional `until` to `correct_memory` (required for constraints), update the prompt, and show on screen that the end is fixed or can be changed via Buddy.
- **Blast radius:** Every temporary situation.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (code-traced).

#### M-55 `p2-background-actions-invisible-no-undo` — Actions Buddy takes in background checks never appear in the UI and cannot be undone

_Also covers:_ `p2-xs-background-check-actions-invisible-and-unundoable`; low `p2-prepare-practice-cancels-reminder-prepared-step` is related.

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** `buddy_check` → `prepare_practice` / `request_material` / `schedule_check` → home.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/check.ts:516-530` (applied with `reply: null`); `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/delivery.ts:157-167` (in-app outreach without `decision_id`); `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/home.ts:375-390,437-448` (cards attached by `decision_id` only); `home.done` is never rendered.
- **Current behavior:** Check actions get no "✓" card and no undo; a check's `prepare_practice` silently replaces a step the learner asked for.
- **Expected behavior:** Every action Buddy takes is visible with status and undo (`docs/architecture.md` §Home).
- **Problem:** The traceability and undo promise covers only turn decisions.
- **User impact:** The now card changes overnight with no explanation, the practice she requested disappears, and scheduled check-ins cannot be cancelled.
- **Concrete scenario:** Mon: "Übungen zum Kürzen" prepared → Tue d-3 check re-prepares a generic set → the old card and its undo are gone.
- **Root cause:** Checks have no reply, and outreach messages are not linked to their decision.
- **Recommended fix:** Set `decision_id` on posted outreach (or add a short system line), or render `home.done`; do not let a check cancel a learner-requested step.
- **Blast radius:** All background `prepare_practice`/`request_material`/`schedule_check` actions.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (code-traced).

#### M-56 `thread-undo-without-undoapplies` — "Rückgängig" in the thread is offered without `undoApplies`, so stale buttons end in 409

_Also covers:_ `repro-06` (runtime proof), `thread-undo-offered-without-undoApplies`.

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Home thread action cards → `POST /buddy/actions/:id/undo`.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/home.ts:442-446` (thread `undoable` without `undoApplies`) vs `:279-284` (`doneOf`, which the app never renders).
- **Current behavior:** After the requested photo arrives, the card still shows "Rückgängig" for 7 days; every tap returns 409 `changed_since`, and the button survives the refresh (reproduced).
- **Expected behavior:** Undo is offered only when it would work (`tools.ts:859-863`).
- **Problem:** The gate was built for a list the UI does not show.
- **User impact:** A 12-year-old taps a button that cannot work and is told so afterwards.
- **Concrete scenario:** `request_material` → photo sent → capture step done → tap Rückgängig → 409 toast.
- **Root cause:** `threadOf` skips `undoApplies`.
- **Recommended fix:** Compute `undoable` once per action with `undoApplies` and reuse it for thread and done.
- **Blast radius:** Every action card in the conversation.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced).

#### M-57 `p2-ux-heute-nicht-skips-for-good` — "Heute nicht" permanently skips a prepared step and cancels its reminder, with no feedback or undo

_Also covers:_ low `p2-ml-home-taps-irreversible`.

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Home "Übung bereit" → "Heute nicht".
- **Location:** `apps/mobile/components/buddy/NowCard.tsx:82-88`; `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/routes.ts:116-140` (`state='skipped'`, queued checks cancelled).
- **Current behavior:** A label meaning "not today" performs a terminal skip; the card vanishes with no toast, thread line or undo.
- **Expected behavior:** Postpone to tomorrow, or say plainly "Übersprungen" and offer undo (UX principles: undo over confirmation).
- **Problem:** The label does not match the handler, and taps bypass the undoable action log.
- **User impact:** An agreed practice and its reminder never come back.
- **Concrete scenario:** "Vokabeln Mittwoch 17:00" prepared → "Heute nicht" → nothing on Thursday.
- **Root cause:** Skip was modelled as a terminal state change.
- **Recommended fix:** Implement it as a move to the next day, or rename it with a toast, and record it as a `buddy_action` with a restore undo.
- **Blast radius:** Every prepared practice card.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (code-traced).

### 6a.7 Proactivity, contact and delivery

`agreed-reminder-vanishes` (with `repro-15`) is HIGH and lives in §5 (H-37). `repro-02` is §5 H-29.

#### M-58 `agreed-reminder-silently-not-scheduled` — An agreed reminder "today" without a time is never scheduled once the preferred start has passed

_Also covers:_ `agreed-reminder-today-silently-unscheduled`.

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** `plan_step`/`update_step` with `agreed=true` and no time.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/tools.ts:555-560` (`if (when > now) scheduleStepReminder`), `:635-639` (`update_step` same).
- **Current behavior:** At 17:00 with a preferred start of 15:00, `when` is in the past, so no job is created, while the card says "Verabredet: Englisch heute" and Buddy confirms.
- **Expected behavior:** Reject with "ask for a time", or schedule at the next allowed slot (rule 5).
- **Problem:** A silent no-op on a promise the learner explicitly agreed to.
- **User impact:** The reminder the child asked for never comes, with no error.
- **Concrete scenario:** "Erinner mich heute noch ans Englisch" at 17:00 → nothing fires.
- **Root cause:** The scheduling guard swallows the past case.
- **Recommended fix:** Throw a `ToolRejection` asking for a time, or pick the next non-quiet slot via `policy.findSlot` and state it in the summary.
- **Blast radius:** Any agreed step for today after the window start.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced).

#### M-59 `p2-send-time-recheck-ignores-tightened-rules` — The send-time re-check covers only opt-in, pause and quiet hours

_Also covers:_ `p2-settings-tightening-not-rechecked-at-send`; low `p2-NEW-post-outage-delivery-ignores-contact-rules` is the outage variant.

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** `planOutreach` → settings tightened or an earlier push → `sendDueOutreach`.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/delivery.ts:270-310` (re-checks `contact_enabled`, `paused_until`, quiet hours only); `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/policy.ts:137-146` (future scheduled rows invisible to the unanswered gate).
- **Current behavior:** Newly avoided weekdays, a shifted window, lowered caps and the 48 h unanswered rule do not stop an already-scheduled Buddy message.
- **Expected behavior:** The full policy holds at send time; reducing contact takes effect for queued messages (rule 6, `docs/architecture.md` §Delivery).
- **Problem:** The policy runs in full only at planning time.
- **User impact:** "Am Wochenende bitte keine Nachrichten" still brings a Saturday push; two unanswered pushes arrive on consecutive days.
- **Concrete scenario:** Fri 19:00 initiative scheduled for Sat 15:00 → 19:30 `set_contact avoid [6,7]` → Saturday push sent.
- **Root cause:** `sendDueOutreach` re-implements a partial policy.
- **Recommended fix:** Re-run `decideContact` for buddy-origin rows at send time (excluding the row itself), or re-plan scheduled rows whenever settings change.
- **Blast radius:** All buddy-origin outreach.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (code-traced).

#### M-60 `p2-NEW-countdown-relative-day-baked-at-plan-time` — Countdown texts freeze "Morgen" at planning time but can be delivered on the exam day

_Also covers:_ `p2-countdown-text-day-label-frozen` (runtime-reproduced on the fallback path).

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Exam countdown d-1 check → `planOutreach` → tick delivery.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/check.ts:636-651` (`dayLabel` baked into the body), `:716-733` (`expiresAt = now + 24 h`); `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/plan.ts:71-84`.
- **Current behavior:** When d-1 is an avoided weekday, capped or hit by an outage, `findSlot` moves the message to the exam day and it still says "Morgen ist «Mathearbeit»".
- **Expected behavior:** The relative day is true when delivered: expire at the end of the planning day, or render the label at send time.
- **Problem:** The wording is computed at planning time; the send time is decided later.
- **User impact:** After her morning exam, Lena's lock screen says the exam is tomorrow.
- **Concrete scenario:** `avoid_weekdays=[6,7]`, exam Monday → Sunday check → push Monday 15:00 "Morgen ist …".
- **Root cause:** Relative words stored in `outreach.body`; expiry not tied to the referenced date.
- **Recommended fix:** Cap `expiresAt` at the start of the exam day in the learner's zone, or store a template key and render at send.
- **Blast radius:** d-1 messages whenever d-1 is avoided or capped.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced) for the fallback path; the model path is likely.

#### M-61 `p2-reading-result-silent-when-contact-off` — Buddy's reaction to the learner's own action goes through the unsolicited-contact policy: silent with contact off, deferred or suppressed with contact on

_Also covers:_ `p2-xs-contact-off-drops-buddy-messages-despite-in-app-copy`, `p2-xs-learner-triggered-results-go-through-initiative-policy`.

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Photos read / practice finished → `material_ready` or `session_finished` check → result message.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/check.ts:48,312-315` (`LEARNER_TRIGGERED`: "do it now"); `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/apply.ts:182-211` and `check.ts:717-734` (outreach planned without `inAppWhenOff`, origin `buddy`); `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/registry.ts:275-280` (`CheckDecision` has no reply).
- **Current behavior:** With contact off (the default), the result outreach is stored `suppressed/contact_disabled` and the home shows nothing. With contact on, it is deferred to the preferred window or suppressed by caps or the unanswered gate while the "working" note disappears.
- **Expected behavior:** A reaction the learner is waiting for appears in the app immediately, as agreed reminders do via `inAppWhenOff`.
- **Problem:** The policy for unsolicited pushes also gates the in-app acknowledgement of her own upload or practice.
- **User impact:** The spinner vanishes and nothing says whether the sheet was read; the sheet is only discoverable via Mein Stoff.
- **Concrete scenario:** Fresh account, contact off → vocab list photographed → 30 s later now/working/notice null, thread unchanged (reproduced).
- **Root cause:** No origin for learner-triggered feedback; checks have no reply channel.
- **Recommended fix:** Pass `inAppWhenOff: true` and bypass the window, caps and unanswered gate for learner-triggered checks while she is in the app, or add a "material_ready" now card.
- **Blast radius:** Every learner before opt-in (all minors until the PIN step) and every reading/practice result.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced).

#### M-62 `p2-stop-contact-immediately-reasks-optin-on-home` — Stopping contact in settings makes the home ask for opt-in again immediately

_Also covers:_ `p2-optin-card-reappears-right-after-contact-stopped`.

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Settings → "Nicht mehr erlauben" → home.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/home.ts:253-259` (only `opt_in_prompt_hidden_until` suppresses the card); `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/routes.ts:378-383` (stop sets `contact_changed_by/at`, never the hide window).
- **Current behavior:** The next home load shows the opt-in card ("Eltern fragen" for a minor) while any active goal exists.
- **Expected behavior:** An explicit stop counts at least as "not now" (rule 6: Buddy only reduces contact, never pressures).
- **Problem:** Two independent flags with no link between stop and prompt suppression.
- **User impact:** The child's choice seems ignored, and she is steered straight back to the PIN pad.
- **Concrete scenario:** Stop → toast "Ausgeschaltet." → back → opt-in card.
- **Root cause:** `decisionOf` ignores `contact_changed_at`.
- **Recommended fix:** Set `opt_in_prompt_hidden_until = now + 14 d` when contact flips to false.
- **Blast radius:** Every learner with an active goal who stops contact.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced).

#### M-63 `send-uncertain-lease-no-thread` — Lease-expired "sending" rows become `send_uncertain` without the thread copy

_Also covers:_ `send-crash-leaves-no-thread-copy`, `delivery-loop-crash-loses-messages`.

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** `sendDueOutreach` crash or timeout recovery.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/delivery.ts:232-236` (status flip only) vs `:318,335,367,392` (in-loop paths call `postToThread`).
- **Current behavior:** The push may have reached the phone, but the text exists nowhere in the app, and the "unklar, ob es ankam" label is never rendered.
- **Expected behavior:** "The text is always also in the thread" (`docs/architecture.md:195`, `delivery.ts:4-5`).
- **Problem:** The recovery transition skips the mirror invariant.
- **User impact:** Tapping a notification opens an app with no such message, or the message is silently lost.
- **Concrete scenario:** Tick A claims the row → provider accepts → function times out before `:373` → tick B flips it to `send_uncertain`.
- **Root cause:** The sweep was written as a status guard, not a full transition.
- **Recommended fix:** Post to the thread for each swept row in the same transaction, or mirror before calling the provider.
- **Blast radius:** Any crash or timeout in the provider window, and batches longer than the 2-min lease.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (code-traced).

#### M-64 `notification-tap-cold-start` — A notification tap that cold-starts the app never records "opened"

_Also covers:_ `repro-19` (native replay path traced; device run pending), `notification-tap-unconditional-and-cold-start`, `notification-tap-unconditional-buddy-route`; low `opened-proof-not-durable` (the persistence side of the same gap).

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Push tap from a killed app → "opened" proof → Buddy home.
- **Location:** `apps/mobile/app/_layout.tsx:37-40` (`loadSession` not awaited), `:55-60` (tap listener fires `outreachOpened` at once, `.catch` only invalidates); `apps/mobile/lib/push.ts:74-85` (only `addNotificationResponseReceivedListener`, no `getLastNotificationResponse`); `apps/mobile/lib/api/client.ts:45-54` (no token before hydration).
- **Current behavior:** The launch response is either replayed before the listener exists or handled before the session loads (401, no retry); the report is sent once, with no outbox.
- **Expected behavior:** "Opened" is recorded for every tap (rule 5), after hydration, and retried like answers.
- **Problem:** Two async initialisations race the earliest external event.
- **User impact:** Opened messages stay "unanswered", and Buddy's next initiative is suppressed for 48 h (`previous_unanswered`).
- **Concrete scenario:** App killed → push tapped → `outreachOpened` without bearer → 401 → lost.
- **Root cause:** No ready-gating, no `getLastNotificationResponse`, no persistence.
- **Recommended fix:** Queue the outreach id until `ready`, read `getLastNotificationResponse()` after hydration, and put opened reports in the outbox.
- **Blast radius:** Every cold start from a notification (a large share of real taps).
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (code-traced) that the report is lost. The exact native replay ordering needs a device run with `PUSH_BACKEND=expo` (steps in `repro-19`).

#### M-65 `p2-push-device-binding-first-device-forever` — Push tokens are bound per learner, not per device: pushes go to a stale or foreign device, including after an offline sign-out

_Also covers:_ `repro-20` (runtime proof: after an offline sign-out the next person on the phone receives the previous learner's pushes), `shared-phone-token-stays-active`, `shared-phone-token-lingers`; low `only-latest-device-receives-pushes`.

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Contact opt-in → device registration → `sendDueOutreach` token choice; sign-out on a shared phone.
- **Location:** `apps/mobile/components/settings/ContactSection.tsx:186` (register card only when no token is active); `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/home.ts:461-471` (push "active" if any token is active); `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/delivery.ts:323-330` (newest active token by learner); `apps/mobile/lib/push.ts:60-71` (unregister swallows failures).
- **Current behavior:** Reproduced: after A's offline sign-out, A's token stays active and A's exam and agreed-reminder pushes ("Übung für Donnerstag") reach the phone B now uses. B's tap returns 404. Separately, a parent's phone that registered first keeps receiving the child's pushes, and the child's own phone cannot register while any token is active.
- **Expected behavior:** The device in use becomes the delivery target (re-register on app start), foreign or stale tokens are deactivated, and settings show "this phone gets no messages" per device.
- **Problem:** Token ownership is "any token active" per learner, and unregistration is best-effort from the client.
- **User impact:** A minor's exam topics reach another person's lock screen; her own phone never rings while the thread claims "an dein Handy geschickt".
- **Concrete scenario:** Mother enables contact on her phone → pushes go to her; or A signs out offline → B receives A's reminders.
- **Root cause:** Registration is tied to the opt-in moment, and the server trusts the client's unregister.
- **Recommended fix:** On every signed-in start with permission, POST the current token (reassigning it from other learners), age tokens by `last_seen_at`, and report per-device status.
- **Blast radius:** Every multi-device or shared-device account once `PUSH_BACKEND=expo`.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced).

### 6a.8 Scheduler, operations and platform

#### M-66 `health-ok-despite-failing-tick` — `/health` and the home scheduler state count a tick as healthy even when every section failed

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** pg_cron tick → `GET /health`, `GET /buddy` `system.scheduler`.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/scheduler/tick.ts:54-60` (errors swallowed into `stats.errors`), `:131-134` (`last_finished_at` written regardless); `apps/api/src/app.ts:107-120` (health reads only `last_finished_at`).
- **Current behavior:** Schema drift (e.g. migration 0010 missing: `column "seq" does not exist`) fails every section, yet `/health` returns ok and the app shows the scheduler as ok.
- **Expected behavior:** A run with failed sections is not a healthy heartbeat (rule 5).
- **Problem:** "Finished" is conflated with "succeeded"; `last_error` is never read.
- **User impact:** A silent outage (no readings, checks, reminders, purges or deletions) while monitoring is green.
- **Concrete scenario:** Hosted DB without 0010 → every tick errors → `{ok:true}`.
- **Root cause:** Health is derived from a timestamp only.
- **Recommended fix:** Write `last_finished_at` only on an error-free run (keep `last_attempted_at`), or treat a non-null `last_error` as not ok; log `stats.errors`.
- **Blast radius:** All background work and operator monitoring.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (code-traced).

#### M-67 `p2-NEW-dead-scheduler-strands-work-and-privacy-promises` — When the tick never runs, the app shows nothing, sheets get stuck and deletion dates pass while shown as "geplant"

_Also covers:_ low `system-status-only-partially-surfaced` (the "unknown" state is invisible).
