- **Severity:** Medium (lowered from high in verification)
- **Affected user flow:** Scheduler health → child home, parent deletion screen, photo retention.
- **Location:** `apps/mobile/app/buddy.tsx:433-443` (banner only for `stale`); `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/home.ts:96-106` (session "working" unbounded), `:214-218`, `:476-480` (`unknown` when no heartbeat).
- **Current behavior:** With a mistyped tick secret no heartbeat exists (`unknown`) and no banner shows; queued sheets become unretryable after 2 h, purges and the 7-day deletion never run, and the parent still sees "Die Löschung ist geplant".
- **Expected behavior:** `unknown`/`stale` are surfaced; time-bound promises (deletion date, "photos go now") are not presented as facts the system cannot keep.
- **Problem:** Request-path features and privacy deadlines depend on the tick, but the app treats it as ops-only.
- **User impact:** Photos and account data outlive the retention and deletion promises in `docs/privacy.md:34,57-58` while the UI says otherwise.
- **Concrete scenario:** Fresh deploy with a wrong `lb_tick_secret` → every tick 403 → deletion due +7 d passes silently.
- **Root cause:** No bound or fallback on UI states that wait for the tick; `unknown` is ignored.
- **Recommended fix:** Treat `unknown` like `stale` after a grace period (or seed the heartbeat on migrate), broaden the banner copy, and show "overdue" in the adult section when `deletion_due_at` has passed.
- **Blast radius:** Every learner on a deployment whose cron is misconfigured or down.
- **Confidence:** Partly confirmed. The mechanisms are traced; the end-to-end outage was not run on a hosted deployment.

#### M-68 `p2-account-deletion-cascade-unindexed-fks` — Foreign-key columns without indexes: the deletion cascade and the home state query scan whole tables

_Also covers:_ `p2-state-subjects-counts-scan-all-tenants` (same root cause: per-learner queries through unindexed FK columns).

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Account deletion (`delete_account` → `auth.admin.deleteUser` → cascade); `GET /buddy`/every turn (`loadBuddyState`).
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/identity/privacy.ts:104-126`; `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/state.ts:246-255` (correlated counts on `items.subject_id`, `materials.subject_id`); `infra/supabase/migrations/0001_baseline.sql` (no index on `buddy_messages.reply_to_id`, `buddy_memories.source_message_id`, `buddy_decisions.trigger_message_id`, `buddy_actions.decision_id`, `items.subject_id`, `materials.subject_id`, …).
- **Current behavior:** Measured on a migration-built database: `delete from accounts` for one learner took 9.7–16 s (about 300k foreign messages); the subject counts seq-scan all tenants (138 ms at 200k items, `loops=5`).
- **Expected behavior:** Cost proportional to the learner's own data.
- **Problem:** Each deleted parent row or subject triggers a full scan of the referencing tables.
- **User impact:** At scale an erasure request can exceed GoTrue and lease timeouts and be parked (see §5 H-23), and home and turn latency grow with total data.
- **Concrete scenario:** 5,000 learners after a year → the deletion cascade runs inside one GoTrue request for hours.
- **Root cause:** FKs with `ON DELETE` actions and correlated subqueries without supporting indexes.
- **Recommended fix:** One new migration adding (partial) indexes on every referencing column; rewrite the subject counts as a learner-scoped `group by`; optionally delete learner rows in batches before `deleteUser`.
- **Blast radius:** Every deletion and every home load, growing with total data.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced) for the scans and timings measured; the saturation at thousands of learners is extrapolated.

#### M-69 `p2-closed-contracts-break-old-apps-on-additive-api-change` — Closed zod unions and all-or-nothing parsing mean any additive API change breaks older app builds

_Also covers:_ low `p2-ml-no-version-compat-strict-contracts-lockout`.

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** `GET /buddy`, `GET /buddy/thread`, send result, every tap returning `BuddyHome`.
- **Location:** `packages/shared-types/src/contracts/buddy.ts:8-90` (`ActionSummary` `discriminatedUnion`), `:172` (`NowCard`), `:215` (`HomeNotice`), `:227` (`Decision`); `apps/mobile/lib/api/client.ts:143-147` (safeParse on the whole body → `invalid_response`).
- **Current behavior:** One unknown tool, card, notice or status anywhere in the last 30 messages fails the whole parse; the home shows a retry-only error.
- **Expected behavior:** Unknown elements are skipped (catch-all variants, per-element `.catch`), or an explicit minimum-app-version gate exists.
- **Problem:** The API deploys instantly while store builds lag, and there is no version negotiation.
- **User impact:** After the next additive release, learners on the previous build lose the only screen.
- **Concrete scenario:** A new tool (ADR 0005 stage 3) is used once → older apps cannot parse `GET /buddy`.
- **Root cause:** Strict closed unions over persisted, evolving JSON (`buddy_actions.result`).
- **Recommended fix:** Tolerant list elements and `.catch` on presentation enums; normalise stored summaries server-side; add a min-version header.
- **Blast radius:** Every installed build older than the API.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced).

#### M-70 `walkthrough-and-fit-never-gated` — Browser walkthroughs and the rule-16 fit check run only when someone invokes them

_Also covers:_ `walkthrough-and-fit-never-automated`.

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** CLAUDE.md rule 16 (390×844 / 360×740), UI rules 13–15, all UI wiring.
- **Location:** `.github/workflows/check.yml:43-50` (typecheck, lint, test only); `.husky/pre-commit:14,24-33`; `scripts/web-walkthrough.sh:13`; `tests/web/fit.ts:55-76`.
- **Current behavior:** Playwright specs and `fit.ts` exist but run neither in CI nor in the hook; the iOS bundle smoke runs only in the hook for staged mobile files.
- **Expected behavior:** Rule 16 reads as an enforced gate; `docs/architecture.md` §Testing presents the walkthroughs as proof of wiring.
- **Problem:** The only automated UI evidence is opt-in and unrecorded.
- **User impact:** Layout or wiring regressions reach learners unnoticed.
- **Concrete scenario:** A Sheet change adds 60 px → CI green → no one runs the walkthrough.
- **Root cause:** Tier 3 was built as a manual tool.
- **Recommended fix:** A CI job (Postgres service + `expo export --platform web` + Playwright), at least layout and fit on PRs, plus `bundle:check`; otherwise soften rule 16.
- **Blast radius:** All UI flows.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (code-traced).

### 6a.9 App shell, session and navigation

#### M-71 `p2-J-home-poll-error-replaces-screen` — A failed background refetch replaces the whole home with the error screen although cached data exists

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Home polling (60 s / 3 s during turns) and refresh after a failed send.
- **Location:** `apps/mobile/app/buddy.tsx:291` (`if (home.isError)` → EmptyState before using `home.data`); `apps/mobile/lib/api/queries.ts:25-30,86-99`.
- **Current behavior:** TanStack v5 sets `status: 'error'` on a background failure while keeping the data; the thread, cards and composer are replaced by "Da ist was schiefgegangen".
- **Expected behavior:** Keep rendering cached data with a banner or toast; show the full error state only without data.
- **Problem:** The screen checks `isError` instead of `isError && !data`.
- **User impact:** During any API hiccup Lena loses sight of her message, Buddy's reply and the composer.
- **Concrete scenario:** API redeploy → SSE breaks → refresh → 502 ×3 → the whole home becomes EmptyState although the turn completed server-side.
- **Root cause:** A background refetch error is treated like a first-load error.
- **Recommended fix:** Use `home.isError && !home.data` and surface refetch errors non-destructively.
- **Blast radius:** Every learner during any API hiccup; also history.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (code-traced).

#### M-72 `signout-leaves-old-buddy-in-stack` — Sign-out leaves the previous learner's Buddy screen under welcome, and back reveals it

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Sign-out from settings, or forced session loss → `router.replace('/')` → welcome.
- **Location:** `apps/mobile/app/_layout.tsx:46-53` (`router.replace('/')`); `apps/mobile/app/index.tsx:19-21`; `apps/mobile/components/settings/AdultSection.tsx:121-131`.
- **Current behavior:** Stack `[buddy, settings]` becomes `[buddy, welcome]`; Android back or an edge swipe shows the old BuddyScreen in a 401 error state whose retry cannot succeed.
- **Expected behavior:** Session loss resets navigation; nothing of the previous learner stays reachable (`_layout.tsx:49` comment, `docs/privacy.md` shared phone).
- **Problem:** `replace` swaps only the top route.
- **User impact:** On a shared family phone the sibling sees the previous child's screen and must kill the app to get out.
- **Concrete scenario:** Settings → Abmelden → welcome → Android back → old Buddy screen.
- **Root cause:** No `dismissAll`, and the Stack is not keyed on the session.
- **Recommended fix:** `router.dismissAll()` before replacing, or key `<Stack>` on the user id; redirect the Buddy error state to welcome when there is no session.
- **Blast radius:** Every sign-out and forced session loss.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (code-traced).

#### M-73 `p2-reset-logs-out-childs-device-and-wipes-queue` — After a password change the child's other device is signed out and its unsent answers and photo drafts are wiped without explanation

- **Severity:** Medium (lowered from high)
- **Affected user flow:** Password reset link or settings → Passwort ändern; the learner's other device.
- **Location:** `apps/mobile/lib/auth/supabase.ts:156-162` (`finishRecovery`), `:202-207` (`changePassword`), `:230-241` (`refreshSession`). The finding's cited `:462-468/:508-513` do not exist (file has 246 lines). `apps/mobile/app/_layout.tsx:46-53` (wipes outbox and drafts on any null session).
- **Current behavior:** GoTrue revokes all other sessions on a password update (`LogoutAllExceptMe`); the other device fails its next refresh, clears `lb.outbox.v1` and the capture drafts, and lands on welcome with no reason.
- **Expected behavior:** Keep the queue across a forced sign-out of the same user (wipe only when a different user signs in), and explain "Das Passwort wurde geändert".
- **Problem:** The null-session handler, written for "shared phone, next person", runs unconditionally.
- **User impact:** Offline answers and unsent worksheet photos are lost, and the child is stuck until a parent is present.
- **Concrete scenario:** Mum resets her password on her phone → within 1 h the tablet refreshes → 400 → queue wiped.
- **Root cause:** The client wipe is keyed on "session became null", not "different user".
- **Recommended fix:** Scope outbox and drafts by `user_id`, purge only on a different sign-in or an explicit sign-out, and show the reason on welcome.
- **Blast radius:** Families where parent and child use different devices (the intended setup). Related: §5 H-28.
- **Confidence:** Partly confirmed. The chain holds (GoTrue behaviour from source), but several cited lines were wrong and have been corrected here.

#### M-74 `p2-signout-offline-ios-may-hang-on-apns` — Sign-out awaits a push-token fetch with no timeout; offline on iOS it may never finish

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Settings → Abmelden with notification permission granted.
- **Location:** `apps/mobile/components/settings/AdultSection.tsx:121-131` (`await unregisterDeviceForPush()` before `signOut()`); `apps/mobile/lib/push.ts:60-71` (`getExpoPushTokenAsync`).
- **Current behavior:** On iOS the token fetch waits for `registerForRemoteNotifications`, which offline commonly calls neither delegate; the buttons stay dimmed and the device stays signed in.
- **Expected behavior:** Sign-out completes locally at once; unregistration is best-effort with a short timeout.
- **Problem:** A local operation is serialised behind an unbounded remote one.
- **User impact:** A parent cannot sign the child out on an offline iPhone.
- **Concrete scenario:** Airplane mode → Abmelden → nothing happens for minutes.
- **Root cause:** An awaited network token fetch with no timeout.
- **Recommended fix:** Store the token at registration and send that; wrap unregister in a 3–5 s `Promise.race`, or fire it after `clearSession`.
- **Blast radius:** iOS devices that opted in to contact and are offline at sign-out.
- **Confidence:** Needs runtime verification. Airplane-mode sign-out on a physical iPhone with push configured.

#### M-75 `history-merge-gap-under-polling` — History silently drops messages when new rows arrive after older pages were loaded

_Also covers:_ `repro-25` (runtime proof). The descriptive id was rated low in phase 1 and is raised to medium with the runtime proof.

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** History with more than 30 messages → "Mehr laden" → a new Buddy message arrives during polling.
- **Location:** `apps/mobile/app/history.tsx:25-26,33-35` (older pages concatenated with the live 30-message window); `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/home.ts:25` (`THREAD_LIMIT = 30`).
- **Current behavior:** Reproduced: after 2 new rows, the merged list had 34 of 36 rows and the 2 boundary messages were missing.
- **Expected behavior:** Merge pages by id and sort by `seq`.
- **Problem:** A fixed older page is concatenated with a sliding window.
- **User impact:** Messages disappear from the middle of the conversation; with `has_more` false there is no way to get them back.
- **Concrete scenario:** 17 exchanges → load older → a reply completes → "Nachricht 3" and "Alles klar!" vanish.
- **Root cause:** Concatenation instead of a keyed union.
- **Recommended fix:** A `Map` by message id over older pages plus `home.thread`, sorted by `seq`.
- **Blast radius:** Anyone reading long histories while Buddy posts.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced).

#### M-76 `p2-ux-toast-hidden-behind-ios-keyboard` — On iOS, error toasts render behind the keyboard, and a failed chat message is already cleared from the composer

_Also covers:_ `p2-ux-narrative-iphone-bus` / `typed-message-lost-on-send-failure` / `chat-message-lost-before-server` (the composer is cleared before the server stored the message, so the only failure signal is the hidden toast).

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Chat send, typed practice answer, sign-in, memory edit while the keyboard is open.
- **Location:** `apps/mobile/components/lb/Toast.tsx:41-47` (absolute, `bottom: insets.bottom + 90`); `apps/mobile/app/_layout.tsx:86` (host at root, outside any `KeyboardAvoidingView`); `apps/mobile/components/buddy/Composer.tsx:45-46` (`onSend` then `setText('')`); `apps/mobile/app/buddy.tsx:209-215`.
- **Current behavior:** The toast sits about 124 pt from the bottom under a 290–340 pt keyboard. A chat message that failed before reaching the server is gone from the composer, and no failed bubble exists.
- **Expected behavior:** Failures are visible where the user looks (inline, or a keyboard-aware toast); unsent text stays as a local failed bubble with "Nochmal senden" (rule 5).
- **Problem:** On iOS a failure produces no visible signal, and the input is lost.
- **User impact:** Lena thinks Buddy ignores her, retypes, and may create duplicate plans; a wrong password just re-enables the button.
- **Concrete scenario:** On the bus: types "Wir schreiben am Donnerstag Englisch" → tunnel → field cleared → toast hidden.
- **Root cause:** The toast is not keyboard-aware, and the composer clears optimistically.
- **Recommended fix:** Offset `ToastHost` by the keyboard height (or anchor it at the top); keep unsent text as a local failed bubble.
- **Blast radius:** All iOS users on mobile networks.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (code-traced).

### 6a.10 Voice

#### M-77 `voice-mode-unreachable-from-home` — Voice mode can only be switched on in the practice header, never from the chat

_Also covers:_ `repro-23` (exhaustive static trace of the store writers), `voice-mode-only-switchable-in-practice`.

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Voice mode in the chat (voice-first Composer, reply reading) and in the TopicSheet.
- **Location:** `apps/mobile/components/voice/VoiceModeToggle.tsx:15-25` (the only `setOn(true)`), rendered only at `apps/mobile/app/practice/[id].tsx:531`; `apps/mobile/components/buddy/Composer.tsx:80-86` (keyboard button → `setVoiceMode(false)` only).
- **Current behavior:** On the home there is no on-switch; one tap on "Tastatur" turns voice mode off until she opens a practice session.
- **Expected behavior:** `docs/architecture.md:497-500` promises voice mode in the chat, every practice mode and the topic sheet.
- **Problem:** The feature exists, but its entry point is missing where the docs and code expect it.
- **User impact:** A child who wants replies read aloud cannot ask for it from home. This also blocks the screen-reader fallback in M-81.
- **Concrete scenario:** Fresh install → home → no switch; the headphones button opens `/talk` instead.
- **Root cause:** The toggle was wired into one screen only.
- **Recommended fix:** Render `VoiceModeToggle` in the home header or composer and in the TopicSheet (same component).
- **Blast radius:** Chat, TopicSheet and home voice use.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (code-traced; the store trace is exhaustive).

#### M-78 `handsfree-recording-outlives-turn` — A hands-free server-path recording is not cancelled when she answers another way, and its late transcript becomes a second attempt

_Also covers:_ `hands-free-server-path-no-tap-hint`.

- **Severity:** Medium (lowered from high)
- **Affected user flow:** Voice mode + hands-free loop in practice on the server recognition path (web, or phones falling back to server).
- **Location:** `apps/mobile/components/voice/useHandsFreeMic.ts:22-29` (loop calls `voice.toggle`); `apps/mobile/lib/speech/record.ts:204` (only a tap or 60 s ends it); `apps/mobile/app/practice/[id].tsx:211-245` (reading question or feedback never stops the mic).
- **Current behavior:** A 60 s recording keeps running after she taps a choice or types; if the question is still open when it ends, the transcript (silence, or Buddy's own TTS) is submitted as another attempt.
- **Expected behavior:** One listening per turn, cancelled when she answers another way or TTS starts (`docs/architecture.md:500-504`).
- **Problem:** The recording has no owner tied to the turn.
- **User impact:** An attempt she never gave counts against her (attempts +1, reveal-after-3 rule, FSRS), and her typed text is replaced.
- **Concrete scenario:** MC question read → mic starts → she taps B (wrong) → feedback read aloud → recording ends → transcript submitted.
- **Root cause:** The server path has no pause detection; `useVoiceInput.send()` uses the latest screen state.
- **Recommended fix:** Tie the recording to a turn token and cancel it on answer, typing, reveal, next or TTS start; do not auto-start the server recorder.
- **Blast radius:** Voice mode on web and on server-path phones.
- **Confidence:** Partly confirmed. The web/server path is traced; the device path with `untilPause` is runtime-dependent.

#### M-79 `p2-home-voice-reads-late-reply-on-other-screens` — The home's voice watcher reads a late chat reply over practice or talk mode

_Also covers:_ `p2-lc-home-voice-watch-speaks-over-other-screens`.

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Voice mode: home send → reply arrives late → learner is in hands-free practice or conversation mode.
- **Location:** `apps/mobile/app/buddy.tsx:136-143` (effect not focus-gated), `:177` (`awaitingReply` set on send), `:209-213` (not cleared on failure); `apps/mobile/lib/speech/spoken.ts:92-105` (`replyAfter` = newest Buddy message).
- **Current behavior:** The still-mounted home reads the reply with `speakInOrder` on whatever screen is showing, cutting off the question being read and stopping the hands-free loop.
- **Expected behavior:** A reply is read only while home is focused, and only if it answers the sent message.
- **Problem:** A screen-scoped side effect acts globally.
- **User impact:** Children relying on voice hear an unrelated chat reply over their practice, and the loop stops listening.
- **Concrete scenario:** Send from home → SSE drops → "Weiter üben" → turn completes → the home reads the reply over question 1.
- **Root cause:** The effect depends only on thread/voiceOn/words; `awaitingReply` is never cleared on failure or blur.
- **Recommended fix:** Gate on `useIsFocused`, clear on blur and failure, and match by `reply_to_id`.
- **Blast radius:** Voice-mode users who leave home with an unresolved turn.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (code-traced).

### 6a.11 Accessibility

#### M-80 `p2-a11y-toast-never-announced-ios` — Toasts, capture/dictation status lines and PIN errors rely on Android-only live regions, so VoiceOver hears none of them

_Also covers:_ `p2-a11y-status-android-only-capture-dictation`, `p2-a11y-pin-error-silent` (same root cause: `accessibilityLiveRegion` as the only channel, 26 uses, no iOS equivalent).

- **Severity:** Medium (lowered from high)
- **Affected user flow:** Every toast (about 40 call sites); capture send progress and failure, photo check, dictation, WorkingNote, talk phase; the PIN modal for every adult-gated action.
- **Location:** `apps/mobile/components/lb/Toast.tsx:36-49` (`accessibilityLiveRegion` + role `alert`, which RN maps to no iOS trait); `apps/mobile/components/capture/SendBar.tsx:52-67`; `apps/mobile/components/capture/PhotoCheckCard.tsx:25`; `apps/mobile/app/pin.tsx:34-50,73-89`.
- **Current behavior:** No `announceForAccessibility` accompanies these states. A repeated identical PIN error is not re-announced on Android either.
- **Expected behavior:** Every such state change is announced on both platforms.
- **Problem:** The app's single channel for errors and confirmations is imperceptible to VoiceOver users.
- **User impact:** A blind child does not learn that a send failed or what the mic transcribed; a blind parent retries the PIN into the lockout without hearing "gesperrt".
- **Concrete scenario:** iOS 18 + VoiceOver, airplane mode → Senden → toast "Das hat nicht geklappt" → silence.
- **Root cause:** Android-only live regions used as the only screen-reader channel.
- **Recommended fix:** Add a `useAnnounce(text)` hook calling `announceForAccessibilityWithOptions(text, {queue:true})` in `toast.show`, SendBar, PhotoCheckCard, MicStatus, WorkingNote and the PIN error; key the PIN error by attempt.
- **Blast radius:** All iOS screen-reader users.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (code-traced). Not run with VoiceOver.

#### M-81 `p2-a11y-buddy-reply-never-announced` — Buddy's reply is never announced to screen readers

- **Severity:** Medium (lowered from high)
- **Affected user flow:** Home: type or dictate a message → hear Buddy's reply and its option buttons.
- **Location:** `apps/mobile/app/buddy.tsx:136-143,169-219`; `apps/mobile/components/buddy/Conversation.tsx:71-95,200-228`.
- **Current behavior:** The reply is read only in voice mode (which cannot be enabled from home, M-77). iOS gets no announcement; Android only for streamed replies with no actions.
- **Expected behavior:** Announce "Buddy: <spoken text>" when the reply for her message is stored, with or without voice mode.
- **Problem:** The core loop gives screen-reader users no signal that, or what, Buddy answered.
- **User impact:** A VoiceOver child must hunt through the thread by swiping; option chips are never mentioned.
- **Concrete scenario:** iOS 18 → "Erklär mir Brüche" → reply with `offer_learning` → silence.
- **Root cause:** Reply feedback was built only for TTS voice mode and Android live regions.
- **Recommended fix:** In the reply-found effect, when voice mode is off, call `announceForAccessibilityWithOptions` with the spoken text (math in words).
- **Blast radius:** Every chat turn for screen-reader users.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (code-traced).

#### M-82 `p2-a11y-practice-feedback-verdict-hint-solution` — Practice feedback for screen readers omits the verdict, is silent for Tipp/Lösung and reads raw LaTeX

- **Severity:** Medium (lowered from high)
- **Affected user flow:** Practice answer → hear right/wrong and the hint; Tipp; Lösung zeigen.
- **Location:** `apps/mobile/app/practice/[id].tsx:230-245,321-322,344-380`; `apps/mobile/components/practice/SolutionCard.tsx:22-37`.
- **Current behavior:** With voice mode off, only `res.reply.text` is announced (no "Noch nicht ganz", raw LaTeX); Tipp and Lösung zeigen announce nothing on either platform.
- **Expected behavior:** Use the voice-mode text (`feedbackReadText` with verdict word and math in words) for all three paths, queued.
- **Problem:** Screen-reader feedback exists on only one of three result paths and bypasses the spoken-text helpers.
- **User impact:** A visually impaired child cannot tell whether her answer was right, and may take the hint for praise.
- **Concrete scenario:** Wrong answer to "Kürze 6/8" → hears only the hint sentence.
- **Root cause:** Feedback added ad hoc to the answer path only.
- **Recommended fix:** A single `speakFeedback(res)` used by answer, hint and reveal.
- **Blast radius:** Every practice, explain and help session for screen-reader users.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (code-traced).

#### M-83 `p2-a11y-pruefen-disappears-large-text` — At the largest text sizes, "Tipp" and "Lösung zeigen" squeeze "Prüfen" to zero width

_Also covers:_ `p2-ux-pruefen-truncated-at-360`, `p2-ux-narrative-small-android-explain`.

- **Severity:** Medium (lowered from high)
- **Affected user flow:** Typed answers when hint and reveal are both available.
- **Location:** `apps/mobile/components/practice/AnswerComposer.tsx:216-245` (two natural-width ghost Btns + `flex:1` primary in one row); `apps/mobile/components/lb/Btn.tsx:40-44,139-151`.
- **Current behavior:** At iOS AX2+ the primary CTA has zero width; at Android 200 % it is about 20 pt; the walkthrough screenshot at 360 px already shows "Pr…" truncated.
- **Expected behavior:** The main action always stays reachable (own row, wrapped side actions, capped font scale).
- **Problem:** A fixed three-button row with non-shrinking siblings.
- **User impact:** Low-vision children cannot submit long answers.
- **Concrete scenario:** Pixel, font 200 %, explain item with hints → no Prüfen to tap; Return inserts a newline.
- **Root cause:** No wrap fallback and no `maxFontSizeMultiplier`.
- **Recommended fix:** Put Prüfen on its own full-width row, Tipp/Lösung in a wrapping row above, and cap Btn scaling at about 1.6–1.8.
- **Blast radius:** All typed practice items at large text.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced via the 360 px screenshot; large-text sizes computed).

#### M-84 `p2-a11y-dynamic-type-clipping-untested` — Fixed-height controls do not accommodate very large system text; clipping at accessibility sizes is untested

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** All screens at large system text (home ring, CTAs, PIN pad, multiple choice).
- **Location:** `apps/mobile/components/lb/Btn.tsx:40-44,96-107,139-151` (fixed heights, overflow hidden); `apps/mobile/components/lb/OrbitMenu.tsx:15-18,135-151`; `apps/mobile/components/lb/PinPad.tsx:81-103` (fixed key boxes); no `maxFontSizeMultiplier` anywhere (grep).
- **Current behavior:** At iOS AX3 a md label's line height (about 58 pt) exceeds its 48 pt box; ring labels can overlap text; `tests/web/fit.ts` measures viewports only.
- **Expected behavior:** Capped scaling where a fixed box is required, `minHeight` elsewhere, and one fit pass at a scaled root font.
- **Problem:** Clipped primary controls for low-vision children, undetectable by the no-scroll guard.
- **User impact:** "Weiter mit Bud…" cut in half, PIN digits clipped.
- **Concrete scenario:** iOS AX3 → home and practice CTAs clipped (computed, not observed).
- **Root cause:** Components designed at scale 1.0 with no scaling policy.
- **Recommended fix:** Add `maxFontSizeMultiplier` to TYPE-based components, use `minHeight` in Btn, add a font-scale fit pass and a native snapshot.
- **Blast radius:** App-wide visual layer.
- **Confidence:** Needs runtime verification. Screenshots at iOS AX3 and Android 200 %.

#### M-85 `p2-a11y-talk-mode-records-voiceover` — Conversation mode opens the mic automatically with a screen reader on, and may record VoiceOver's own speech

- **Severity:** Medium
- **Affected user flow:** Home headphones → `/talk`.
- **Location:** `apps/mobile/app/talk.tsx:79-86,114,188-191,269-275`.
- **Current behavior:** `listen()` runs on mount and after every reply; VoiceOver's "Beenden, Taste" can be recognised and sent; phase changes are not announced on iOS.
- **Expected behavior:** With a screen reader on, start paused, require a tap (or Magic Tap) and announce each phase.
- **Problem:** Mic capture overlaps screen-reader speech.
- **User impact:** A blind child's conversation is driven by VoiceOver's own utterances.
- **Concrete scenario:** iOS + VoiceOver → "Mit Buddy sprechen" → answer("Beenden Taste").
- **Root cause:** The hands-free loop assumes the phone is silent while listening.
- **Recommended fix:** Check `isScreenReaderEnabled()` on mount, start paused, add `onMagicTap`, and announce phases.
- **Blast radius:** Conversation mode for screen-reader users.
- **Confidence:** Needs runtime verification. A VoiceOver session on a device with on-device recognition.

### 6b. Lower-priority findings

The 319 low findings in `final-rest.json` (205 confirmed, 111 partly confirmed, 3 needing runtime) are grouped by theme below. To keep the list actionable, 56 were taken out of the tables or combined, on these grounds:

- **6 promoted to medium.** Each is the descriptive id of a merged runtime reproduction (BRIEF addendum): `apply-vs-tap-lock-order-deadlock` (M-45), `check-lease-shorter-than-runtime` (M-47), `timezone-header-no-context-bump` (M-48), `quote-checked-against-trigger-only` (M-49), `merged-part-no-wake` (M-12), `history-merge-gap-under-polling` (M-75).
- **15 folded into a higher finding.** Same root cause and location; each is named in that entry's "Also covers" line: `minor-consent-never-refreshed` (M-2), `p2-ml-learner-profile-uncorrectable` (M-3), `p2-forget-keeps-statement-everywhere` (M-8), `export-web-no-delivery` and `repro-27` (M-11), `p2-blocks-look-like-outages` (M-17), `p2-F4b` (M-29), `p2-ux-offer-card-reopens-old-session` (M-37), `dialogue-may-end-with-model-turn` (M-50), `p2-ml-home-taps-irreversible` (M-57), `opened-proof-not-durable` (M-64), `only-latest-device-receives-pushes` (M-65), `system-status-only-partially-surfaced` (M-67), `p2-ml-no-version-compat-strict-contracts-lockout` (M-69), `p2-sec-pin-lockout-no-escalation` (§5 H-17).
- **13 combined with another low into one row.** These are duplicates of each other; the absorbed id is shown as "(+`id`)" in the row.
- **22 dropped.** Each is a pure style nit, has no behavioural effect, or affects only the web build, which is a declared non-target (`docs/architecture.md`: "Test tooling only; never deployed"):
  - Comments and doc nits: `answercomposer-stale-speak-comment`, `config-fast-tier-comment-contradicts-routes`, `background-contract-false-for-hints`, `docs-body-limit-route`.
  - No effect: `goals-steps-created-at-sql-default`, `pricing-fallback-not-max` (cost records for unknown model ids only), `events-marked-handled-when-deferred` and `plan-status-on-dedupe-conflict` (no reader), `scripted-gateway-skips-schema` (downstream zod catches it), `wiring-audit-regex-weak` (no false signal today), `lookup-outage-masked-as-nothing-found` (the turn fails elsewhere anyway), `repair-errors-never-reset` (documented), `routine-queued-during-pause` (writes only a "wait" row), `voice-cache-null-forever` (platform-side; a restart fixes it).
  - By design: `signup-existing-email-fake-confirm` (enumeration protection).
  - Web or dev only: `dev-stack-cors-and-clock-differ-from-production`, `unsupported-hint-asks-to-retry`, `history-spinner-dead-end`, `p2-lc-web-tabs-stale-refresh-token`, `p2-web-sent-photos-crowd-out-outbox`, `web-oversize-draft-stale`, `web-tokens-localstorage`.

That leaves **263 rows in 16 themes**. Confidence per row: 136 confirmed (code-traced), 43 confirmed (runtime-reproduced), 82 partly confirmed, 2 need runtime verification. The Problem column is the verified title, shortened. The Location column is the first cited file:line. Fourteen citations were corrected: 13 pointed past the end of the file at HEAD `62a6028` (mostly `apps/mobile/lib/auth/supabase.ts`, `recovery.ts` and `app/index.tsx`, which are much shorter than the audit data assumed), and one lacked a path. Items a maintainer should look at first are called out after the tables.

**Low items worth doing early.** Each is cheap to fix and touches a hard rule:

- `p2-undo-of-set-contact-loosens-without-pin`: undo lets a minor restore looser contact settings without the PIN (rule 6; runtime-reproduced).
- `schedule-check-compatible-dst`: a model-given time inside a clock change is resolved "compatible" instead of rejected (rule 2).
- `answers-do-not-bump-context`: per-answer progress does not bump `context_version` (rule 4).
- `p2-sec-pin-gate-vanishes-at-16-for-child-profile`: account-holder rights pass to the child at 16 with no notice.
- `reply-to-id-not-scoped`: a cross-learner id is accepted.
- `p2-model-outreach-lock-screen-unchecked` and `lock-screen-text-carries-title-and-count`: the lock-screen rule is enforced by prompt only.
- `timeouts-consume-budget`: failed calls count against the budget, contrary to the docs.
- `turn-seq-collision-500`: reproduced 12/12 under concurrency.

---

## 7. Broken or Incomplete User Journeys

19 journeys were traced step by step from screen to database and back (source: `final-journeys.json`). Each trace was checked against browser walkthroughs, integration tests and persona runs. **None of the 19 holds end to end.** 3 break, meaning a core promise of the journey fails on a common path. 16 hold only partly, meaning the happy path works and is tested but a realistic branch fails.

How ids are cited: finding ids are the canonical ids from §4–6. Where a runtime reproduction (`repro-NN`) proves the same defect, it appears in brackets as runtime proof, for example `homework-sheet-practice-dead-end` (runtime: `repro-05`).

### 7.1 Journeys that break

#### J-HW. Homework help: photo → help session → continue the next day. **Verdict: breaks**

Who is hit: the learner (Lena, 12, doing homework in the evening). The parent is hit indirectly because no trace of the homework remains after 12 h.

The same-evening half works. The rest of the journey does not exist.

| #   | Step                                                         | What happens                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | Status                                                                                           |
| --- | ------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| 1   | Home → 'Hausaufgabe' → ChoiceSheet → 'Aufgabe fotografieren' | `purpose=homework` travels through capture (`buddy.tsx` ChoiceSheet → `/capture`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | ok                                                                                               |
| 2   | Photo, quality check, 'Senden'                               | Idempotent create/upload/submit; the extraction job is queued                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | ok (WorkingNote says 'Übungen', `p2-HW-07`)                                                      |
| 3   | Extraction with `HOMEWORK_SYSTEM`                            | Items get `origin='homework'` and a `practice_sessions` row with mode `help` is created. `homework_ready` wakes no one (declared non-goal)                                                                                                                                                                        | ok                                                                                               |
| 4   | Home: 'Weiter mit deiner Hausaufgabe'                        | The resume card appears only while `now − started_at < 12 h` (`apps/api/src/modules/buddy/home.ts:136-141`)                                                                                                                                                                                                       | ok for 12 h                                                                                      |
| 5   | **Help screen: stuck on task 1**                             | No skip, no next, no 'Tipp', no flag. Help mode passes no `skip` (`apps/mobile/app/practice/[id].tsx:524`). The only other control is 'Beenden'                                                                                                                                                                   | **breaks** `p2-HW-03`                                                                            |
| 6   | **Answer that is right but written differently**             | The tutor says 'correct'. The server demotes the verdict to `partially_correct` unless the learner's text contains the key literally (`apps/api/src/modules/practice/service.ts:658-666`). '0,875' for '7/8' stays 'Fast' forever                                                                                 | **breaks** `p2-HW-02`                                                                            |
| 7   | **Tutor over-praises an intermediate step**                  | The leak check runs before the demotion (`service.ts:634-665`), so a reply that contains the final result is stored and shown                                                                                                                                                                                     | **breaks the core promise** `p2-HW-01`, `p2-sec-homework-leak-check-skipped-on-correct`, `p2-T3` |
| 8   | Model outage mid-homework                                    | 'Das kann ich gerade nicht prüfen … Lösung ansehen', but help mode has no solution button                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | degrades `p2-F-journey-cannot-check-steers-to-reveal`                                            |
| 9   | **'Beenden' with tasks open**                                | The session is finished for good, and the copy says 'Deine Antworten bleiben gespeichert'. It cannot be reopened                                                                                                                                                                                                  | **breaks** `beenden-finishes-for-good`                                                           |
| 10  | **Next morning (> 12 h)**                                    | No card. Library 'Üben' → `selectPracticeItems` excludes homework (`apps/api/src/modules/practice/selection.ts:54`) → 404 `no_questions` → 'Fotografier zuerst ein Arbeitsblatt'. The material screen never uses `session_id`. Buddy's STATE counts homework as practice questions and no tool can open a session | **breaks** `homework-sheet-practice-dead-end` (runtime: `repro-05`), `p2-HW-06`                  |
| 11  | Session left open                                            | Never abandoned. It stays in STATE as 'active 2/6' forever                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | degrades `stale-active-sessions-never-abandoned`                                                 |
| 12  | Only recovery: photograph again                              | Progress is lost and duplicate sessions and items pile up                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | degrades                                                                                         |
| 13  | Sheet deleted in 'Mein Stoff'                                | The help session stays active. The home still offers the deleted tasks and accepts answers                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | degrades `p2-HW-05`                                                                              |

Related: `homework-failed-new-photo-becomes-study` ('Neues Foto' after a failed homework reading drops `purpose`), `homework-no-hint-button`.

#### J-PR. Practice a session: answer → hints → solution → finish → summary → 'nochmal'. **Verdict: breaks**

Who is hit: the learner in every mode (practice, explain, test, vocab), and the parent, because the grades feed Buddy's picture of what 'sits'. The screen flow is coherent. The journey's central promise, **server-side rule grading that is right wherever rules decide**, fails in both directions on common school inputs.

| #   | Step                                                          | What happens                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | Status                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| --- | ------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 1–4 | Open session, question shown, choice or typed answer composed | One question at a time, answer kept in the outbox before sending                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | ok                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| 5   | **Typed answer → rule check**                                 | Short, long and formula answers are compared after `normalizeShortAnswer(plainMath())` (`apps/api/src/modules/practice/evaluate.ts:147-149`). The normaliser replaces every non-letter/digit with a space (`packages/shared-math/src/normalize.ts:8-16`), so `x=5` ≡ `x=-5` and `x^2-2x` ≡ `x^2+2x` are ruled correct with no model call and FSRS Good. It also ignores capitalisation, ß/ss and punctuation, so wrong German spelling passes | **breaks (wrong → right)** `p2-F1`, `p2-NM-01`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| 5b  | **Numeric answer → parser**                                   | '3 1/2' is read as 31/2 = 15.5 and '25 %' as 0.25. Three-decimal keys (0.125) are read as thousands in de/fr/es/it and also for English learners who type a comma. A 1 % tolerance accepts 242 for 240. An unevaluated expression equal to the key passes. A rule 'incorrect' can never be rescued by the tutor (`apps/api/src/modules/practice/tutor.ts:128`)                                                                                | **breaks (right → wrong and wrong → right)** `p2-F2`, `p2-percent-answer-divided-by-100`, `p2-latex-mixed-key-plainmath-31-2`, `numeric-key-three-decimals-misparsed` (runtime: `repro-03`), `p2-en-comma-three-decimals-answer-misgraded`, `tolerance-accepts-wrong-integers` (runtime: `repro-04`), `p2-T1` |
| 5c  | Near-miss and choice rules                                    | `missing_word` is positional. A spoken 'A' collides with an option whose text is 'a'. A prefix match ignores 'nicht …'                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | degrades `p2-NM-02`, `p2-NM-04`, `p2-NM-07`, `p2-NM-03`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| 8   | Tutor path under outage                                       | The field is cleared although nothing was graded, and the copy points to a solution that tests and homework do not have                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | degrades `p2-F3`, `p2-F4b`, `p2-F-journey-cannot-check-steers-to-reveal`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| 10  | Hints                                                         | 'Tipp' after the last prepared hint silently reveals the solution and closes the question                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | degrades `tipp-after-last-hint-reveals`, `hint-not-idempotent-from-app`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| 14  | Auto-finish                                                   | If `/finish` never succeeds, the session vanishes from the home and the step stays `in_progress`. The spinner has no timeout                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | degrades `all-answered-unfinished-session-invisible`, `p2-F9`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| 15  | 'Beenden' mid-session                                         | Finishes for good. An explain session's text is gone with it. Untouched test questions show as 'Noch nicht richtig'                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | **breaks** `beenden-finishes-for-good`, `test-beenden-untouched-as-missed`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| 16  | Summary                                                       | 'Beantwortet' counts questions that were only revealed or skipped, and the step is marked done on that evidence                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | degrades `p2-F5`, `summary-vs-home-secure-shaky-disagree`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| 17  | **'Die wackligen nochmal üben'**                              | Asks the model to invent new questions from a topic string. The shaky items themselves are not re-asked                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | **breaks the promise of the button** `p2-F4`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| –   | Retyped answer after a 502                                    | The first answer replays later from the outbox as a phantom attempt                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | degrades `p2-F6`, `outbox-flush-vs-resumed-screen-double-attempt`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

#### J-MD. Model unavailable or daily budget used up, across every surface. **Verdict: breaks**

Who is hit: the learner (all surfaces), and the parent, who cannot tell an outage from a broken phone. Each layer fails without crashing. Across screens the journey breaks in five places.

| #     | Step                                       | What happens                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | Status                                                                                                           |
| ----- | ------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 1     | **Open the app on an outage day**          | `system.model` = `deps.llm.available` (`apps/api/src/modules/buddy/home.ts:482`), and `VertexGateway.available` is hard-coded `true` (`apps/api/src/llm/vertex.ts:108`). An outage or a used-up budget is invisible. In the no-model deployment the banner claims practice still works                                                             | **breaks** `p2-F-journey-outage-invisible-on-home`, `p2-ux-model-outage-inconsistent`                            |
| 2–3   | **Chat message fails**                     | A 4.5 s toast gives the reason. After that the thread permanently says 'Nicht angekommen' (`apps/mobile/locales/de/buddy.json:101`) although the server stored the message. The reason is not persisted                                                                                                                                            | **breaks (false claim, rule 5)** `p2-F-journey-failed-label-claims-not-arrived`, `p2-J-failed-label-not-arrived` |
| 4     | 'Nochmal senden'                           | Futile until local midnight when the budget is used up. Nothing says 'morgen' once the toast is gone                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | degrades same ids, `p2-F-journey-budget-copy-global-per-purpose`                                                 |
| 5     | Starters (homework, vocab, speak, explain) | 503 after up to 60 s, or 429 with 'Üben geht trotzdem', shown exactly when she tried to start practice                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | degrades `p2-ux-model-outage-inconsistent`                                                                       |
| 6     | **Photo reading**                          | A retryable `LlmError` is retried at +1 and +2 min, then fails as `model_error`. Each 'Nochmal lesen' uses one of `MAX_EXTRACTION_ATTEMPTS = 3` (`apps/api/src/modules/materials/service.ts:46`). A 10-minute outage blames good photos permanently                                                                                                | **breaks** `p2-ux-narrative-outage-day`, `budget-refusals-consume-retry-runs`                                    |
| 8     | **Practice fallback**                      | 'Das kann ich gerade nicht prüfen … Lösung ansehen'. Following it records a possibly correct answer as skipped with FSRS Again. Nothing re-checks answers with verdict null                                                                                                                                                                        | **breaks (data poisoning)** `p2-F-journey-cannot-check-steers-to-reveal` (runtime-proven)                        |

Patterns across all 19 journeys:

1. **Only happy paths are proven.** The dev stack runs on the wall clock with auto-confirmed e-mail and a scripted model, so every time-driven, confirmation or outage branch is invisible to the walkthroughs (`dev-stack-wall-clock-hides-time-driven-ui`, `dev-stack-scripted-fifo-invisible-failures`).
2. **The app cannot re-enter closed or aged objects.** This affects sessions older than 12 h, finished sessions, cards beyond the last six messages and a tapped outcome.
3. **Many failures end in a false or mute statement** instead of a crash: 'Nicht angekommen', 'Fotografier zuerst ein Arbeitsblatt', 'Lösung ansehen', 'Buddy vergisst das'. This conflicts with CLAUDE.md rule 5.

---

## 8. Missing Product Logic

These are rules the product needs but no code implements. They come from findings in the categories `missing_logic`, `completeness` and `journey`, and from the hypotheses in `model.md`. Declared non-goals (push off by default, homework excluded from drilling, one learner per account, no dashboards or due counts) are not listed. Where a fix touches the UI, it must follow CLAUDE.md rule 16: Buddy in the chat first, no new screens or lists.

| #   | Missing rule                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | Why the product needs it                                                                                                                                                                | Where it would live                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | Finding ids                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |

| 1   | **Session lifecycle: re-entry and expiry.** Any open session, especially a help session, needs a re-entry path after 12 h. Sessions left for days need an abandonment rule that returns the step to `prepared`. 'Beenden' needs a pause-vs-finish distinction                                                                                                                | Homework is typically resumed the next day. Stale active sessions block `startFromStep` and pollute STATE forever                                                                       | `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/home.ts` (resume card keyed on last activity, not `started_at`); a scheduler job in `scheduler/`; a Buddy tool `open_session` in `modules/buddy/tools.ts`; the material screen using `MaterialView.session_id` | `homework-sheet-practice-dead-end` (runtime: `repro-05`), `stale-active-sessions-never-abandoned`, `beenden-finishes-for-good`, `all-answered-unfinished-session-invisible`, `p2-HW-06`                                                                                                                                                           |

`model.md` hypotheses and what became of them: most were turned into runtime reproductions. The lock-order deadlock (`repro-01`), in-app volume (`repro-02`), numeric grading (`repro-03`, `repro-04`), homework unreachability (`repro-05`), undo dead-end (`repro-06`), PIN brute force (`repro-08`, `repro-09`), onboarding dead-end (`repro-10`), 16/17-year-old profile (`repro-11`), draft resume (`repro-12`), delete during extraction (`repro-13`), photo retention (`repro-14`), agreed-reminder loss (`repro-15`), merged pages (`repro-16`), lease overrun (`repro-17`), shared-phone push token (`repro-20`), time-zone fence (`repro-21`), split utterances (`repro-22`), voice-mode reachability (`repro-23`), deletion limbo (`repro-24`), history gap (`repro-25`), refresh wipe (`repro-26`), web export (`repro-27`) and speakable-before-validation (`repro-28`) hypotheses were reproduced. Three remain open. Streaming on Vercel (`repro-18`) and the notification cold start (`repro-19`) **need runtime verification** on a real host or device. The web hands-free misattribution is only code-traced and partly confirmed (`handsfree-recording-outlives-turn`). The i18n-plural-on-Hermes hypothesis produced no finding. The PIN-takeover chain (`repro-07`) holds only in the narrowed form: medium severity, intended fresh-amr path, not reachable by a child inside the app.

## 9. Cross-System Contradictions

The repository makes many explicit promises in `docs/privacy.md`, `docs/architecture.md`,
CLAUDE.md, zod contracts, prompts and UI copy. A reader could reasonably treat each of them as
enforced. The tables below list where one side promises something the other side does not do.
They are grouped by which two systems disagree.

### 9.1 Privacy promise vs code and infrastructure

| Side A says                                                                                            | Side B does                                                                                                                                                             | Consequence                                                                                    | Ids                                                                                                                                        |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| privacy.md:63 "Supabase … EU region of the project"                                                    | Committed link state points the project at aws eu-west-2 (London). The live project could not be queried.                                                               | Processor location differs from the documented EU. This needs an operator check before launch. | `supabase-region-london-not-eu`                                                                                                            |
| privacy.md:73-74 recognition "only on-device … the audio does not leave the phone"                     | iOS drops the on-device flag for any locale other than the phone's, so Apple server recognition is used                                                                 | A privacy claim made for minors does not hold for bilingual use                                | `p2-ios-on-device-flag-dropped-for-other-locale`                                                                                           |
| privacy.md:34 photos kept "7 days after reading … immediately when the learner deletes"                | The tick recovery sweep fails materials with no purge job. A photo that arrives after the purge is orphaned. A single not-material page is kept 7 days.                 | Some photos are kept indefinitely                                                              | `recovery-failed-material-never-purged`, `delete-during-upload-orphans-photos`, `p2-page-not-material-photo-kept-7-days`                   |
| privacy.md:37 transcript and questions kept "until the material … is deleted"; UI says "Blatt löschen" | `archiveMaterial` only sets `archived_at`. The rows remain and appear in the export.                                                                                    | A "deleted" sheet persists until account deletion                                              | `p2-ml-material-delete-is-soft-only`                                                                                                       |
| privacy.md:31 memories kept "until corrected/removed"                                                  | A removed memory is only `status='retracted'`.
