### 9.5 Status of the model.md contradiction candidates

All 21 candidates were checked. None was refuted outright; each maps to a verified finding.

| Candidate                            | Outcome                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Undo offered only when it works      | Holds as a contradiction: `thread-undo-without-undoapplies`                                                                                                                                        |
| set_contact reduce-only              | Holds, but narrowed: 0 is reachable only through a raw PATCH (`set-contact-fewer-raises-cap-from-zero`). The undo path is a second loosening route (`p2-undo-of-set-contact-loosens-without-pin`). |
| All contact counts                   | Holds: `p2-in-app-contact-not-counted`                                                                                                                                                             |
| Practice resumability copy           | Folded into the homework and step findings (`homework-sheet-practice-dead-end`, `all-answered-unfinished-session-invisible`)                                                                       |
| Homework help entry points           | Holds: `homework-sheet-practice-dead-end`                                                                                                                                                          |
| Agreed reminders wait in the app     | Holds: `agreed-reminder-vanishes`                                                                                                                                                                  |
| "opened" as the only proof           | Holds: `notification-tap-cold-start`, `opened-proof-not-durable`, `previous-unanswered-needs-push-tap`                                                                                             |
| Voice mode in the chat               | Holds: `repro-23` (UX, §6)                                                                                                                                                                         |
| Turn failure semantics               | Holds: `non-model-error-leaves-message-processing`                                                                                                                                                 |
| Context fence completeness           | Holds for time zone (`timezone-header-no-context-bump`) and for practice answers (`answers-do-not-bump-context`, partly)                                                                           |
| Photo retention                      | Holds: see 9.1                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| material_ready wake-up               | Holds: `merged-part-no-wake`                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Two definitions of secure/shaky      | Holds: `summary-vs-home-secure-shaky-disagree`                                                                                                                                                     |
| PIN lockout                          | Holds: `put-pin-no-lockout`, `admin-session-lockout-lost-update`                                                                                                                                   |
| Adult gate for login credentials     | Narrowed to MEDIUM: `repro-07` / `account-change-gated-client-only` (see §12.2)                                                                                                                    |
| Turn cost limits                     | Holds: `turn-cost-and-stall-window`                                                                                                                                                                |
| Delivery status wording              | Holds: `ticket-vs-receipt-same-wording`                                                                                                                                                            |
| `abandoned` / `response` dead states | Holds: `stale-active-sessions-never-abandoned`                                                                                                                                                     |
| On-device speech guarantee           | Holds, and is stronger than suspected: `p2-ios-on-device-flag-dropped-for-other-locale`                                                                                                            |
| Onboarding freshness window          | Holds: `onboarding-pin-dead-end` (= `repro-10`)                                                                                                                                                    |
| Web build dev-only vs app.json       | Holds only as a documentation contradiction, not as a defect (`web-tokens-localstorage`, partly)                                                                                                   |

**Pattern.** Most contradictions are not wrong documentation. The documents describe the intended
invariant correctly, and one code path escapes it. That path is usually a fallback: `in_app`
instead of push, the recovery sweep instead of the normal job, undo instead of apply, the PIN
change route instead of the admin session. Each invariant is enforced on the main route and missed
on a side route. A fix that goes route by route will keep reproducing this pattern. Each invariant
needs one choke point: one PIN-check helper, one contact-counting query that includes `in_app`,
and one "material failed" function that always schedules a purge and bumps context.

---

## 10. State, Concurrency & Lifecycle Risks

The items are grouped by mechanism. Confidence uses the brief's wording.

### 10.1 Context fence gaps (CLAUDE.md rule 4)

**Mechanism.** `applyDecision` locks `buddy_settings FOR UPDATE` and rejects if
`context_version` changed (`apply.ts:70,82`). The fence protects only against writes that call
`bumpContext`.

**How it fails.**

- `requireLearner` upserts `buddy_settings.timezone` from `x-timezone` on every request with no bump
  (`http/context.ts:72-83`, verified). A decision planned in one zone is then resolved in another,
  giving an off-by-one due date. Any device on the account changes the zone.
  `timezone-header-no-context-bump` (= `repro-21`), `device-timezone-overwrites-learner-zone`,
  `p2-F-journey-budget-day-set-by-client-zone`.
  Confirmed (runtime-reproduced).
- Answers, hints and reveals update `session_items` and FSRS state with no bump. Only session
  start, flag and finish bump. `answers-do-not-bump-context`. Partly confirmed: the missing bump is
  confirmed; the impact is limited because finishing a session does bump.
- The tick recovery sweep fails materials with no bump (`tick.ts:66-71`, verified).
  `recovery-failed-material-never-purged`. Confirmed (runtime-reproduced).
- The fence works but is circumvented by time: `p2-turn-day-offsets-anchored-to-now-resolved-at-message-time`
  (a resend on a later day resolves `in_days` from the original date). Countdown text is baked at
  plan time and delivered a day later: `p2-NEW-countdown-relative-day-baked-at-plan-time`.
  Confirmed (code-traced).

**Over-fencing, the opposite failure.** A fence that trips on unrelated writes costs a model call.
`set_level` bumps `learners.version`, so a language change fails with "stale"
(`p2-set-level-version-bump-409-on-language-change`). A second learner message bumps context and
the first message's decision goes stale, so split utterances fail (`quote-checked-against-trigger-only`
= `repro-22`). The fence therefore has both false negatives (time zone, answers) and false
positives (unrelated learner writes).

### 10.2 Lock ordering and deadlocks

**Mechanism.** Apply locks `buddy_settings` and then steps and goals. Taps (skip/outcome) and
`PATCH /buddy/memory` lock the row first and then bump settings.

**How it fails.** Postgres detects the deadlock after `deadlock_timeout`. The turn is the victim
after its reply has already been streamed. The SSE stream sends `error internal`, and the message
stays "processing" until stall recovery re-runs the model.
`apply-vs-tap-lock-order-deadlock` (= `repro-01`; `pg_stat_database.deadlocks` 0 → 1),
`p2-J-memory-F4`. Confirmed (runtime-reproduced) for taps; Confirmed (code-traced) for memory.

**Fix direction.** Use one lock order everywhere (settings first), or have tap routes take the
settings lock before the row.

### 10.3 Non-atomic read-modify-write and missing idempotency

| Mechanism                                                                                                       | How it fails                                                            | Ids                                                                                                                                                                         | Confidence                     |
| --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------ |
| PIN failure counter read in `requireAccount`, written back as an absolute value (`routes.ts:229-231`, verified) | 40 parallel wrong PINs → counter 2, no lock                             | `admin-session-lockout-lost-update`                                                                                                                                         | Confirmed (runtime-reproduced) |
| `createMaterial` idempotency is lookup-then-insert                                                              | A concurrent duplicate gets a 500 instead of the same row               | `create-material-idempotency-race`                                                                                                                                          | Confirmed (runtime-reproduced) |
| `nextSeq` for practice turns is locked per item, not per session                                                | Concurrent answers on two items collide with a 500 (12/12)              | `turn-seq-collision-500`                                                                                                                                                    | Confirmed (runtime-reproduced) |
| No client request id on `POST /practice/sessions`, hint taps, or a retyped answer after a 5xx                   | A retry after a lost response creates a second session, hint or attempt | `manual-start-not-idempotent`, `hint-not-idempotent-from-app`, `p2-F6`, `outbox-flush-vs-resumed-screen-double-attempt`, `p2-offline-answer-invisible-after-leaving-screen` | Confirmed (code-traced)        |
| step_due dedupe key uses the version the handler bumps                                                          | A rerun inserts a second agreed reminder                                | `agreed-reminder-duplicate-on-rerun`                                                                                                                                        | Confirmed (runtime-reproduced) |
| Undo of a forget skips dedupe and the 60-item cap                                                               | Duplicate memories; STATE drops the newest ones                         | `p2-J-memory-F7`                                                                                                                                                            | Confirmed (code-traced)        |

### 10.4 Job leases, fencing and parking (scheduler)

**Mechanism.** Jobs are claimed with a lease. Recovery requeues an expired lease. After 3 attempts
a job is parked as `failed` (`jobs.ts:65-78`). Most workers do not re-check their lease before
writing.

**How it fails.**

- The 150 s check lease (`check.ts:44`, verified) is shorter than a slow check. The recovered worker
  and the original both apply, which gives two contacts and duplicate actions.
  `check-lease-shorter-than-runtime` (= `repro-17`). Confirmed (runtime-reproduced).
- Status writes are not fenced by lease: `extraction-status-writes-unfenced` (a ready sheet flips to
  failed), `p2-delivery-status-writes-unfenced` (a `send_uncertain` row is overwritten and sent).
  Confirmed (runtime-reproduced) and Confirmed (code-traced) respectively.
- **Parking with no follow-up** is the most consequential lifecycle defect in the scheduler. A
  parked job has no owner:
  - account deletion: `deletion-job-parks-failed-account-stuck` (HIGH, privacy)
  - extraction: the recovery sweep fails the material but does not schedule a purge
    (`recovery-failed-material-never-purged`)
  - countdown check: `failed-countdown-never-fires-no-fallback`
  - learner-triggered checks: stale three times, then dropped
    (`p2-check-stale-exhaustion-drops-reaction`)
  - `send-uncertain-lease-no-thread`: the lease-expired sending row gets no thread copy.
- The health signal hides all of this. `/health` reads only `last_finished_at`, and a tick whose
  every section failed still reports OK (`health-ok-despite-failing-tick`,
  `p2-NEW-dead-scheduler-strands-work-and-privacy-promises`). Nothing reads `last_error` or counts
  parked jobs.

**Fix direction.** Every job kind needs a defined terminal state that is visible to someone (thread
copy, settings state, operator metric). Every write from a job worker should be conditioned on
`claim_token`.

### 10.5 Turn lifecycle (chat)

- A non-model exception outside the model try leaves the message "processing" for about 3 minutes.
  Stall recovery then re-claims it with no attempt cap and re-bills the model:
  `non-model-error-leaves-message-processing`, `p2-J-stall-recovery-loop-drains-budget`,
  `p2-poisoned-turn-strands-learner-jobs-and-loops`, `p2-J-stuck-processing-not-closed`.
  Confirmed (runtime-reproduced) for the first; the others Partly confirmed (the loop is traced;
  whether a deterministic failure repeats forever was not run).
- A successful reply marks every earlier failed message done, including ones the model never saw:
  `old-failed-messages-marked-done-unseen`. Confirmed (runtime-reproduced).
- An outreach posted during a turn makes the retry's dialogue end with a model-role turn:
  `trailing-model-turn-after-outreach`. Needs runtime verification: run the retry against real
  Gemini and check whether it is accepted.
- The streamed reply is shown and spoken before zod validation, and then withdrawn: `repro-28`,
  `p2-safety-verdict-after-speech`, `streamed-vs-stored-reply-reread`. Confirmed (runtime-reproduced).

### 10.6 Material, draft and session lifecycle

| Mechanism                                                                                                         | How it fails                                                                                                                    | Ids                                                                                                                                                               | Confidence                                                                                          |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Server abandons `awaiting_upload` after 24 h; the client keeps the draft 7 days with the same `client_request_id` | Resume loops on 404                                                                                                             | `draft-resume-dead-end` (= `repro-12`), `orphan-awaiting-upload-rows`                                                                                             | Confirmed (runtime-reproduced)                                                                      |
| Delete checks `archived_at` only before the model call                                                            | An archived sheet becomes ready with live items and a Buddy wake-up                                                             | `delete-during-extraction-ready` (= `repro-13`)                                                                                                                   | Confirmed (runtime-reproduced)                                                                      |
| Purge is a one-shot job and uploads outlive it                                                                    | Photos are orphaned in storage                                                                                                  | `delete-during-upload-orphans-photos`                                                                                                                             | Partly confirmed (the mechanism holds; the timing window was not measured on hosted storage)        |
| Archiving a material does not reach sessions, steps or merged parts                                               | Deleted questions are still asked; the homework session stays active; prepared cards show old counts; the part notice dead-ends | `p2-xs-deleted-questions-still-asked-in-active-sessions`, `p2-HW-05`, `p2-stale-prepared-step-after-material-delete`, `root-delete-leaves-part-notice`, `p2-J-03` | Confirmed (runtime-reproduced) for `p2-HW-05`, `root-delete-leaves-part-notice`; others code-traced |
| A retry is offered after the photo purge                                                                          | Fails with a false "photos never arrived"                                                                                       | `retry-after-purge-false-claim`                                                                                                                                   | Confirmed (runtime-reproduced)                                                                      |
| No code sets `abandoned`; auto-finish is not retried                                                              | Sessions and steps stay `in_progress` forever; an all-answered session disappears                                               | `stale-active-sessions-never-abandoned`, `all-answered-unfinished-session-invisible`                                                                              | Confirmed (runtime-reproduced)                                                                      |
| Client: a fresh capture discards the draft that is still uploading                                                | The in-flight send loses its photos                                                                                             | `p2-lc-fresh-capture-kills-inflight-send`                                                                                                                         | Confirmed (code-traced)                                                                             |

**Cross-cut.** "Delete" is implemented as `archived_at` on one row. It does not cascade to
dependent live state (sessions, steps, parts, jobs in flight). The privacy side of the same
decision is in §12.3.

### 10.7 Outreach lifecycle

Planning and delivery are separated in time, but the message body and eligibility are fixed at
planning time:

- the relative day label is baked in (`p2-NEW-countdown-relative-day-baked-at-plan-time`)
- tightened rules are not re-checked at send time (`p2-send-time-recheck-ignores-tightened-rules`)
- after an outage, overdue rows planned for different days are all sent in one tick
  (`p2-NEW-post-outage-delivery-ignores-contact-rules`)
- a moved agreed step keeps its old job (`moved-agreed-step-keeps-old-reminder-job`)
- a later `prepare_practice` cancels an agreed prepared step
  (`p2-prepare-practice-cancels-reminder-prepared-step`)
- a pause while a reminder is deferred cancels it with no thread copy (`agreed-reminder-vanishes`
  = `repro-15`).

Presence is account-wide and triggered by polling, so any device keeps deferring checks
(`p2-presence-is-account-wide`, `p2-lc-idle-client-defers-buddy-checks`).
Confidence: `repro-15` Confirmed (runtime-reproduced); the others Confirmed (code-traced) unless
marked partly in §6.

### 10.8 Auth, session and sign-out lifecycle (client)

**Mechanism.** One handler for a null session runs whenever the session becomes null
(`_layout.tsx:46-53`). It clears the outbox, drafts and query cache. It was written for the
"shared phone, next person" case, but many causes other than an intentional sign-out lead to it.

**Causes that end in a wipe or a false sign-out:**

- a 5xx or 429 during refresh is treated as invalid (`refresh-5xx-signs-out-and-wipes`, HIGH)
- an Auth outage becomes a 401 on the API (`p2-api-verifier-outage-becomes-401`, HIGH)
- a password change on another device (`p2-reset-logs-out-childs-device-and-wipes-queue`, partly)
- global sign-out scope from a second device (`signout-global-scope-other-devices`)
- a process kill during rotation (`p2-refresh-interrupted-by-kill-loses-session-and-drafts`)
- web tabs sharing a rotated token (`p2-lc-web-tabs-stale-refresh-token`)
- an unreachable auth host, ending in "Bitte melde dich neu an" (`refresh-network-failure-says-sign-in-again`).

Even an intentional sign-out gives no warning before discarding unsent work
(`p2-signout-wipes-unsent-answers-and-photos-without-warning`).

**Remnants of the previous user after sign-out:**

- the push token stays active offline (`p2-push-device-binding-first-device-forever` = `repro-20`, MEDIUM because push is off by default, runtime-reproduced: the next person
  receives the previous learner's pushes)
- the refresh token is not revoked after a cold start (`signout-no-server-revoke`)
- the old Buddy screen stays in the stack (`signout-leaves-old-buddy-in-stack`)
- a late `setHome` repopulates the cleared cache (`p2-lc-late-sethome-after-signout`)
- a PIN-card sign-in in flight re-saves the session (`p2-signout-resurrected-by-inflight-pin-signin`)
- a recovery user switch keeps the previous user's drafts (`recovery-user-switch-keeps-drafts`).

**Fix direction.** Split "session invalid" (confirmed 400/401 from the auth server) from
"session unknown" (network, 5xx, 429). Keep the local queue when the same `user_id` signs back in.
Make sign-out an explicit sequence with a timeout: flush or warn, unregister push, revoke, then
wipe. `p2-signout-offline-ios-may-hang-on-apns` shows the missing timeout (Needs runtime
verification: sign out on an offline iOS device with push configured).

### 10.9 App kill, background and device lifecycle

- A notification cold start never records "opened" because `getLastNotificationResponse` is never
  read. That keeps the 48 h unanswered gate closed:
  `notification-tap-cold-start` (= `repro-19`), `opened-proof-not-durable`,
  `previous-unanswered-needs-push-tap`. Needs runtime verification on a device; the native replay
  semantics were traced in the expo-notifications source.
- Android activity recreation loses the camera result (`p2-lc-android-camera-kill-loses-photo`).
  The composer draft is not persisted (`p2-J-composer-draft-not-persisted`).
- Outbox: head-of-line blocking up to 7 days (`outbox-head-of-line-blocking`), a joined flush
  strands entries (`p2-outbox-flush-join-strands-entries`), and there is no resend while the device
  counts as online but has no data (`p2-no-resend-while-device-counts-online`). Web quota:
  `p2-web-sent-photos-crowd-out-outbox`, `web-oversize-draft-stale`.
- Voice and recording tasks without an owner: `handsfree-recording-outlives-turn` (partly: the
  server-path recording is confirmed; the double-attempt effect is inferred),
  `stale-recording-answers-new-question`, `p2-lc-chat-mic-survives-leaving-home`,
  `tts-reads-feedback-after-leaving`, `p2-home-voice-reads-late-reply-on-other-screens`,
  `composer-parked-transcript`.
- No client timeouts on streaming, voice, `/finish` or photo send: `no-client-timeout-voice`,
  `streaming-send-no-timeout-locks-composer`, `p2-F9`, `p2-no-cancel-during-send`,
  `speak-offline-wait-no-cancel`.

### 10.10 Client cache races

`home-setqueries-race-with-poll` (no `cancelQueries` before `setQueryData`, runtime-reproduced
flicker), `history-merge-gap-under-polling` (= `repro-25`, a message vanishes), `p2-J-memory-F5`,
`p2-pin-saved-then-stale-gate-says-no-pin`. These are low impact. The shared root is optimistic
`setQueryData` against a list that is polled or windowed.

---

## 11. Agent / Execution / Tooling Risks

### 11.1 CLAUDE.md hard rules 1-5 against the code

| Rule                                                                                    | Where it holds                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | Where it is violated or thin                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | Ids                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **1. Model interprets, code enforces**                                                  | Tool permissions, tenant scoping through aliases, time resolution and the context fence are in `tools.ts`, `apply.ts` and `policy.ts`. Contact loosening for minors is PIN-gated in the settings route.                              | These rules are **prompt-only**: lock-screen text free of scores and personal details (`prompts.ts:67`, verified; template bodies also carry exam titles and counts); "never the homework solution" in chat (`prompts.ts:46`); reply language (`prompts.ts:26`); "never claim a change you didn't make" (`prompts.ts:16`). Code-level gaps in enforcement: contact caps are void for `in_app`; `set_contact fewer` 0 → 1; undo loosens without the PIN; the homework leak check is skipped on "correct"; the quote provenance check is a ≥2-character substring (`text.ts:21-25`). | `p2-model-outreach-lock-screen-unchecked`, `lock-screen-text-carries-title-and-count`, `p2-sec-homework-solution-chat-unenforced`, `p2-T5`, `p2-T6`, `p2-T4`, `p2-in-app-contact-not-counted`, `set-contact-fewer-raises-cap-from-zero`, `p2-undo-of-set-contact-loosens-without-pin`, `p2-sec-homework-leak-check-skipped-on-correct`, `p2-HW-01`, `p2-sec-quote-provenance-two-chars` |
| **2. Model never writes ids, dates or instants; clock-change ambiguities are rejected** | Aliases throughout. `plan_step`/`update_step` resolve model-given times with `'reject'` (`tools.ts:534,620`, verified).                                                                                                              | `schedule_check` resolves a model-given `a.time` through `zonedToInstant` with `'compatible'` (`tools.ts:805`, `time.ts:195-199`, verified), so it guesses instead of rejecting. `in_days` offsets are anchored to the send date but resolved at message time. The time zone can change under a decision.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | `schedule-check-compatible-dst`, `p2-turn-day-offsets-anchored-to-now-resolved-at-message-time`, `timezone-header-no-context-bump`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| **3. No word lists as fake language understanding**                                     | No keyword lists found in production code (grep over `apps/api/src` excluding tests and testing). Decisions come from zod-validated output: `TurnDecision.safeParse` (`turn.ts:256`) and `CheckDecision.safeParse` (`check.ts:467`). | Borderline, not violations: `choiceNamed`'s prefix rule picks the first option named even if it is negated (`p2-NM-07`). The diacritic "close" reply is a fixed string (`p2-NM-08`). Superseded ADR 0002 still mandates a give-up word list (`legacy-adrs-and-setup-vertex-not-superseded`); the code correctly does not follow it.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | `p2-NM-07`, `p2-NM-08`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| **4. Every decision applied atomically behind the context fence**                       | `applyDecision` runs one transaction with `FOR UPDATE` and the version check (`apply.ts:70-82`, verified).                                                                                                                           | The time-zone write and practice answers do not bump (10.1). A check that outlives its lease is applied twice (`check-lease-shorter-than-runtime`). The deadlock with taps aborts apply (10.2). Background actions from checks are applied but never shown or undoable.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | `timezone-header-no-context-bump`, `answers-do-not-bump-context`, `check-lease-shorter-than-runtime`, `apply-vs-tap-lock-order-deadlock`, `p2-background-actions-invisible-no-undo`                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| **5. Never claim what isn't proven**                                                    | Delivery status comes from tickets and receipts; `send_uncertain` is never resent.                                                                                                                                                   | Ticket and receipt use the same copy. "Nicht angekommen" is shown for stored messages. "opened" is lost on cold start. Streamed text is spoken before validation. Thread action lines are frozen "✓" snapshots. The storage gateway maps outages to "photos missing". A retry after purge claims "never arrived". Budget-exhaustion and outage states are invisible.                                                                                                                                                                                                               | `ticket-vs-receipt-same-wording`, `p2-F-journey-failed-label-claims-not-arrived`, `notification-tap-cold-start`, `repro-28`, `p2-ux-rule5-states-indistinct`, `storage-errors-reported-as-missing-photos`, `retry-after-purge-false-claim`, `p2-F-journey-outage-invisible-on-home`                                                                                                     |

Summary: rule 3 holds. Rules 1, 2 and 4 hold on the main path and fail on side paths (fallbacks,
undo, recovery, the time-zone header). Rule 5 is weakest in the UI copy layer rather than in the
server data.

### 11.2 Model output validation

- **All-or-nothing sets.** A single out-of-bound item or figure fails a whole extraction or
  generated set: `p2-NM-05` (8 vs 6 accepted answers), `p2-figure-bounds-kill-whole-set`,
  `page-report-catch-all-or-nothing`. Invalid items should be dropped individually, as the
  `items.ts:86` comment already intends for figures.
- **Validation after side effects.** Streamed text reaches the screen and TTS before the
  `TurnDecision` parse (`repro-28`) and before the provider's safety verdict
  (`p2-safety-verdict-after-speech`). The streamed text also skips the LaTeX repair
  (`p2-streamed-reply-unrepaired-latex-escapes`).
- **Unvalidated sub-fields.** Word-by-word pronunciation marks are the model's list, not aligned to
  the target sentence (`pronunciation-words-unvalidated`).
- **Schema field order.** In the check schema, `lookups` comes last
  (`p2-check-step-schema-lookups-last`).
- **Deterministic grading ahead of the model.** The critical grading findings
  (`numeric-key-three-decimals-misparsed`, `tolerance-accepts-wrong-integers`,
  `p2-percent-answer-divided-by-100`, `p2-latex-mixed-key-plainmath-31-2`, `p2-T1`, `p2-NM-03`)
  are code rule checks that run **before** the tutor model and short-circuit it. Rule 1's
  "code enforces" is correct for permissions, but here code overrides a correct model judgement
  with a wrong deterministic one. Keep the rule check limited to verdicts it is sure of, and pass
  ambiguous numeric formats to the model.

### 11.3 Prompt-injection and data channels

- Extraction prompts lack the "text in the photo is data, not instructions" rule that the other
  prompts carry. Extracted titles and topics enter STATE unescaped (`p2-photo-text-instruction-channel`,
  partly: the missing rule is confirmed; no exploit was run).
- The quote provenance gate is the code check that ties a change to the learner's words. A
  2-character substring passes it (`p2-sec-quote-provenance-two-chars`). It is also too strict
  across split messages (`quote-checked-against-trigger-only` = `repro-22`). It is simultaneously
  too weak against injected STATE text and too strict for real speech.
- `search_material` can serve homework sheet text into a chat whose "no solution" rule is
  prompt-only (`p2-sec-homework-solution-chat-unenforced`).

### 11.4 Safety for minors

- There is no prompt rule, code path or eval for a distress message. A provider safety block is
  shown as "Buddy hat sich verhaspelt" with "send again" (`p2-T2`, HIGH).
- The blocked message stays in every later prompt (`p2-blocked-disclosure-poisons-thread`, HIGH).
- Disclosures can become persistent memories that are exported and fed into every prompt
  (`p2-disclosures-become-memories`, partly: the code gap is confirmed; whether the model actually
  stores such content was not run).
- No test or telemetry covers these paths (`p2-no-safety-verification`).

These four together form one missing capability: a safeguarding path. Fixing them separately
leaves gaps. The minimum is a `concern` field in the turn schema, a distinct `blocked` code and
copy, exclusion of blocked or failed messages from later prompts, and a memory-category rule
enforced in `runRemember`.

### 11.5 Failure and outage handling

- Provider 4xx is classified as retryable (`provider-4xx-classified-retryable`). Storage errors are
  classified as "absent" (`storage-errors-reported-as-missing-photos`). Budget refusals count as
  runs (`budget-refusals-consume-retry-runs`). Timeouts keep their reservations
  (`timeouts-consume-budget`). The `buddy_check` budget counts calls, not checks
  (`p2-check-budget-exhausted-by-normal-day`), so promised check-ins end silently
  (`p2-F-journey-checkin-promise-dropped`).
- Outage visibility: `p2-F-journey-outage-invisible-on-home`, `p2-ux-model-outage-inconsistent`,
  `health-ok-despite-failing-tick`.
- Error classification is too coarse at every seam: Auth (outage → 401), Storage (error → absent),
  Vertex (4xx → unavailable), safety (block → invalid). Each seam needs at least four outcomes: ok,
  definitive refusal, transient, unknown.

### 11.6 Eval and test coverage of agent behaviour

Six of eight eval scripts cannot fail (`evals-not-machine-gradable`). The dev stack's scripted FIFO
is global and most background checks run unscripted into the fallback
(`devstack-shared-fifo`, `dev-stack-scripted-fifo-invisible-failures`). The prompt-only rules in
11.1 have no eval (`p2-T4`, `p2-T5`, `p2-T6`). Model-behaviour guarantees are therefore currently
unverified by the repository's own tooling.

---

## 12. Security & Privacy Findings

### 12.1 PIN brute force (HIGH): `put-pin-no-lockout` + `admin-session-lockout-lost-update`

The following is treated as one HIGH finding with two legs. `put-pin-no-lockout` merges `repro-08`
and `pin-change-without-lockout`. `admin-session-lockout-lost-update` merges `repro-09`.

- **Severity:** HIGH. This was reported as critical in the first pass and in one second-opinion run.
  It is set to HIGH per the editorial decision: the attacker must already hold the account's device
  session, and the 4-digit PIN protects the adult area, not the account itself.
- **Affected user flow:** the parents' PIN gate for a minor. It protects export, deletion,
  loosening contact and changing the PIN.
- **Location:** `apps/api/src/modules/identity/routes.ts:188-215` (PUT `/account/pin`: `verifyPin`
  at :197, no read of `pin_locked_until`, no increment, and :211 resets the counter and lock on
  success); `routes.ts:217-241` (POST `/account/admin-session`: :229 computes
  `account.pin_failed_count + 1` from the row loaded by `requireAccount`, and :231 writes the
  absolute value). All lines verified at HEAD.
- **Current behavior:** Wrong `current_pin` values on PUT return 403 indefinitely with the counter
  staying at 0. PUT with the correct PIN succeeds even while admin-session is locked (423), and
  then clears the lock. 40 concurrent wrong admin-session attempts leave `pin_failed_count` at 2.
- **Expected behavior:** privacy.md:17-19 promises 5 wrong attempts lock for 15 minutes, on every
  path that accepts the PIN as proof.
- **Problem:** The lockout exists on one of two PIN-verifying routes, and on that route it is a
  lost-update counter.
- **User impact:** Someone holding the child's (the account's) session can find the parents' PIN.
  The space is at most 10 000 values, limited only by scrypt cost. They can then set a new PIN and
  gain the full adult area.
- **Concrete scenario:** A script loops `PUT /account/pin {current_pin: n, pin: '1234'}`. When the
  response is 200, the PIN is replaced and `POST /account/admin-session` with the new PIN returns
  an admin token (reproduced end-to-end on real Postgres).
- **Root cause:** PIN verification is duplicated per route instead of going through one guarded
  helper. The counter is computed in the application from a stale read.
- **Recommended fix:** Create one `checkPin(accountId, pin)` helper. It rejects while locked,
  increments atomically on failure
  (`update … set pin_failed_count = pin_failed_count + 1, pin_locked_until = case when … end returning`),
  and resets only on success. Use it in both routes. Do not let PUT clear an active lock. Add
  integration tests for the PUT lock, PUT during a lock (423) and concurrent lockout.
- **Blast radius:** Every minor profile with a PIN. The fix is limited to `identity/routes.ts`
  plus tests.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced). Two independent runs agreed on 20 wrong PUTs
  (counter 0), PUT succeeding during a lock, and 40 parallel attempts counting as 2.

Related, not part of the HIGH item:

- `p2-sec-pin-lockout-no-escalation`: even when implemented correctly, 5 per 15 minutes allows
  about 480 guesses per day, with no escalation and no trivial-PIN rejection. Partly confirmed:
  this matches the documented design, so it is a design weakness, not a deviation.
- `p2-double-tap-eltern-fragen-stacks-pin-pads-and-leaks-admin-token`, `pin-modal-cancel-race`,
  `admin-token-clock-skew-loop`: client paths that leave a live 10-minute admin token behind.

### 12.2 `repro-07` narrowed to MEDIUM (merged with `account-change-gated-client-only`)

**Original claim (critical).** A child with the device session changes the parents' password
through Supabase `updateUser`, signs in freshly, and `PUT /account/pin` accepts a new PIN without
`current_pin`. That would be a full takeover.

**What holds.**

- `routes.ts:192-199` deliberately accepts a new PIN without the current one when the JWT `amr`
  shows a non-refresh authentication less than 300 s old (`fresh` at :195, verified). This is the
  intended "forgot PIN" path. It is covered by the repo's own test
  (`identity.int.test.ts:143-158`) and documented in `PinCard.tsx`'s header.
- Supabase password change needs no current password or nonce. `config.toml` sets neither, and the
  app has no `reauthenticate()` flow. The adult check before the password sheet is client-only
  (`AccountAccessCard.tsx` → `confirmAdult`).
- Any fresh sign-in method counts, including the e-mail recovery link (`amr` `otp`/`recovery`).

**Why it is narrowed.**

- A child cannot reach the chain inside the app. For a minor's profile, the password and e-mail
  sheet opens only after the PIN screen, and PinCard asks for the current PIN or the account
  password.
- Session tokens are stored in Keychain/Keystore on native builds. localStorage is used only in
  the web build, which is not deployed.
- Calling `updateUser` directly therefore needs extracted tokens (a rooted device or dev tooling),
  the parents' password, or the parents' mailbox. The design already treats the password and the
  mailbox as adult proof.

**Remaining risk (MEDIUM).** Defence in depth is missing. A leaked device session is enough to
rotate the account password, and then (through the intended path) the PIN. The server does not
distinguish a password sign-in from an e-mail-link sign-in as adult proof.

**Fix.**

- Enable "Require current password when changing password" on the hosted project, record it
  (config.toml cannot carry it), and send `current_password` from the password sheet.
- Optionally accept only `amr.method === 'password'` as proof for overwriting an existing PIN.

**Confidence:** Partly confirmed. The server rule and the missing Supabase reauth are confirmed at
runtime. The claimed in-app reachability by a child is refuted by the client gate and the token
storage.

### 12.3 Deletion, retention and export against privacy.md

Details are in the 9.1 table. The cross-cutting view:

1. **"Delete" means archive, everywhere below account level.** Sheets, questions and memories are
   all flagged, never removed. They keep flowing into the export and, for memories, into later
   prompts until account deletion (`p2-ml-material-delete-is-soft-only` HIGH,
   `p2-J-memory-F3` MEDIUM, `p2-J-memory-F2`). The fix is either a real delete with a hard-delete
   job, or a privacy.md change and UI copy that say "hidden". The current state satisfies neither.
2. **Account deletion has three independent failure modes, and none is observable:**
   - parking after 3 attempts (`deletion-job-parks-failed-account-stuck` HIGH, runtime)
   - one storage call with every historical path, which fails past about 1000 objects where the
     limit is enforced (`p2-sec-deletion-over-1000-photos-never-completes` HIGH; the test fake has
     no limit, so the passing test does not cover this)
   - the cascade through unindexed foreign keys (`p2-account-deletion-cascade-unindexed-fks`,
     16 s at about 300k rows).

   Also, cancelling while the job runs returns 200 but the account is still deleted
   (`cancel-vs-execute-race`), and `privacy.ts:128` `assertNotDeleting` is unused
   (`deletion-job-parks-failed-account-stuck` evidence). Verified: `executeAccountDeletion`
   (`privacy.ts:102-126`) runs storage remove, then `deleteUser`, then the cascade, with no chunking
   and no idempotent resume.

3. **Photo retention** leaks through the recovery sweep, a delete during upload, and single
   not-material pages (9.1). On the device, copies remain in the cache and in backups
   (`p2-photo-pipeline-leaves-copies-in-cache`, `p2-prepared-copies-leak-on-early-exit`,
   `p2-child-data-in-device-backups`, `p2-recording-file-left-on-unmount`,
   `p2-orphan-recording-file-after-kill`). privacy.md:35 covers only the draft copy.
4. **The erasure path is gated on having a learner profile** (`no-erasure-without-learner`), and
   the export is incomplete (`export-omits-llm-calls-and-jobs`).

### 12.4 Region and processors

- `supabase-region-london-not-eu` (HIGH): committed link state shows London. Confirmed
  (runtime-reproduced) as a repository fact. The live project region needs an operator check in
  the Supabase dashboard.
- `p2-ios-on-device-flag-dropped-for-other-locale` (HIGH): iOS audio goes to Apple servers for any
  non-device locale. Confirmed (code-traced). Needs runtime verification on a device to observe the
  network path.
- `database-tls-not-enforced` (MEDIUM, partly): no `ssl` option in `lib/db.ts`. TLS depends on an
  undocumented `sslmode` in the secret.
- Expo push adds a US subprocessor and is documented as off by default (a declared non-goal). Once
  it is enabled, lock-screen text is protected by prompt only
  (`p2-model-outreach-lock-screen-unchecked`), and template bodies carry the exam title and counts
  (`lock-screen-text-carries-title-and-count`, partly). This contradicts CLAUDE.md "Lock-screen
  texts carry no scores or personal details".

### 12.5 Minors, consent and the PIN gate lifecycle

- Consent: a child can re-consent for the account holder after a version bump
  (`child-can-reconsent-for-account-holder` MEDIUM, runtime). The server never enforces the
  version (`p2-ml-consent-version-not-enforced-server-side`). `CONSENT_VERSION` is bound to no text
  (`consent-version-names-no-text`).
- Age transitions: at 16 a child profile silently becomes its own account holder
  (`p2-sec-pin-gate-vanishes-at-16-for-child-profile`). Creating a 16- or 17-year-old child fails
  with a 500 (`child-16-17-profile-500`). The self-profile age check teaches the bypass
  (`p2-self-profile-age-gate-teaches-bypass`). App and API disagree on the date at midnight
  (`age-local-vs-utc`).
- Contact loosening without the PIN: undo (`p2-undo-of-set-contact-loosens-without-pin`, runtime)
  and `set_contact` from 0 (`set-contact-fewer-raises-cap-from-zero`). The home "Eltern fragen"
  path skips the `pin_set` pre-check (`home-optin-without-pin`).
- The PIN pad names no action, and the deletion confirmation is taken before the PIN
  (`p2-parents-pin-pad-names-no-action`).
- Safeguarding: see 11.4 (`p2-T2`, `p2-blocked-disclosure-poisons-thread`,
  `p2-disclosures-become-memories`).

### 12.6 Session, tokens and auth links

- Push token: `p2-push-device-binding-first-device-forever` (= `repro-20`, MEDIUM, runtime-reproduced). After an offline sign-out the token row stays
  `active` for the previous learner, and the next person on the phone receives that learner's
  Buddy pushes (exam and reminder texts). The fix is to deactivate tokens server-side when a
  different learner registers or signs in on the same device, and to retry the unregister call.
  The push is off by default, which limits exposure today.
- Sign-out does not revoke the refresh token after a cold start (`signout-no-server-revoke`), and
  can sign out other devices (`signout-global-scope-other-devices`).
- Recovery links:
  - the implicit flow returns tokens to the custom scheme (`p2-sec-recovery-redirect-custom-scheme`)
  - arbitrary tokens in a reset link silently switch the account (`p2-sec-recovery-link-login-csrf`,
    partly)
  - the recovery session shares the client with app refresh (`p2-lc-recovery-session-swapped-by-app-refresh`)
  - leaving an invalid link signs out the app session (`p2-used-link-retap-revokes-fresh-session`)
  - there is no `token_hash` template, so mail scanners can consume links (`p2-mail-scanner-consumes-link`)
  - the sign-up redirect is hard-coded (`signup-redirect-hardcoded-scheme`).

  Together these show the auth-link flow is built on the implicit flow and custom schemes. PKCE
  with verified app links is the structural fix.

- `deleted-user-401-proven-by-fake`: "old token no longer works after deletion" is asserted only
  against FakeAuth. The server never revokes; a Supabase JWT stays valid until expiry. The claim is
  unproven for the real stack.
- Scoping: `reply-to-id-not-scoped` (the `reply_to_id` insert in `turn.ts:82` is not
  learner-scoped). Low, but it breaks the "every query scoped by learner" statement
  (privacy.md:46-48).

### 12.7 Infrastructure exposure

- `p2-sec-anon-rpc-lb-invoke-tick` (MEDIUM, runtime): anyone with the app's anon key can trigger a
  full tick (up to 300 s on the host). This contradicts privacy.md:49-50. The fix is a new
  migration that revokes execute from `anon` and `authenticated`. Migrations are immutable (rule
  10), so `0001` cannot be edited.
- `mathjs-evaluate-unbounded-input` (HIGH, resources): learner text reaches the mathjs evaluator,
  and a range expression allocates without bound. It is listed here because it is reachable by any
  signed-in learner and can take down an API instance.
- `vercel-deploy-config-invalid` (HIGH): the first deploy fails. It is listed for completeness;
  its effect is availability, not confidentiality.
- No rate limiting exists at any layer, according to the second-opinion search of `app.ts` and
  `vercel.json`. That is what makes 12.1 and the tick RPC cheap to abuse.

### 12.8 Where privacy.md holds

Checks that passed and should not be reworked:

- RLS is enabled with no policies on tables.
- The model is scoped through aliases.
- Voice recordings are not stored server-side.
- The `vertex` region guard runs at startup.
- Photos use signed URLs under the account path.
- The 7-day hold and cancel exist.
- `llm_calls` holds no content.

The defects above are gaps at the edges of these mechanisms, not absent mechanisms.

---

## 13. Product UX Failure Scenarios

The scenarios are narrated from the person's point of view. Each chains verified findings into one failure the person actually experiences. The personas are illustrative; the mechanics are the cited findings. Each scenario is judged against CLAUDE.md: simplicity (rule 16), a gentle tone, no counts of missed days or due items (rule 6), and never claiming what isn't proven (rule 5).

### S1. Evening homework, morning nothing

- **Setup:** Lena, 12, grade 6, German. She photographs six fraction tasks at 19:30 via 'Hausaufgabe' and solves two with hints. She goes to bed with the session open.
- **What happens:** At 07:40 the resume card is gone, because it is keyed to extraction time + 12 h. 'Mein Stoff' shows the sheet with 'Üben', which answers 'Dafür gibt es noch keine Aufgaben. Fotografier zuerst ein Arbeitsblatt.' She asks Buddy to continue. STATE only says 'Mathe: 1 sheets, 6 questions … active 2/6' and no tool can open the session, so Buddy asks for a photo.
- **What she sees and feels:** The app tells her something false ('photograph a sheet first': she did). She re-photographs and starts at task 1 before school, late and annoyed. Her two solved tasks are invisible.
- **Findings:** `homework-sheet-practice-dead-end` (runtime: `repro-05`), `p2-HW-06`, `stale-active-sessions-never-abandoned`.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced).

### S2. Stuck on task 1 means homework over

- **Setup:** Same session. Task 1 is a word problem she does not understand after four hints.
- **What happens:** Help mode has no skip, no next, no 'Tipp' button and no flag. The only other button is 'Beenden'. She taps it to get to task 2. The session is finished for good, although the end copy said her answers stay saved.
- **What she sees and feels:** 'Hausaufgabe geschafft' on a summary for work she did not do. Tasks 2–6 are unreachable. The calm promise of 'Tipps, die Lösung findest du selbst' ends in a trap.
- **Findings:** `p2-HW-03`, `beenden-finishes-for-good`, `homework-no-hint-button`.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced).

### S3. Right answer, 'Fast' forever, and then the solution anyway

- **Setup:** The extractor stored the key for task 3 as `$\frac{7}{8}$`. Lena writes '0,875'.
- **What happens:** The tutor says 'Genau, 0,875 ist richtig!'. The server demotes the verdict because '0.875' does not contain '7/8'. The task stays open under a 'Fast' tag and she cannot skip. On task 4 she writes 'Hauptnenner 12'. The tutor over-praises and adds '… = 11/12'. The leak check ran before the demotion, so that reply is stored and shown.
- **What she sees and feels:** She is told she is right and wrong at once, and is stuck. On the next task she is handed the answer she was supposed to find herself. Her mother, who chose the homework mode for exactly that promise, sees it broken.
- **Findings:** `p2-HW-02`, `p2-HW-03`, `p2-HW-01`, `p2-sec-homework-leak-check-skipped-on-correct`.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced).

### S4. The sign error that becomes 'Stimmt – gut gemacht!'

- **Setup:** Jonas, 14, grade 8, practises from his photographed algebra sheet a week before a test. 'Löse 2x + 10 = 0' has the key `$x=-5$`.
- **What happens:** He types 'x=5'. `plainMath` + `normalizeShortAnswer` turn both into 'x 5', and the rule says correct without asking the model. FSRS schedules the item days ahead. The summary lists the topic under 'Sitzt'. Buddy's STATE calls it secure and the exam prep never revisits it. The same happens to 'a-b' vs 'a+b', and to German spelling items where only capitalisation, ß/ss or punctuation is wrong ('das fahrrad' for 'das Fahrrad', 'Strasse' for 'Straße').
- **What he sees and feels:** Praise and confidence, then a bad grade on the exact rule he got wrong. From the family's view Buddy 'said he knew it'.
- **Findings:** `p2-F1`, `p2-NM-01`, `p2-T1`.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced).

### S5. '3 1/2' is right three times and still gets the worked solution

- **Setup:** Lena answers numeric items. The key is 3.5, and in a second set it is 0.125.
- **What happens:** '3 1/2', her teacher's notation, is read as 31/2 = 15.5. '0,125' is read as 125. A rule 'incorrect' is final (`tutor.ts:128`), so she gets hint 1, hint 2, then 'Schau, so geht's' and the item closes as revealed with FSRS Again. Conversely, '242' for 240 is accepted because of the 1 % tolerance.
- **What she sees and feels:** 'Noch nicht ganz' three times for a right answer. The gentle copy makes it worse, because she concludes she does not understand fractions. The summary lists the topic as shaky, and Buddy drills it again.
- **Findings:** `p2-F2`, `numeric-key-three-decimals-misparsed` (runtime: `repro-03`), `tolerance-accepts-wrong-integers` (runtime: `repro-04`), `p2-latex-mixed-key-plainmath-31-2`.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced).

### S6. The outage evening that turns correct answers into 'did not know'

- **Setup:** Vertex is down for an evening. Lena practises 'Photosynthese' from a prepared step and, two days before her vocab test, starts the Probetest.
- **What happens:** Free-text answers get 'Das kann ich gerade nicht prüfen. Du kannst dir die Lösung ansehen …', and the field is cleared. She taps 'Lösung zeigen' as told, and the item is stored as skipped with FSRS Again. In the Probetest no solution button exists, so every article answer ('der Hund') loops until she taps 'Überspringen'. Nothing ever re-checks these answers.
- **What she sees and feels:** She was right, but the result card says 'Schauen wir nochmal an: Photosynthese', and half the vocab list is 'Übersprungen' although she typed every word.
- **Findings:** `p2-F-journey-cannot-check-steers-to-reveal`, `p2-F3`, `p2-F4b`.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced) for the reveal path. The per-mode copy is Confirmed (code-traced).

### S7. 'Nicht angekommen' all evening

- **Setup:** Lena used Buddy heavily; `buddy_turn` (80 reservations, charged per lookup round) is used up by 19:00. Alternatively, a Vertex incident is running.
- **What happens:** Her message shows 'Für heute kann Buddy nicht mehr antworten' for 4.5 s. After that only a red 'Nicht angekommen' + 'Nochmal senden' remains. The home banner is absent because `system.model` reflects configuration only.
- **What she and her mother see:** A network problem. Lena taps resend six times, and her mother restarts the router. The message had arrived: the label is a false claim (rule 5), and 'tomorrow' is said nowhere that lasts.
- **Findings:** `p2-J-failed-label-not-arrived`, `p2-F-journey-failed-label-claims-not-arrived`, `p2-F-journey-outage-invisible-on-home`, `p2-J-stall-recovery-loop-drains-budget`.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (code-traced). The home-status part is Partly confirmed: configuration-only status holds; how often the budget is actually exhausted needs usage data.

### S8. Seven careful minutes, and the PIN is never set

- **Setup:** Sabine, parent, signs in at 19:02. She reads the privacy points, checks Lena's birth year and picks a PIN Lena won't guess. She taps 'Los geht's' at 19:09.
- **What happens:** POST /learner succeeds, then PUT /account/pin returns 403 `reauth_required`, because the freshness clock started at sign-in. The toast asks her to confirm with her password, but the screen has no password field and no sign-out. Tapping again gives 409 'Das hat sich gerade geändert'. She force-quits. The app opens on Lena's home as if all went well.
- **What she sees and feels:** An error she cannot act on. Later, 'Eltern fragen' only says no PIN exists. The recovery path (Settings › Für Eltern › PIN festlegen › password) is nowhere suggested. A parent setting up a 16-year-old as 'Mein Kind' instead gets a plain 500.
- **Findings:** `onboarding-pin-dead-end` (runtime: `repro-10`), `trapped-at-consent-or-profile`, `home-optin-without-pin`, `child-16-17-profile-500` (runtime: `repro-11`).
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced).

### S9. A two-minute auth incident deletes the photos from the bus

- **Setup:** Lena photographed three worksheet pages on the bus without signal. The draft is kept. At home, Supabase Auth returns 503 for two minutes.
- **What happens:** The API verifier turns the failure into 401. The app refreshes, and auth-js retries for about 30 s ('Einen Moment …') and then returns a 503. `reasonOf` → 'unknown' → `clearSession` → `_layout` clears the outbox and deletes the draft photos → welcome screen.
- **What she sees and feels:** The app suddenly asks for an e-mail and password she does not know. The photos and her queued answers are gone without a word. The incident ended two minutes later.
- **Findings:** `p2-api-verifier-outage-becomes-401`, `refresh-5xx-signs-out-and-wipes` (runtime: `repro-26`), `p2-signout-wipes-unsent-answers-and-photos-without-warning`.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced) for the refresh wipe. Verifier → 401 is Confirmed (runtime-reproduced) at the API.

### S10. The photo taken 'the wrong way'

- **Setup:** Buddy asks for the worksheet for Friday's maths test. The home shows 'Schick mir ein Foto' with a small card button. Lena uses the camera in the composer, the control she always uses.
- **What happens:** The material carries no step or goal id. It is read and practice is prepared, but the capture step stays planned. Next morning 'Schick mir ein Foto' is back. She photographs the same sheet again, now through the card: two 'Brüche' sheets, duplicate questions in practice.
- **What she sees and feels:** Buddy seems not to have noticed her photo. The simple 'take a photo' interaction (rule 16) fails because only one of four camera entries counts.
- **Findings:** `p2-ml-photo-not-linked-to-goal-or-capture-step`, `p2-reading-result-silent-when-contact-off`.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced).

### S11. English reminder, maths questions

- **Setup:** Lena writes 'erinner mich morgen um 16 Uhr an Englisch Vokabeln'. Her library contains only a maths sheet.
- **What happens:** `plan_step` has no subject. At 16:00 the deterministic reminder prepares items from any subject: 'Wie verabredet: Englisch Vokabeln. 6 Aufgaben liegen bereit'. 'Jetzt üben' opens fraction questions. If her father had meanwhile widened quiet hours to cover 16:00, the reminder would instead have been suppressed with no in-app copy.
- **What she sees and feels:** Buddy 'doesn't listen'. Nothing on the home showed the reminder was set in the first place.
- **Findings:** `p2-reminder-practice-ignores-step-subject`, `p2-planned-reminder-invisible-on-home`, `agreed-reminder-vanishes` (runtime: `repro-15`).
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced) for the subject mix-up and the dropped reminder. The invisible plan is Confirmed (code-traced).

### S12. 'Nicht am Wochenende', and Saturday Buddy writes anyway

- **Setup:** Default deployment (push off, everything in-app). Buddy already planned 'Deine Übung liegt bereit' for Saturday 15:00. On Friday Lena writes 'schreib mir am Wochenende nicht' and later 'schreib mir weniger'.
- **What happens:** The card confirms new rules but does not mention the weekend. The scheduled Saturday row is never re-checked, because send time checks only on/off, pause and quiet hours. 'Weniger' halves a weekly cap that never counts in-app rows.
- **What she sees and feels:** Her explicit request changed nothing. That is the one guarantee rule 6 makes ('Buddy can only reduce it').
- **Findings:** `p2-send-time-recheck-ignores-tightened-rules`, `p2-set-contact-card-omits-avoided-weekdays`, `p2-in-app-contact-not-counted` (runtime: `repro-02`).
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced) for the uncounted in-app contact. The send-time re-check is Confirmed (code-traced).

### S13. Papa's 'no' is undone and re-asked within a minute

- **Setup:** Lena's father switches contact off in settings. Separately, her mother had Lena tell Buddy 'nicht nach 19 Uhr'.
- **What happens:** Back on the home, the opt-in card reappears at once: 'Darf ich dir aufs Handy schreiben? … [Eltern fragen]'. That evening Lena taps 'Rückgängig' on the 19:00 card. Quiet hours return to 20:00 with no PIN, although the same change in settings is PIN-gated for minors.
- **What the parents see and feel:** Their decisions do not stick, and the child is prompted to lobby them. This contradicts `privacy.md` ('anything that increases contact needs the PIN').
- **Findings:** `p2-stop-contact-immediately-reasks-optin-on-home`, `p2-undo-of-set-contact-loosens-without-pin`.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced).

### S14. A hard message that Buddy cannot answer

- **Setup:** Lena, 12, writes to Buddy that classmates are bullying her and she does not want to go to school.
- **What happens:** No prompt rule, code path or eval covers distress. If Gemini's filter blocks the prompt or the answer, the turn fails as `model_invalid`. She sees 'Buddy hat sich verhaspelt. Schick's nochmal.' and 'Nicht angekommen'. The failed message stays in every later prompt, so the following turns can fail the same way. If nothing is blocked, the model improvises with no guidance.
- **What she sees and feels:** After the hardest thing she has said to the app, she is told to send it again and that it did not arrive. No kind words, and no pointer to a trusted adult or a helpline. This is the opposite of 'never harsh'.
- **Findings:** `p2-T2`, `p2-blocked-disclosure-poisons-thread`, `p2-no-safety-verification`.
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (runtime-reproduced) for the blocked-turn handling and the missing path. Whether Vertex blocks a given disclosure needs runtime verification against the live safety settings (`apps/api/src/llm/vertex.ts:34-51`).

### S15. The PIN that approved a deletion nobody meant

- **Setup:** Lena, bored, opens 'Für Eltern', taps 'Konto löschen …' and the red 'Ja, in 7 Tagen löschen'.
- **What happens:** The PIN pad only says 'PIN der Eltern – Das dürfen nur deine Eltern erlauben. Gib ihnen kurz das Handy.' Her father types the PIN. A 2-second toast says 'Die Löschung ist geplant.' Nothing changes for seven days. On day 8 the phone shows the welcome screen. Sign-in says 'E-Mail oder Passwort stimmen nicht' and no screen explains why. If the deletion job had failed three times, the settings would instead keep saying 'geplant ab <past date>' forever.
- **What the father sees and feels:** Every conversation, memory and worksheet is gone, or never goes, and he was never told what he confirmed.
- **Findings:** `p2-parents-pin-pad-names-no-action`, `p2-deletion-executed-no-explanation-on-device`, `deletion-job-parks-failed-account-stuck` (runtime: `repro-24`).
- **Confidence:** Confirmed (code-traced) for the blind PIN and the silent sign-out. The parked deletion is Confirmed (runtime-reproduced).

**Common thread:** in almost every scenario the person is not hit by a crash. They are hit by a calm, friendly UI saying something untrue ('noch keine Aufgaben', 'Nicht angekommen', 'Stimmt', 'Buddy vergisst das', 'geplant'). The fix pattern is the same everywhere: code-backed state (rules 1 and 5) delivered through Buddy's chat or the existing card, never a new dashboard, list or counter (rules 6 and 16).

---

## 14. Unfinished / Suspicious Implementation

### 14.1 Fresh grep (repo HEAD 62a6028)

Scope: `apps/`, `packages/`, `infra/`, excluding `node_modules`, `.expo`, `dist`, `test-results` and the gitignored web build `apps/mobile/dist-web/` (`.gitignore:63`; it is not tracked, and its hits come from bundled vendor code).

| Pattern                                               | Hits          | Notes                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| ----------------------------------------------------- | ------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `TODO` / `FIXME` / `XXX` / `HACK`                     | 0 / 0 / 0 / 0 |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `notImplemented` / "not implemented"                  | 0 / 0         | The only 3 "not implemented" hits are vendor code in the ignored `dist-web` bundle                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| "kommt später"                                        | 0             | Every "später"/"later" hit in the locales and modules is legitimate copy or a comment (e.g. `errors.model_unavailable`, `now.ready_later`)                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `@ts-ignore` / `@ts-expect-error` / `@ts-nocheck`     | 0 / 0 / 0     |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `eslint-disable`                                      | 0             |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `as unknown as`                                       | 1             | `apps/mobile/lib/api/streamingFetch.ts:6`. This bridges `expo/fetch`'s Response type to the DOM `Response`. It is a narrow, documented cast at a library seam and is acceptable.                                                                                                                                       |
| `any` as a type (`: any`, `as any`, `<any>`, `any[]`) | 0             | The one regex hit, `apps/mobile/lib/math/typed.ts:412` `worth: any`, is a boolean variable named `any` and not the type                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Imports of `src/testing/` from production code        | 0             | `server.ts`, `production.ts` and `api/[[...slug]].ts` import only `DisabledGateway`/`VertexGateway`, `DisabledPush`/`ExpoPush`, `SupabaseStorage` and `SupabaseAuthVerifier`. No fake, scripted gateway or dev stack is reachable from a production entry point (rule 11 holds).                                       |
| Banner `requires live verification`                   | 18 files      | 17 are test files or the test harness (`src/__tests__/*`, `testing/database.ts`, `fakes.ts`, `harness.ts`). One is production code: `apps/mobile/lib/speech/recognize.ts:6`. That file carries the banner because its behaviour is unverified on a device (see §17, `p2-ios-on-device-flag-dropped-for-other-locale`). |

The hygiene markers are clean. The repo has no stubs, suppressions or `any`. What is unfinished shows up as dead wiring and half-implemented features, listed below, not as markers.
