### 14.3 Fakes and test-only paths

- **The dev stack diverges from production** (`dev-stack-cors-and-clock-differ-from-production`, `dev-stack-wall-clock-hides-time-driven-ui`, `dev-stack-scripted-fifo-invisible-failures`). The browser walkthrough runs against permissive CORS, the wall clock and one FIFO script queue, and unscripted model calls silently take the fallback. Nothing reaches production from here. The problem is that the green walkthrough proves less than it appears to. Confirmed (code-traced).
- **`scripted-gateway-skips-schema`**: Partly confirmed. The downstream zod parse catches script/schema mismatches, so the risk is low.
- **Fake adapters cannot fail the way the real ones do** (`apps/api/src/testing/fakes.ts:135-196`):
  - `MemoryStorage.remove/download/existing` never throw and have no request cap.
  - `FakeAuth.verify` can only return a user or `null` and has no outage mode.
  - `FakeAuth.deleteUser` never fails.

  This is why `deletion-job-parks-failed-account-stuck`, `p2-sec-deletion-over-1000-photos-never-completes`, `storage-errors-reported-as-missing-photos` and `p2-api-verifier-outage-becomes-401` were not caught. It is also why `deleted-user-401-proven-by-fake` asserts FakeAuth's own bookkeeping rather than the system's. See §15.3.

### 14.4 Behavioural dead ends (rule 12 "buttons without a handler" in spirit)

No button lacks an `onPress`. Several CTAs lead nowhere or answer with a false message. The evidence is in §4–5:

- `homework-sheet-practice-dead-end` (merged with `repro-05`): "Üben" on a homework sheet.
- `draft-resume-dead-end`: "Nochmal senden" loops.
- `onboarding-pin-dead-end` (merged with `repro-10`): the CTA answers 409.
- `trapped-at-consent-or-profile`: no exit before a profile exists.
- `p2-HW-03`: help mode has no way past an open task.
- `beenden-finishes-for-good`: the copy promises answers are kept.

---

## 15. Missing Tests

Suite at HEAD:

- 14 API integration files (`apps/api/src/__tests__/*.int.test.ts`, real Postgres).
- 12 API unit files.
- 3 shared-math unit files.
- 22 mobile vitest files, `lib/**` only (`apps/mobile/vitest.config.ts:10`; screens and components are "not under test here yet").
- 5 Playwright specs (`tests/web/*.spec.ts`) plus `fit.ts`.

CI (`.github/workflows/check.yml:45-54`) runs install, typecheck, lint and test only. The walkthrough and the fit check are never gated (`walkthrough-and-fit-never-gated`). The pre-commit hook passes with every integration test skipped when no Postgres is up (`precommit-green-without-postgres`). Neither `fast-check` nor any other property-testing library is a dependency.

### 15.1 Critical findings (grading)

All seven critical findings are in the deterministic rule check, where a `rule` verdict is final and no model sees the answer. They share one missing artefact: a **grading truth table**, a table-driven unit test over `ruleCheck(item, answer, locale)` with rows _(kind, key, typed/spoken answer, locale) → expected verdict_. Rows for correct answers must yield `correct` or `unknown`, never `incorrect`. Rows for wrong answers must yield `incorrect` or `unknown`, never `correct`. It belongs in `apps/api/src/modules/practice/__tests__/evaluate.test.ts`, with parser-level rows in `packages/shared-math/src/__tests__/numeric-input.test.ts`. One integration case per family in `modes.int.test.ts` (test mode, zero tutor calls) should prove the verdict reaches the stored item and FSRS.

| Finding                                                         | Test that would have caught it                                                                                                                                                                         | Existing test that passes wrongly or misses it                                                                                                                                                                                                | Where the new test belongs                                                                                                               |
| --------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `numeric-key-three-decimals-misparsed` (merged with `repro-03`) | Key `0.125`, typed `0,125`, locale `de` (and `fr/es/it`) → `correct`. Also key `2.375` with `2,375`, and key `1.250` with `1,25`.                                                                      | `numeric-input.test.ts:10-13` pins `'1.000'` (de) → 1000. That is right for _learner input_, but no test parses a _stored key_, which is always point-decimal. The shared parser therefore reads `0.125` as 125 while every test stays green. | `evaluate.test.ts` (truth table), plus an integration case in `modes.int.test.ts` asserting `evaluated_by='rule'` does not mark it wrong |
| `tolerance-accepts-wrong-integers` (merged with `repro-04`)     | Key `240`, typed `242` → not `correct`. Key `1000`, typed `999` → not `correct`. Key `0.5`, typed `0.51` → not `correct`.                                                                              | None covers `closeEnough` (`evaluate.ts:80`). `evaluate.test.ts:55-59` tests `differentNumber` only on values far apart (`3/7` vs `3/4`).                                                                                                     | `evaluate.test.ts`, plus a test-mode integration case asserting `first_try_correct=false`                                                |
| `p2-F1` (operator/sign-blind exact match)                       | Key `x^2+2x`, answer `x^2-2x` → not `correct`. Key `x=-5`, answer `x=5` → not `correct`. Key `3.4`, answer `3,4` for a non-numeric kind → handled deliberately.                                        | `evaluate.test.ts:21` ("typed 3/4 and a stored \frac{3}{4} are the same") asserts only the positive case, so the normaliser's operator stripping is never exercised negatively.                                                               | `evaluate.test.ts`                                                                                                                       |
| `p2-F2` (`3 1/2` → 15.5, `25 %` → 0.25)                         | Parser: `parseNumericInput('3 1/2','de')` is 3.5 or flagged as residue, never 15.5. `parseNumericInput('25 %','de')` keeps 25 plus a unit `%`, or is flagged.                                          | `evaluate.test.ts:67` asserts `differentNumber('3,5','3 1/2') === false`, which only covers the "leave it to the model" branch, while `ruleCheck` on the numeric path still returns `incorrect`. **Structural gap for this family:** the grading tests are hand-picked examples of what the author intended. There is no property test. See §15.3.1.

### 15.3 Structural gaps

1. **No property tests for numeric parsing across locales.** For all locales in {de, fr, es, it, en} and generated values v (integers; 1–4 decimals; fractions; mixed numbers; percentages; with or without a unit):
   - Formatting v the way a learner in that locale writes it, then running `ruleCheck` against the canonical key, must give `correct`.
   - Formatting v' ≠ v (off by one unit in the last place, sign flipped, operator changed) must never give `correct`.

   Add `fast-check` to `packages/shared-math` and `apps/api` dev dependencies. This single test would have found the three-decimal, tolerance, mixed-number, percent, en-comma and sign/operator findings (all 7 criticals and 3 highs).

2. **Fakes have no failure modes.** `MemoryStorage` and `FakeAuth` (`apps/api/src/testing/fakes.ts:135-196`) cannot throw, time out, rate-limit or cap request size, while `ScriptedGateway` and `FakePush` can. Add `failNext(op, error)` and limits to both. That unlocks the deletion-limbo, storage-outage, verifier-outage and >1000-photo tests.
3. **Concurrency is tested in one place only**: overlapping ticks (`delivery.int.test.ts:356`). There are no committed tests for:
   - parallel wrong PINs (`admin-session-lockout-lost-update`);
   - the apply-vs-tap lock order (`repro-01`, `apply-vs-tap-lock-order-deadlock`, `p2-J-memory-F4`);
   - a check outliving its lease (`repro-17`);
   - delete-during-extraction (`repro-13`);
   - cancel-vs-execute deletion (`cancel-vs-execute-race`);
   - the zone change mid-turn (`repro-21`).

   All of these were reproduced with temporary tests and then deleted. The reproductions should be committed as regression tests.

4. **Clock changes are well covered at unit level** (`apps/api/src/lib/__tests__/time.test.ts:54-96`: spring-forward gap, fall-back ambiguity, New York, Lord Howe). The gap is end-to-end: no integration test drives the harness `TestClock` across a DST transition for quiet hours, agreed reminders or exam countdown jobs. The dev stack uses the wall clock (`dev-stack-wall-clock-hides-time-driven-ui`), so no browser test sees time-driven states.
5. **Mobile has no screen or component tests** (`apps/mobile/vitest.config.ts:3-10`), and no tests for `lib/auth/supabase.ts` refresh, `lib/push.ts`, `lib/capture/put.ts` or the storage adapters (`no-native-path-coverage`, `push-registration-and-opened-untested-on-app-side`). Every mobile-side HIGH (refresh wipe, trapped at consent, parent handover, onboarding dead end, y-labels, on-device flag) falls in this gap.
6. **Gates**:
   - The walkthrough and the 390×844/360×740 fit check are not in CI (`walkthrough-and-fit-never-gated`), although CLAUDE.md rule 16 relies on `tests/web/fit.ts`.
   - The pre-commit hook is green without Postgres (`precommit-green-without-postgres`); `pnpm -F @learnbuddy/api test:db` exists but is not used by the hook.
   - The streaming test reads the body after completion (`stream-test-buffered`, `stream.int.test.ts:49`), so it cannot detect buffering.
   - The pg_cron → pg_net → `/internal/tick` path has no smoke test (`tick-trigger-path-untested`).
7. **Safety and model behaviour**: no eval or test covers distress, indirect injection or safety blocks (`p2-no-safety-verification`, `p2-T8`). The live-model evals under `apps/api/evals/` are not run in CI, which is appropriate, but no release checklist ties them to a deploy.

---

---

## 16. Remediation Plan

This plan groups the findings from §4–6 into work packages. Each package lists the ids it closes, the change, the test that proves the fix, what it depends on, and a rough effort (S ≤ 2 days, M ≤ 1 week, L > 1 week for one developer who knows the codebase). Ids follow the merges in the BRIEF, and a runtime twin is named only where it adds a test. File paths were checked against HEAD `62a6028`. Line numbers are left out where §4–6 already give them.

Ground rules that apply to every package (CLAUDE.md):

- **Tests.** Each fix comes with an integration test on real Postgres (`apps/api/src/__tests__/*.int.test.ts`) that covers the failure path, not only the happy path. Only the model, push, auth and storage are faked. A file that injects a fault carries the banner `// requires live verification in Claude Code session`. Pure parser and normaliser rules also get unit tests next to the code.
- **Migrations.** `0001`–`0011` are immutable. New numbers start at `0012` and are assigned in merge order. The numbers below are the proposed order, not fixed names.
- **Division of labour.** Grading, contact, time and permission rules live in code. The model may add structured, zod-validated fields (for example "this item is rounded"). It never writes ids, dates or instants, and no fix may introduce word lists.
- **Docs.** Each package updates the doc section it changes (`docs/architecture.md` §Practice/§Proactivity/§Delivery/§Testing, `docs/privacy.md`) in the same change.

Summary:

| Tier            | Packages | Covers                                                                |
| --------------- | -------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 16.1 Immediate  | 11       | 7 critical, 36 of 37 high, and 11 mediums that belong to the same fix |
| 16.2 Near-term  | 12       | H-36 and the medium clusters                                          |
| 16.3 Structural | 9        | Root-cause changes that stop these classes of bugs from coming back   |

Coverage: every critical, high and medium id in `final-rest.json` is covered by an I- or N-package (S-packages list what they prevent, not new assignments), except three that are not defects of their own. The composite narratives `p2-ux-narrative-iphone-bus`, `p2-ux-narrative-outage-day` and `p2-ux-narrative-probetest` retell findings that are already assigned. Low findings appear only where they share a fix with a listed item.

---

### 16.1 Immediate (before any real learner uses it)

This tier covers the critical findings and every high finding that grades children wrongly, loses their work, breaks a privacy or erasure promise, removes a parental control, or blocks a core journey (homework, practice, photo, onboarding, deploy). `p2-function-plot-y-labels-offscreen` (H-36) is the only high finding moved to 16.2, because it is cosmetic and has a workaround.

#### I-1 Numeric grading: parse keys canonically; the rule check returns `unknown` when unsure

- **Covers:** C-1 `numeric-key-three-decimals-misparsed` (`repro-03`), C-2 `tolerance-accepts-wrong-integers` (`repro-04`), C-3 `p2-F2`, C-4 `p2-percent-answer-divided-by-100`, H-1 `p2-T1`, H-2 `p2-en-comma-three-decimals-answer-misgraded`, H-6 `mathjs-evaluate-unbounded-input`.
- **Change:**
  - `packages/shared-math/src/numeric-input.ts`:
    - Split learner-input parsing from key parsing. Add `parseCanonicalKey()`, which accepts only point-decimal input, is locale-independent, and understands a mixed number and a unit.
    - In learner input, treat a single separator followed by exactly three digits as ambiguous in every locale (`value: null`) unless it is unambiguous from context (for example `0,125`, which has a leading zero).
    - Recognise `^-?\d+\s+\d+/\d+` as a mixed number before any whitespace is removed.
    - Replace `mathjs.evaluate` with `parse` plus a node whitelist (Constant, Operator, Parenthesis, `sqrt`, `abs`, `pi`). Cap numeric input at about 64 characters.
    - Set `has_residue` truthfully, so that "the learner typed an operator" is visible.
  - `packages/shared-math/src/units.ts`: add `%` (and `‰`) as a unit, so that `stripTrailingUnit` removes it instead of passing it to mathjs. Localised spoken forms ("Prozent", "percent", "pour cent", "por ciento", "per cento") are normalised to `%` in `apps/mobile/lib/speech/spoken.ts`, which already maps spoken units to symbols. This is a unit-token mapping, not language understanding.
  - `apps/api/src/modules/practice/evaluate.ts`:
    - Parse `item.answer` with `parseCanonicalKey`, and also check `accepted_answers` in the numeric branch.
    - Replace `closeEnough` with an exact comparison. An integer key must match exactly. A decimal key allows half a unit in its last written place until I-1b/S-1 add an explicit tolerance.
    - If the learner text contains an operator the key does not, return `unknown`, so the tutor judges.
    - `differentNumber` uses the same comparison.
  - Nothing else changes in `service.ts`. A rule `unknown` already reaches the tutor.
- **Proof:**
  - A grading truth table in `apps/api/src/modules/practice/__tests__/evaluate.test.ts`. Rows are (kind, key, answer, locale) → verdict, with rows for de/fr/es/it/en:
    - `0.125`/`0,125` → correct; `0.125`/`125` → not correct; `240`/`242` → not correct;
    - `3.5`/`3 1/2` → correct; `25`+`%`/`25 %` → correct; `391`/`17·23` → unknown;
    - `0.125`/`0,125` in `en` → not incorrect.
  - Parser rows go in `packages/shared-math/src/__tests__/numeric-input.test.ts`, including a timeout case for `1:1e8`.
  - An integration case per family in `apps/api/src/__tests__/modes.int.test.ts` (test mode, scripted tutor). It asserts the stored `session_items` verdict, `item_states` and the test summary, including that a correct answer is never `missed` with zero tutor calls.
- **Depends on:** nothing. Do this first. It also needs the tolerance decision (§16.5, D-1).
- **Effort:** M.

#### I-2 String and math exact match: keep operators, signs, case, ß and punctuation

- **Covers:** C-5 `p2-latex-mixed-key-plainmath-31-2`, C-6 `p2-F1`, C-7 `p2-NM-01`, H-3 `p2-NM-02`, H-4 `p2-NM-03`, H-5 `p2-NM-04`, and `p2-NM-07` (medium, prefix rule on choices).
- **Change:**
  - `apps/api/src/modules/practice/evaluate.ts`:
    - `plainMath` inserts a separator between a whole number and `\frac`, the same way `valuesIn` already does.
    - The `correct` comparison uses a math-preserving canonical form for any key or answer containing digits or operators. It keeps `+ − = < > ≤ ≥ / ^ ·` and the decimal separator, and normalises the Unicode minus and superscripts. Otherwise it uses trim, whitespace collapse and NFC only.
    - `normalizeShortAnswer` (`packages/shared-math/src/normalize.ts`) is no longer the correctness oracle. It is used only to recognise a near miss and return `typo`/`unknown`.
    - `missing_word` and `typo` are skipped when the key contains digits, operators or `=`. A typo reply that prints the key sets `revealed = true` (`service.ts`).
    - `choiceNamed` returns `null` when a letter maps to two indices, and when more text follows the named option. The tutor then decides, so no negation list is introduced (rule 3).
- **Proof:** negative rows in the same truth table:
  - `x^2+2x`/`x^2-2x`, `x=-5`/`x=5`, `\frac{3}{4}`/`3,4`, `ß`/`ss`, `L`/`l` and a missing comma → never `correct`;
  - `$3\frac{1}{2}$`/`7/2` → correct or unknown, never incorrect; `$3\frac{1}{2}$`/`31/2` → never correct;
  - `x = 5`/`5` → not `missing_word`; `14:30`/`14:50` → not `typo`;
  - choices `['the','a','an']` with spoken "A" → unknown.

  Add one `modes.int.test.ts` case for the `3,4` → "Kürzen" secure-topic chain.

- **Depends on:** I-1 (shared truth table), and D-2 (spelling strictness).
- **Effort:** M.

#### I-3 Homework help: re-entry, pause instead of finish, closing and leak order

- **Covers:** H-7 `homework-sheet-practice-dead-end` (`repro-05`), H-8 `beenden-finishes-for-good`, H-9 `p2-HW-01`, H-10 `p2-HW-02`, H-11 `p2-HW-03`, plus the mediums `p2-sec-homework-leak-check-skipped-on-correct` (`p2-T3`), `p2-HW-05`, `test-beenden-untouched-as-missed` and `stale-active-sessions-never-abandoned`.
- **Change:**
  - API, `apps/api/src/modules/practice/service.ts`:
    - Compute `homeworkSolved` and the demoted verdict first, then run `givesAwayHomework` (`tutor.ts`) on the final verdict and the final reply.
    - Leak detection treats the child's own listed candidates as hers, not as task text.
    - `homeworkSolved` compares values through the I-1 comparison and checks `accepted_answers`.
    - Add an item state `deferred` for help mode ("Später"), which moves the open pointer without solving or revealing anything.
    - `finish` on a help session with open tasks becomes a pause.
  - API, `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/home.ts`: the resume card is keyed on last activity, not on `started_at` + 12 h, for mode `help`. `GET /materials/:id` exposes the help session.
  - API, `archiveMaterial` (`materials/service.ts`) closes the help session in the same transaction (`p2-HW-05`).
  - Add a stale-session sweep in `scheduler/tick.ts` that abandons sessions idle for N days and returns their step to `prepared`.
  - App, `apps/mobile/components/library/MaterialCard.tsx`: for `purpose=homework`, show "Weiter mit der Hausaufgabe", which uses `MaterialView.session_id`.
  - App, `apps/mobile/app/practice/[id].tsx`: `close()` navigates back without finishing while items are open. The help-mode "Später" is a `<Btn>`.
  - App: fix `end_hint` in all 5 locales.
  - App: test review shows untouched items as "nicht bearbeitet", not as "Noch nicht richtig".
- **Proof:** `modes.int.test.ts` cases:
  - help session, TestClock +13 h → still reachable from `GET /buddy` and the material;
  - "Beenden" with 3 open tasks → session active;
  - a scripted tutor `correct` on an intermediate step with a reply containing `= 11/12` → the reply is replaced;
  - key `\frac{3}{4}`, answer `0,75`, tutor correct → the task closes;
  - "Später" on task 1 → task 2 shown, task 1 still open;
  - delete the sheet → the help session is closed.

  Add `tests/web/modes.spec.ts` coverage for the "Später" and "Beenden" paths.

- **Depends on:** I-1 (value comparison) and D-5 (Beenden semantics).
- **Effort:** L.

#### I-4 Practice and material lifecycle: nothing answered or photographed gets lost

- **Covers:** H-12 `all-answered-unfinished-session-invisible`, H-13 `draft-resume-dead-end` (`repro-12`), H-14 `p2-NM-05`, H-15 `p2-figure-bounds-kill-whole-set`, H-16 `p2-ml-photo-not-linked-to-goal-or-capture-step`.
- **Change:**
  - Finish the session on the server inside the answer transaction when the last item closes (`practice/service.ts`). The app's automatic finish becomes a no-op on repeat.
  - `materials/service.ts` `client_request_id` lookup excludes archived or abandoned rows, or answers with a distinct code that makes the app rotate the id (`apps/mobile/lib/capture/draft.ts`). Align the draft max age with the 24 h abandon window.
  - `practice/items.ts`: parse items one by one (`safeParse` per item), with `accepted_answers` clipped to the prompt's limit and `Figure.nullable().catch(null)`. Align the prompt numbers in `materials/extract.ts` and `practice/generate.ts` with the schema.
  - `createMaterial` attaches the single open capture step and its goal on the server when the app sends none, so every camera entry point (`apps/mobile/app/buddy.tsx`, `library.tsx`) works.
- **Proof:**
  - `modes.int.test.ts`: answer every item, drop `/finish` → session finished and result card shown.
  - `learning.int.test.ts`: create, then +25 h, then resend with the same id → a new material, never an endless 404.
  - `learning.int.test.ts`: a scripted extraction with one item carrying 8 accepted answers and one 8-column table → the other items are saved.
  - `material-pages.int.test.ts`: a composer photo while a capture step is open → the step is done and the items are goal-linked.
- **Depends on:** none.
- **Effort:** M.

#### I-5 PIN and adult gate: one atomic lockout, a short-lived admin token, credential changes checked on the server

- **Covers:** H-17 `put-pin-no-lockout` (`repro-08`, `pin-change-without-lockout`), H-18 `admin-session-lockout-lost-update` (`repro-09`), H-19 `p2-ux-narrative-parent-handover`, plus the mediums `account-change-gated-client-only` (with `repro-07` narrowed), `set-contact-fewer-raises-cap-from-zero` and `child-can-reconsent-for-account-holder`, and the low `p2-undo-of-set-contact-loosens-without-pin`, which shares this fix.
- **Change:**
  - `apps/api/src/modules/identity/routes.ts`:
    - Add one `verifyPinWithLock`, a single `update accounts set pin_failed_count = pin_failed_count + 1, pin_locked_until = case … end … returning`. On success it resets the counter.
    - Use it in POST `/account/admin-session` and PUT `/account/pin`. PUT refuses with 423 while locked.
    - Password and e-mail changes require an admin session on the server when the account has a minor learner.
    - POST `/account` re-consent requires an admin session for a minor profile.
  - `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/tools.ts`: `set_contact fewer` never raises a cap from 0.
  - `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/routes.ts`: undo of a tightening goes through `loosens()` + `assertAccountHolder`.
  - App: `apps/mobile/lib/admin.ts` clears the token after each gated action and on `AppState` background. `apps/mobile/app/settings.tsx` no longer keeps it while it stays mounted.
- **Proof:** `identity.int.test.ts` cases:
  - 6 wrong `current_pin` on PUT → 423;
  - a lock set via admin-session also blocks PUT;
  - 10 parallel wrong PINs (`Promise.all`) → count ≥ 5 and locked;
  - a password change without an admin session on a minor account → 403;
  - re-consent without an admin session → 403.

  `delivery.int.test.ts` or `buddy-loop.int.test.ts`: undo of "weniger" on a minor without an admin token → refused. A unit test in `apps/mobile/lib/__tests__` covers admin-token expiry and clearing. §16.3 S-3 later generalises the lock.

- **Depends on:** none. S-3 builds on it.
- **Effort:** M.

#### I-6 Onboarding: no dead ends, no 500s, always a way out

- **Covers:** H-20 `onboarding-pin-dead-end` (`repro-10`), H-21 `child-16-17-profile-500` (`repro-11`), H-22 `trapped-at-consent-or-profile`, plus the medium `no-erasure-without-learner`.
- **Change:**
  - POST `/learner` accepts the first PIN in the same transaction (`identity/routes.ts`), so there is no second call that needs a fresh sign-in. `apps/mobile/app/profile.tsx` sends one request.
  - Store consent for every `relation='child'` row, not only for ages under 16, so the constraint `learners_child_consent` holds. Whether a 16/17-year-old "child" keeps the PIN gate is D-8.
  - `apps/api/src/lib/db.ts` maps 23514 (check violation) to a 4xx instead of 500.
  - `apps/mobile/app/consent.tsx` and `profile.tsx` get a ghost `<Btn>` "Abmelden", and a "Nicht einverstanden" that signs out.
  - Export and deletion work on an account without a learner (`identity/privacy.ts`, `routes.ts`).
- **Proof:**
  - `identity.int.test.ts`: `POST /learner` child at ages 15/16/17/18 → 201 or a reasoned 4xx, never 500.
  - Sign in, then TestClock +6 min, then create the learner with the PIN → success.
  - An account without a learner → deletion is scheduled and executed.
  - `tests/web`: a new onboarding-exit spec (sign up, then sign out at consent).
- **Depends on:** D-8.
- **Effort:** M.

#### I-7 Erasure and retention actually happen

- **Covers:** H-23 `deletion-job-parks-failed-account-stuck` (`repro-24`), H-24 `p2-sec-deletion-over-1000-photos-never-completes` (`p2-T7`), H-25 `recovery-failed-material-never-purged` (`repro-14`), H-26 `p2-ml-material-delete-is-soft-only` (materials half of `p2-xs-delete-is-soft-archive-content-kept`), plus the mediums `p2-J-memory-F3`, `delete-during-upload-orphans-photos`, `delete-during-extraction-ready` (`repro-13`) and `p2-xs-delete-is-soft-archive-content-kept`.
- **Change:**
  - `apps/api/src/modules/identity/privacy.ts` `executeAccountDeletion`:
    - Select only `photos_deleted_at is null`.
    - Remove in chunks of ≤ 1000 and ignore "not found".
    - Delete the account even when photo removal fails, and queue the leftover paths as a separate `purge_photos` job.
    - `delete_account` jobs are never parked: backoff with no attempt limit, `last_error` recorded.
    - A repeated request re-enqueues when no job is queued.
    - `/me` and `/health` expose an overdue deletion.
  - `scheduler/tick.ts` recovery calls the same `fail()` path as `materials/service.ts` (purge enqueue and `bumpContext`) in one transaction. Add a maintenance net that enqueues a purge for any failed, ready or archived material that still has photos.
  - Content purge, migration `0012_purge_content.sql`: extend `jobs_kind_check` with `purge_content`. `archiveMaterial` enqueues it: null the transcript and title, delete items, their states and turns.
  - Retracted and superseded memories are purged the same way.
  - The export excludes archived rows.
  - An extraction for an archived material aborts before writing items (checked under the material row lock).
  - Late photo uploads for an archived material are deleted on arrival.
  - If D-7 chooses "hide, not delete", the alternative is to change `apps/mobile/locales/*/library.json` and `docs/privacy.md` instead. The current mismatch must not ship.
- **Proof:** add `failNext` and a 1000-path cap to `MemoryStorage` in `apps/api/src/testing/fakes.ts` (the minimal part of S-2).
  - `identity.int.test.ts`:
    - `remove` throws 3 times → job still queued with backoff, `/me` shows pending, the 4th run deletes;
    - 1 200 photo rows (1 000 purged) → chunked, completes;
    - an account without photos in storage → completes.
  - `learning.int.test.ts`:
    - a stuck extract gets swept → a `purge_photos` job exists and the context is bumped;
    - delete during extraction → no ready state, no live items, no wake-up.
  - `material-items.int.test.ts`: "Blatt löschen" → transcript and items gone after the job, and the export no longer contains them.
- **Depends on:** D-7. S-4 later turns the deletion into a resumable state machine.
- **Effort:** L.

#### I-8 Auth outages never sign families out or wipe unsent work

- **Covers:** H-27 `p2-api-verifier-outage-becomes-401`, H-28 `refresh-5xx-signs-out-and-wipes` (`repro-26`), plus the lows `p2-signout-wipes-unsent-answers-and-photos-without-warning` and `p2-reset-logs-out-childs-device-and-wipes-queue` (same wipe code).
- **Change:**
  - `apps/api/src/auth/verifier.ts`: return `null` only for an invalid or expired token. Throw `AppError('unavailable')` (503) on network errors, 5xx and 429.
  - `apps/mobile/lib/auth/supabase.ts` `reasonOf` and `refreshSession`: status ≥ 500, 429 and network errors are transient, so the session is kept and the refresh retried with backoff. Only `invalid_grant`-class responses sign out.
  - `apps/mobile/app/_layout.tsx`: clear the outbox and drafts only when a different user signs in, never on an involuntary sign-out. A voluntary sign-out with pending items warns first.
- **Proof:**
  - Add an outage mode to `FakeAuth` (`fakes.ts`). An `endpoints.int.test.ts` case expects 503 while `verify` throws.
  - A new `apps/mobile/lib/auth/__tests__/refresh.test.ts` (node vitest, fetch stubbed): 503 and 429 keep the session and the outbox, and 400 `invalid_grant` signs out.
- **Depends on:** none.
- **Effort:** M.

#### I-9 Safeguarding path for children

- **Covers:** H-31 `p2-T2`, H-32 `p2-blocked-disclosure-poisons-thread`, plus the mediums `p2-disclosures-become-memories`, `p2-safety-verdict-after-speech`, `repro-28` (streamed reply spoken before validation) and `p2-T8` (block on school material ends in a retry loop).
- **Change:**
  - `apps/api/src/llm/vertex.ts` and `llm/call.ts`: keep `blocked` as its own error class and release the budget reservation.
  - `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/turn.ts`: a `blocked` on a learner message produces a fixed, localised caring reply chosen by code (trusted adult or helpline per locale). It is stored as Buddy's answer, with no "send again".
  - Migration `0013_message_failure_code.sql`: add `buddy_messages.failure_code` with a check list (`blocked`, `model_unavailable`, `budget`, `invalid`). Failed and blocked learner messages are excluded from later dialogue (`buddy/state.ts`, `turn.ts`).
  - Add a CORE distress rule in `prompts.ts`, and a structured `concern` field in `TurnDecision` (zod) that code acts on (fixed reply, no memory write). This keeps the rule out of the prompt alone (S-6).
  - `runRemember` (`buddy/tools.ts`) refuses memories while `concern` is set.
  - TTS and speech start only after the decision is validated and the provider's finish reason is known (`apps/mobile/components/buddy/Conversation.tsx` and the stream client).
  - An extraction `blocked` becomes a non-retryable `failure_reason` with honest copy.
- **Proof:**
  - `buddy-robustness.int.test.ts`: script `LlmError('blocked')` for `buddy_turn` → a fixed reply is stored, the next turn's prompt does not contain the blocked text, and the budget is unchanged.
  - A `concern` decision → no `buddy_memories` row.
  - `learning.int.test.ts`: a blocked extraction → no retry offered.
  - Eval cases in `apps/api/evals/buddy/cases.ts` for distress messages in 5 locales, graded by machine (S-8).
- **Depends on:** D-10 (helpline and parent notification policy).
- **Effort:** M.

#### I-10 Contact promises hold: in-app counts, agreed reminders never vanish, the reminder's subject is kept

- **Covers:** H-29 `p2-in-app-contact-not-counted` (`repro-02`), H-30 `p2-reminder-practice-ignores-step-subject`, H-37 `agreed-reminder-vanishes` (`repro-15`), plus the medium `agreed-reminder-silently-not-scheduled`.
- **Change:**
  - `apps/api/src/modules/buddy/delivery.ts`: `COUNTED` includes `in_app` for caps, dedupe and the unanswered gate, or the copy says "aufs Handy" (D-12).
  - For `origin='agreed'`, every terminal path posts to the thread with honest copy:
    - `no_slot` is treated like contact-off (`policy.ts`);
    - `sendDueOutreach` turns suppressed or expired into `in_app` + `postToThread`, with a "late" template when past due;
    - the bulk cancels in `buddy/routes.ts` and `tools.ts` convert agreed rows instead of cancelling them.
  - `plan_step` (`buddy/decision.ts`, `tools.ts`) takes an optional subject alias and focus topics, stored in the step payload. `buddy/check.ts` prepares items for that subject or prepares nothing, with honest copy.
- **Proof:** `delivery.int.test.ts`, one case per `repro-15` path (1B, 1B′, 1C, 1D), each ending in a thread message. Push disabled, 2 initiatives on one day → the second is suppressed. "weniger" → fewer in-app rows. `buddy-loop.int.test.ts`: a reminder for English with due maths items → English or none.
- **Depends on:** D-12, D-13.
- **Effort:** M.

#### I-11 Data location, deploy and infrastructure exposure

- **Covers:** H-33 `supabase-region-london-not-eu`, H-34 `p2-ios-on-device-flag-dropped-for-other-locale`, H-35 `vercel-deploy-config-invalid`, plus the mediums `p2-sec-anon-rpc-lb-invoke-tick` and `database-tls-not-enforced`.
- **Change:**
  - Region: either move the Supabase project to an EU region (a new project, re-link, migrate; cheap before learners exist) or correct `docs/privacy.md` and the consent text (D-4). Remove `supabase/.temp/` from git.
  - Add a boot check next to the Vertex EU check in `apps/api/src/config.ts` that refuses a non-EU database or pooler host.
  - `apps/api/src/lib/db.ts`: require TLS (`ssl: { rejectUnauthorized: true }` or `sslmode=verify-full`), and fail at boot otherwise.
  - Migration `0014_revoke_tick_rpc.sql`: `revoke execute on function lb_invoke_tick() from anon, authenticated`.
  - `apps/api/vercel.json`: drop `runtime` (set Node through `engines`), use a routing pattern that matches nested paths, and fix `README.md` to `/v1/health`.
  - iOS speech (`apps/mobile/lib/speech/recognize.ts`, `engine.ts`): check on-device support per target locale (a patched `expo-speech-recognition` or a small native call), otherwise route to the recording path. Until verified on a device, `docs/privacy.md` states the fallback.
- **Proof:**
  - A boot unit test for the host check.
  - An integration test that `anon` cannot call `lb_invoke_tick` (run on the real Postgres with the Supabase roles created by the harness, or as a SQL assertion in the migration test).
  - A CI step running `@vercel/fs-detectors` on `apps/api` (S-9), plus one recorded preview deploy answering `/v1/health`.
  - An `engine.test.ts` case: iOS with a target locale different from the device locale → not `'device'` unless support is proven. Runtime check on an iPhone (§17).
- **Depends on:** D-4 and D-11.
- **Effort:** M, plus operator time for the region move.

### 16.2 Near-term

These packages cover the remaining high finding and the medium clusters that affect trust, rule 5 (honest states), accessibility or scale. Where a package touches the UI it follows CLAUDE.md rule 16: Buddy says it in the chat first, with no new screens.

#### N-1 Math rendering

- **Covers:** H-36 `p2-function-plot-y-labels-offscreen`, plus `p2-blank-inside-math-becomes-subscript`, `p2-dollar-currency-parsed-as-math`, `p2-dollarmath-wraps-whole-prompt` and `p2-unknown-latex-glued-names`.
- **Change:**
  - Extract label placement from `apps/mobile/components/math/FigureView.tsx` into a pure function in `apps/mobile/lib/math/`, which widens `left` when the axis sits at the edge.
  - The math tokenizer in `apps/mobile/lib/math/` recognises the blank token inside `$…$` and escapes currency `$`.
  - The server-side dollar repair wraps only the maths span, never the whole sentence.
  - Unknown LaTeX commands render as a readable fallback.
- **Proof:** unit tests in `apps/mobile/lib/math/__tests__` (every y-label x ≥ 0 for `x_min = 0`; `$5 and $10` stays text), and a `tests/web` screenshot of one figure item.
- **Effort:** M.

#### N-2 Context fence, lock order and lease fencing

- **Covers:** `apply-vs-tap-lock-order-deadlock` (`repro-01`), `check-lease-shorter-than-runtime` (`repro-17`), `timezone-header-no-context-bump` (`repro-21`), `p2-turn-day-offsets-anchored-to-now-resolved-at-message-time` and `quote-checked-against-trigger-only` (`repro-22`, medium). Also the lows `p2-J-memory-F4`, `extraction-status-writes-unfenced`, `p2-delivery-status-writes-unfenced`, `schedule-check-compatible-dst`, `turn-seq-collision-500` and `create-material-idempotency-race`, which share the mechanism.
- **Change:**
  - One lock order: tap and memory routes in `buddy/routes.ts` take `buddy_settings FOR UPDATE` before the row.
  - `apps/api/src/http/context.ts` bumps `context_version` when `x-timezone` changes the stored zone.
  - `in_days` resolves against the date the model saw (stored in the decision).
  - The quote check (`buddy/text.ts`) accepts every learner message the turn answers.
  - Lease-conditioned writes (`… where lease_token = $x`) in the check, extraction and delivery workers. The check lease is heartbeated or sized to the worst-case runtime.
  - `schedule_check` resolves with `'reject'` (rule 2).
- **Proof:** commit the deleted `repro-01`/`repro-17`/`repro-21`/`repro-22` reproductions as regression tests in `buddy-robustness.int.test.ts` and `buddy-loop.int.test.ts`. Assert zero deadlocks (`pg_stat_database.deadlocks` delta), a single apply, a zone change → stale, and a split utterance → applied.
- **Effort:** M.

#### N-3 Send-time contact policy and learner-triggered replies

- **Covers:** `p2-send-time-recheck-ignores-tightened-rules`, `p2-NEW-countdown-relative-day-baked-at-plan-time` (`p2-countdown-text-day-label-frozen`), `p2-xs-learner-triggered-results-go-through-initiative-policy`, `p2-reading-result-silent-when-contact-off`, `send-uncertain-lease-no-thread`, `p2-stop-contact-immediately-reasks-optin-on-home`, `p2-background-actions-invisible-no-undo` and `p2-ux-heute-nicht-skips-for-good`.
- **Change:**
  - `delivery.ts` re-runs the full `decideContact` (`policy.ts`) at send time.
  - Relative day words ("Morgen", "Heute") are rendered at send time from the DaySpec.
  - Results of the learner's own action (reading done, session finished) always get an in-app line, separate from initiative caps (rule 6 covers initiatives).
  - `send_uncertain` rows get thread copy.
  - `home.ts` respects `contact_changed_at` before re-asking.
  - Actions from background checks are listed in the thread with undo.
  - "Heute nicht" postpones by a day.
- **Proof:** `delivery.int.test.ts`: tighten weekdays after scheduling → suppressed; a D-1 countdown delivered on exam day reads "Heute". Contact off, reading done → a thread message. Stop contact → no opt-in card for N days.
- **Effort:** M.

#### N-4 Push token binding and sign-out hygiene

- **Covers:** `p2-push-device-binding-first-device-forever` (with `repro-20`), `notification-tap-cold-start` (`repro-19`, plus `opened-proof-not-durable`), `signout-leaves-old-buddy-in-stack` and `p2-signout-offline-ios-may-hang-on-apns`.
- **Change:**
  - Migration `0015_push_device_binding.sql`: add `push_tokens.device_id` (an install id) with a unique active token per device.
  - Registering or signing in on a device deactivates any other learner's token on it on the server. Unregister is retried from the outbox.
  - `apps/mobile/lib/push.ts` reads `getLastNotificationResponseAsync` at start and persists "opened" until it is acknowledged.
  - Sign-out resets the router stack and wraps the token fetch in a timeout.
- **Proof:** `delivery.int.test.ts`: learner A's unregister fails, then learner B registers on the same device → A's token is inactive and A's outreach sends nothing to it. A device run for the cold-start tap (§17).
- **Depends on:** D-6.
- **Effort:** M.

#### N-5 Reading pipeline: outages are not failures, deletion cascades

- **Covers:** `merged-part-no-wake` (`repro-16`, `added-pages-do-not-wake-buddy`), `budget-refusals-consume-retry-runs`, `retry-after-purge-false-claim`, `storage-errors-reported-as-missing-photos`, `p2-J-04`, `p2-merged-part-page-numbers-wrong-sheet-page`, `pages-notice-expiry-vs-context`, `p2-xs-deleted-questions-still-asked-in-active-sessions` and `p2-model-page-numbers-unlabeled-images`. Also the low `p2-stale-prepared-step-after-material-delete`.
- **Change:**
  - `materials/service.ts`: budget refusals and transient provider or storage errors do not count toward `MAX_EXTRACTION_ATTEMPTS`.
  - The storage gateway (`apps/api/src/storage/gateway.ts`) distinguishes absent from failed (S-7).
  - Retry after a purge is not offered.
  - Merged parts emit their own wake event (`apps/api/src/modules/buddy/events.ts`, keyed on the part).
  - A failed retake stays attached to its sheet.
  - Page numbers come from explicit per-photo labels in the extraction request.
  - The notice expires relative to `ready_at`.
  - `archiveMaterial` also withdraws its items from active sessions and re-plans the prepared steps.
- **Proof:** `learning.int.test.ts` and `material-pages.int.test.ts`: storage `failNext` → the material is retried, not `photos_missing`; 3 budget refusals → still retryable; add a page → wake; delete a sheet mid-session → its items are no longer served.
- **Effort:** L.

#### N-6 Capture on the device

- **Covers:** `homework-failed-new-photo-becomes-study`, `p2-old-failed-card-hides-new-reading`, `orphan-awaiting-upload-rows`, `fresh-capture-discards-draft-silently`, `p2-lc-fresh-capture-kills-inflight-send`, `p2-lc-android-camera-kill-loses-photo`, `p2-tilt-subsampling-lattice-false-tilted` and `p2-blur-check-measured-below-upload-resolution`.
- **Change:**
  - The failed-reading card carries `purpose` and the target sheet into `/capture`.
  - `home.ts` ranks the sheet being read above old failures.
  - A new capture never deletes a draft that is uploading (the draft store in `apps/mobile/lib/capture/`).
  - Changing the photo set reuses the reserved material.
  - Android calls `getPendingResultAsync`.
  - The quality checks measure at upload scale and are recalibrated on real photos.
- **Proof:** vitest in `apps/mobile/lib/capture/__tests__`, a `core-loop.spec.ts` "failed homework → Neues Foto" path, and a device calibration run (§17).
- **Effort:** M.

#### N-7 Honest outage and failure states (rule 5)

- **Covers:** `p2-F-journey-cannot-check-steers-to-reveal`, `health-ok-despite-failing-tick`, `p2-NEW-dead-scheduler-strands-work-and-privacy-promises`, `p2-J-home-poll-error-replaces-screen`, `non-model-error-leaves-message-processing`, `tipp-after-last-hint-reveals` and `trailing-model-turn-after-outreach` (needs the §17 run first). Also the lows `p2-J-failed-label-not-arrived`, `p2-F-journey-outage-invisible-on-home` and `p2-F-journey-checkin-promise-dropped`.
- **Change:**
  - Answers whose verdict is null stay open and are re-checked by a job when the model is back. The copy depends on the mode, never "Lösung ansehen" in a test.
  - `/health` and the home scheduler state read `last_error` and the parked-job count (S-5).
  - A failed background refetch keeps the cached home.
  - Non-model exceptions close the turn as failed.
  - A failed message shows its `failure_code` (I-9 migration) instead of "Nicht angekommen".
  - "Tipp" after the last hint asks before revealing.
  - `home.ts` uses real gateway health and budget, not `available = true`.
- **Proof:** `modes.int.test.ts`: tutor `unavailable`, then back → the answer is re-checked, not skipped. `endpoints.int.test.ts`: a tick with every section failing → `/health` degraded. `stream.int.test.ts`: a thrown non-model error → the message is failed, not processing.
- **Effort:** M.

#### N-8 Profile, consent and export corrections

- **Covers:** `p2-uf-profile-uncorrectable`, `p2-J-memory-F1`, `p2-export-share-too-large-android`, and the lows `p2-J-memory-F2`, `p2-outcome-tap-irreversible`, `p2-ml-consent-version-not-enforced-server-side`, `p2-parents-pin-pad-names-no-action` and `p2-double-tap-eltern-fragen-stacks-pin-pads-and-leaks-admin-token`.
- **Change:**

- **Proof:** `identity.int.test.ts`: correct the birth year → minor flags recomputed; stale consent version → 409. `buddy-loop.int.test.ts`: withdrawn memory absent from the prompt. An Android device export of a seeded large account (§17).
- **Effort:** M.

- **Covers:** `p2-non-vocab-questions-read-and-heard-in-app-language`, `handsfree-recording-outlives-turn`, `speak-commits-after-finish`, `speak-offline-wait-no-cancel`, `repro-23` (`voice-mode-unreachable-from-home`), `p2-home-voice-reads-late-reply-on-other-screens` and `p2-a11y-talk-mode-records-voiceover`.

#### N-10 Accessibility

- **Covers:** `p2-a11y-buddy-reply-never-announced`, `p2-a11y-pin-error-silent`, `p2-a11y-practice-feedback-verdict-hint-solution`, `p2-a11y-pruefen-disappears-large-text`, `p2-a11y-status-android-only-capture-dictation`, `p2-a11y-toast-never-announced-ios`, `p2-a11y-dynamic-type-clipping-untested` and `p2-ux-toast-hidden-behind-ios-keyboard`.

### 16.3 Structural

These changes remove the causes behind whole families of findings. Each one makes a class of the bugs above impossible, or turns it into a red test.

#### S-1 One canonical answer-key representation, parsed locale-independently, proven by property tests

- **Prevents:** the whole grading family: C-1–C-7, H-1–H-5, H-10, and the preview-versus-grader mismatch (`p2-mixed-number-preview-vs-grader`).
- **Change:**
  - Migration `0017_item_answer_spec.sql` adds `items.answer_spec jsonb`, of the form `{type:'number'|'expression'|'text'|'choice', value, decimals, unit, tolerance, match:'exact'|'folded'}`.
  - It is produced by code in `apps/api/src/modules/practice/items.ts` when an item is written. The model supplies the key text and at most a zod-validated `rounded_to` or `case_sensitive` hint (D-1, D-2). It never supplies a tolerance number without bounds.
  - A backfill job fills existing rows. Rows it cannot parse are graded only by the tutor.
  - `ruleCheck` grades against `answer_spec`, never against raw text.
  - `apps/mobile/lib/math/typed.ts` imports the same grammar from `packages/shared-math`, so the preview and the grader cannot disagree.
- **Proof:** add `fast-check` to `packages/shared-math` and `apps/api` dev dependencies. Across locales {de, fr, es, it, en} and generated values (integers, 1–4 decimals, fractions, mixed numbers, percentages, with and without units):
  - formatting v as a learner in that locale writes it → `correct` or `unknown`;
  - v′ ≠ v (last-place ±1, sign flip, operator swap) → never `correct`.

  A parity test runs the preview parser and the grader on the same generated strings.

- **Effort:** L.

#### S-2 Fakes that can fail, and a clock the dev stack shares

- **Prevents:** tests that are only green because the fake cannot fail: `deleted-user-401-proven-by-fake`, the >1000-photo limit, storage and auth outages, and `dev-stack-wall-clock-hides-time-driven-ui`.
- **Change:**
  - `MemoryStorage` and `FakeAuth` in `apps/api/src/testing/fakes.ts` get `failNext(op, error)`, latency, request-size caps matching the providers' documented limits, and revocation semantics.
  - The dev stack exposes the harness `TestClock` (a test-only endpoint under `src/testing/`) so `tests/web` can advance time.
  - Every file that injects faults carries the rule-8 banner.
- **Proof:** a meta-test that each fake method has a failure mode. The H-12, H-23, H-24 and H-27 tests above use them.
- **Effort:** M.

#### S-3 Lockout and rate limit as one shared policy primitive

- **Prevents:** PIN brute force on any future gated route, answer-endpoint abuse (`mathjs-evaluate-unbounded-input`), tick RPC abuse, and auth-link or reset spam.
- **Change:**
  - Migration `0018_attempt_counters.sql` adds `attempt_counters(scope, key, window_start, count, locked_until)`.
  - Add one `apps/api/src/lib/limits.ts` with an atomic `consume(scope, key, policy)`, a single `insert … on conflict do update … returning`, using the app clock (rule 7).
  - The PIN routes (replacing the I-5 bespoke columns), `/practice/.../answer`, `/buddy/messages` and the recovery requests use it. A Hono middleware in `apps/api/src/app.ts` covers per-account defaults.
- **Proof:** an integration test with 50 parallel `consume` calls → an exact count. The route tests assert 429/423 with `Retry-After`.
- **Effort:** M.

#### S-4 Erasure as a monitored, resumable, chunked job

- **Prevents:** every "deletion silently never completes" variant (H-23, H-24, `cancel-vs-execute-race`, `p2-account-deletion-cascade-unindexed-fks`).
- **Change:**
  - `executeAccountDeletion` becomes a state machine stored in the job payload: `photos (cursor)` → `auth_user` → `cascade (table by table, batched)` → `done`. Each step is idempotent and resumes from its cursor.
  - Cancellation is refused once the machine has left `photos`, and the API says so.
  - Overdue deletions raise a `/health` failure and an operator alert.
  - The same machine runs material and memory content purges (I-7).
- **Proof:** an integration test kills the worker after each state (fault injection via S-2) → the next tick resumes and completes. Cancel during `cascade` → 409 with an honest message.
- **Effort:** L.

#### S-5 Every job kind has a visible terminal state, and every worker write is lease-fenced

- **Prevents:** parked jobs with no owner (`failed-countdown-never-fires-no-fallback`, `p2-check-stale-exhaustion-drops-reaction`, H-25), double applies (`repro-17`), and a false healthy signal.
- **Change:**
  - A registry in `apps/api/src/modules/scheduler/jobs.ts` maps each kind to its terminal-state handler (thread copy, settings state or operator metric), enforced by a type.
  - `finishJob` and all status writes take `lease_token` in the WHERE clause.
  - `/health` reports parked counts and `last_error` per kind.
- **Proof:** a table-driven integration test: for each job kind, force `max_attempts` failures → the defined terminal effect is observable. Stale-lease writes → 0 rows updated.
- **Effort:** M.

#### S-6 Rules that are only in the prompt get enforced in code

- **Prevents:** the rule-1 violations in §11.1: `p2-model-outreach-lock-screen-unchecked`, `lock-screen-text-carries-title-and-count`, `p2-sec-homework-solution-chat-unenforced`, `p2-sec-quote-provenance-two-chars`, and safeguarding (I-9).
- **Change:**
  - Lock-screen text is built by code from a template id plus placeholders that cannot hold scores or personal data (`apps/api/src/push/`). Model prose goes only into the in-app thread.
  - The homework leak guard runs on every chat reply while a help session or homework material is in STATE, including `search_material` results.
  - Quote provenance requires token-level overlap with the learner's own messages from this turn, with a minimum length. This is a structural comparison against her text, not a word list.
  - Confirmations of changes are rendered from applied `buddy_actions`, not from model prose.
- **Proof:** integration tests in `delivery.int.test.ts` (a push body never contains the exam title), `modes.int.test.ts` (chat during homework with a scripted leaking reply → replaced) and `buddy-robustness.int.test.ts` (a 2-character quote → refused). Eval cases for each rule (S-8).
- **Effort:** M–L.

#### S-7 One error classification at every external seam

- **Prevents:** outage → 401 (H-27), storage error → "absent", provider 4xx → retryable (`provider-4xx-classified-retryable`), block → invalid (H-31).
- **Change:** a shared `Outcome = ok | refused | transient | unknown` in `apps/api/src/lib/errors.ts`. The auth verifier, storage gateway, `llm/vertex.ts` and the push transport return it. One mapping takes it to HTTP status and to UI states in `apps/mobile/lib/api/client.ts`.
- **Proof:** a unit test per adapter for each provider status class, and S-2 fakes emitting each outcome in integration tests.
- **Effort:** M.

#### S-8 Gates that actually run: walkthrough, fit, DB-backed pre-commit, evals

- **Prevents:** `walkthrough-and-fit-never-gated`, `precommit-green-without-postgres`, `stream-test-buffered`, `tick-trigger-path-untested` and `evals-not-machine-gradable`, plus the missing mobile screen tests (§15.3.5).
- **Change:**
  - `.github/workflows/check.yml` adds a job running `scripts/web-walkthrough.sh`, including `tests/web/fit.ts` at 390×844 and 360×740.
  - `.husky/pre-commit` uses the `test:db` path and fails when no Postgres is reachable, instead of skipping.
  - The stream test reads chunks incrementally.
  - A smoke test for pg_cron → pg_net → `/internal/tick` against the local Supabase.
  - Evals get machine-checkable assertions for safety, leak and lock-screen rules. They run on release, not per PR.
  - Mobile vitest is extended to `components/**` with a React Native test renderer for the screens named in §15.2.
- **Proof:** the gates themselves: a deliberately broken fit fails CI on a throwaway branch.
- **Effort:** M.

#### S-9 Deploy and region checks

- **Prevents:** H-33 and H-35 recurring, and the TLS drift in `database-tls-not-enforced`.
- **Change:**
  - A `scripts/deploy-check.sh` run in CI and before promote:
    - Vercel builder detection;
    - the Supabase project region via the Management API against an EU allowlist;
    - TLS on the DB URL;
    - `revoke` state of `SECURITY DEFINER` functions;
    - a `/v1/health` smoke on the preview deploy.
  - Boot-time checks in `apps/api/src/config.ts` mirror the region and TLS parts.
- **Proof:** the CI job, plus a unit test of the boot checks.
- **Effort:** S–M.

---

### 16.4 Sequencing

1. **Week 1:** I-1 and I-2 come first, because every practice, test and homework verdict depends on them. They share one truth table, and one developer should own both. I-8 (auth wipe) and I-5 (atomic PIN lock) run in parallel. Both are small, self-contained and remove harm that cannot be undone.
2. **Next:** I-3 (homework) needs I-1's value comparison. I-4, I-6 and I-10 are independent. I-7 needs the minimal failing `MemoryStorage` from S-2, so pull that slice forward. I-9 needs D-10 answered, so ask for it on day one. I-11's region move is operator work that should happen before any real data exists: after that it becomes a data migration.
3. **Structural work:** S-1 and S-4 are the structural follow-ups of I-1/I-2 and I-7. Start them once the hotfixes are green, not before, so learners are protected early. S-2 and S-8 should land early in the near-term phase, because most N-package tests depend on failing fakes, the shared clock and a gated walkthrough.
4. **Near-term:** N-2 (fence, locks, leases) goes before N-3 and N-5, which add more writes behind the same fence.
5. **Release gate for the first real learner:** every 16.1 package merged with its integration tests, the §17 device checks for H-34 and the cold-start push tap done, and D-1 to D-13 answered in writing (in an ADR where they change a documented rule).

### 16.5 Decisions the product owner must make

| #    | Decision                                         | Options                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | Affects   |
| ---- | ------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | --------- |
| D-1  | **Numeric tolerance policy**                     | (a) Exact by default; tolerance only when an item declares `rounded_to`/measured. (b) Half a unit of the key's last written decimal. (c) Per subject (physics measurements lenient, maths exact). Who may set it: code only, or a zod-validated model hint?                                                        | I-1, S-1  |
| D-2  | **Spelling strictness per subject**              | Is case, ß/ss or punctuation a wrong answer, a "Fast" near miss, or ignored? It likely differs for German and foreign-language vocab versus content subjects (biology terms). Recommended: exact for `german`/language subjects, near miss elsewhere, set per item (`match`) with the subject kind as the default. | I-2, S-1  |
| D-3  | **Expressions and forms as answers**             | Is `17·23` for 391 accepted, sent to the tutor, or answered with "rechne noch aus"? Does "als gemischte Zahl" reject `7/2`? The rule check cannot know the requested form, so recommended: `unknown`, and the tutor decides.                                                                                       | I-1, I-2  |
| D-4  | **Supabase region**                              | Move to an EU region now (cheap before real data) or change `privacy.md` and the consent text to name London (UK adequacy). Legal review needed either way.                                                                                                                                                        | I-11, S-9 |
| D-5  | **"Beenden" and help-session lifetime**          | Pause versus finish. How long a help session stays resumable. Whether an abandoned practice session returns its step.                                                                                                                                                                                              | I-3       |
| D-6  | **Push token binding**                           | Bind tokens to an install id with one active learner per device (recommended), or to the account. What happens to a sibling's phone after sign-out.                                                                                                                                                                | N-4       |
| D-7  | **Delete versus hide**                           | "Blatt löschen" and memory removal: real erasure after N days (recommended, matches `privacy.md`), or rename to "ausblenden" and change `privacy.md`.                                                                                                                                                              | I-7, S-4  |
| D-8  | **16- and 17-year-old child profiles**           | Allowed as `child` (with parental consent recorded and the PIN gate kept until 18), or forced to `self`. What happens at the 16th birthday (`p2-sec-pin-gate-vanishes-at-16-for-child-profile`).                                                                                                                   | I-6, I-5  |
| D-9  | **Deletion when storage fails**                  | Delete the account and keep retrying the photos separately (recommended), or block the account deletion until the photos are gone.                                                                                                                                                                                 | I-7       |
| D-10 | **Safeguarding response**                        | The fixed reply text per locale, which helplines (for example Nummer gegen Kummer 116 111 in DE), whether and how a parent is told (this touches the child's privacy), and a pedagogical and legal review.                                                                                                         | I-9       |
| D-11 | **iOS speech for non-device locales**            | Route to the server recording path (Vertex, EU), or disable voice for those locales.                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | I-11      |
| D-12 | **What "höchstens N× pro Woche" means**          | Count in-app messages (recommended; rule 6), or relabel as "aufs Handy" and show a separate in-app limit.                                                                                                                                                                                                          | I-10      |
| D-13 | **Agreed reminders under pause and quiet hours** | Confirm that an agreed reminder always appears in the app, even while paused or late, with "late" copy (`docs/architecture.md` §Proactivity already promises this).                                                                                                                                                | I-10      |
| D-14 | **Rate-limit values**                            | Per-account budgets for answers, messages, PIN and recovery requests.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | S-3       |

---

## 17. Requires Runtime Verification

These claims cannot be settled by reading the code. Some are findings with verdict `needs_runtime`. The others are confirmed code paths whose real-world effect depends on a device, a hosted service or the model. The verification environment for this audit had no device, no hosted Supabase or Vercel access, and no egress to `exp.host` (the `p2-expo-collapseid-unverified` probe was blocked by the proxy). Nothing below was run.

### 17.1 Findings with verdict `needs_runtime`

| Id (severity)                                                                            | Claim                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | Why it is unproven                                                                                                                                | Experiment                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | Confirms / refutes                                                                                                                                                          |
| ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `p2-T8` (medium)                                                                         | Vertex blocks ordinary curriculum sheets (Sexualkunde, NS history) at the configured thresholds (`apps/api/src/llm/vertex.ts:34-51`: `SEXUALLY_EXPLICIT` `BLOCK_LOW_AND_ABOVE`). The failure then shows as a retryable "Beim Lesen ist etwas schiefgegangen". | The code path is confirmed (`blocked` → `model_error`, retry offered). The provider's classification of real school material is not.              | Run `apps/api/evals` extraction on 10–20 real photographed pages (a German Year 6–8 biology puberty chapter, a history page with NS propaganda, a German literature page with violence) with production `safetySettings`. Record `finishReason` and `safetyRatings` per page. | **Confirms:** any `SAFETY` finish on ordinary pages. **Refutes:** all `STOP` across the set. Either way, add the non-retryable `blocked` reason.                            |
| `trailing-model-turn-after-outreach` (medium), `dialogue-may-end-with-model-turn` (low)  | After an in-app outreach is posted during a turn, the retry's `contents` end with a `model` role, and Vertex may reject it (400 → `unavailable` → `model_unavailable`).                                                                                       | The trailing-model structure was reproduced on the harness. Gemini's handling of a trailing model turn with `responseJsonSchema` is not known.    | Call the production Vertex model directly with `contents=[{role:'user',…STATE…},{role:'model',text:'Wie verabredet: …'}]` and the TurnDecision schema, streaming and non-streaming, 5 runs each.                                                                              | **Confirms:** HTTP 400, or a response that continues its own message instead of answering. **Refutes:** a valid TurnDecision each time.                                     |
| `repro-19` (medium; same defect as `notification-tap-cold-start`, confirmed code-traced) | A push tapped from a killed app never records `opened`. Buddy then stays silent 48 h ("previous_unanswered").                                                                                                                                                 | Native replay timing in expo-notifications (listener attached after module creation) can only be observed on a device.                            | EAS dev build with `PUSH_BACKEND=expo` on one iPhone and one Android phone. Repeat with the app backgrounded.                                                    | **Confirms:** `opened_at` is null after the cold-start tap and set after the backgrounded tap. **Refutes:** set in both cases.                                              |

### 17.3 Not included here

Claims in `final-refuted.json` (for example `p2-securestore-read-failure-unhandled-shows-welcome` and `vertex-stream-last-chunk-assumption`) are not listed here.

`repro-07` (PIN reset through the fresh-amr path) is MEDIUM (narrowed) per the second opinion. The one runtime question it leaves is operational: whether the hosted project enables Supabase's "Require current password when changing password". To check it, open Supabase dashboard → Auth → Settings and read the flag. **Enabled** closes the chain. **Disabled** keeps it open for anyone who holds the raw device session.
